`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# Contributing to ReShield

**English** | [繁體中文](CONTRIBUTING.zh-TW.md)

Thanks for helping developers protect what they ship. ReShield is an open protocol plus a skill and scanner; contributions to any part are welcome.

## Getting started

You need Python 3.11 or later and nothing else.

```bash
git clone https://github.com/swimchicken777/reshield.git
cd reshield
python -m unittest discover -s tests
python skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py scan <build> --format summary
```

## Where things live

| Folder | What it is |
|---|---|
| `protocol/` | The specifications: asset declaration, protection levels, measurement, report format, adapter interface |
| `skills/reshield/` | The Agent Skill: `SKILL.md`, references and the scanner in `scripts/` |
| `skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/adapters/` | One adapter per technology (Unity, Godot, Unreal, Android, Web, native and .NET) |
| `benchmarks/` | Original sample projects with known answers, and measured results |
| `tests/` | Unit tests for the scanner |

## Good first contributions

- **A new adapter** for a technology we do not cover yet. Read `protocol/adapter-interface.md`, add `adapters/<name>.py`, register it in `adapters/__init__.py`, add a remediation page in `skills/reshield/references/remediation/`, and add tests.
- **Real-build validation.** Scan builds of your own projects and report wrong or missing results. Never submit third-party builds.
- **Fix guides** in plain language for existing rules.
- **Translations** of the docs, as separate `<name>.<locale>.md` files.

## Rules

- **Read-only.** Adapters never modify, run or upload a build.
- **Honest results.** Report what was observed. When something cannot be decided, return `open`, never a guessed level. Missing evidence is never protection.
- **No false alarms.** Prefer missing a finding over reporting a wrong one, and add a regression test for every false result you fix.
- **English in code and docs.** Translations go in separate `.zh-TW.md` (or other locale) files.
- **Only your own builds.** Samples and tests use original code written for this repository.
- **Interface changes** to the Finding format or the adapter interface go through a proposal in `protocol/proposals/` first.

## Pull requests

- Keep each pull request to one change, with a short description.
- Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` before opening it, and add tests for new behavior.
- Commit messages are one short line.

```

`CONTRIBUTING.zh-TW.md`:

```md
# 參與貢獻 ReShield

[English](CONTRIBUTING.md) | **繁體中文**

感謝你幫助開發者保護他們發布的程式。ReShield 包含一套開放協定、一個 skill 和掃描器，任何部分都歡迎貢獻。

## 開始

只需要 Python 3.11 以上。

```bash
git clone https://github.com/swimchicken777/reshield.git
cd reshield
python -m unittest discover -s tests
python skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py scan <build> --format summary
```

## 適合入門的貢獻

- **新的轉接層**：支援我們還沒涵蓋的技術。先讀 `protocol/adapter-interface.md`，新增 `adapters/<名稱>.py`、在 `adapters/__init__.py` 註冊、在 `skills/reshield/references/remediation/` 加上修法頁面，並補上測試。
- **真實 build 驗證**：掃描你自己的專案，回報錯誤或漏掉的結果。請勿提交第三方的 build。
- **白話修法說明**：改善現有規則的修法。
- **翻譯**：以獨立的 `<檔名>.<語系>.md` 檔案提供。

## 規則

- **只讀**：轉接層絕不修改、執行或上傳 build。
- **誠實**：只回報觀察到的事；無法判定時回傳 `open`，不要猜等級。缺少證據永遠不代表有保護。
- **不要誤報**：寧可漏報也不要報錯，修好的每個誤報都要加回歸測試。
- **程式碼與文件用英文**：翻譯放在獨立的 `.zh-TW.md` 等語系檔案。
- **介面變更**：修改 Finding 格式或轉接介面前，先在 `protocol/proposals/` 提出提案。

## Pull request

- 每個 PR 只做一件事，附上簡短說明。
- 開 PR 前執行 `python -m unittest discover -s tests`，新行為要附測試。
- commit 訊息只寫一行。

```

`PRIVACY.md`:

```md
# Privacy Policy

Last updated: 2026-11-02

ReShield is an open-source Agent Skill and command-line scanner. This policy explains what happens to your data when you use it.

## What ReShield collects

Nothing. ReShield has no servers, no accounts, no analytics and no telemetry.

## How your files are handled

- The scanner runs on your own computer. It only reads the project and build files you point it at.
- It never uploads, sends or shares your files, results or any other data.
- It never modifies or runs the build it checks. When you ask your agent to apply a fix, the agent changes your source project on your computer, only after you agree.
- Leaked keys it finds are shown only in part (for example `AKIAIO****`) and are never written out in full.
- Packages such as `.apk` or `.zip` are unpacked into a temporary folder on your computer, which is deleted after the scan.

## Your AI assistant

ReShield runs inside an AI assistant such as Codex or Claude Code. That assistant sends what you type, and the results it reads, to its provider to generate answers. That is covered by your assistant's own privacy policy, not this one, for example the [OpenAI Privacy Policy](https://openai.com/policies/privacy-policy/) or the [Anthropic Privacy Policy](https://www.anthropic.com/legal/privacy).

## Changes and contact

Changes to this policy are made in this repository, so its history shows every version. Questions: [open an issue](https://github.com/swimchicken777/reshield/issues).

```

`README.md`:

```md
![ReShield banner](.github/assets/banner.png)

<p align="center"><b>Find out what an AI can pull out of your build, before someone else does.</b></p>

<p align="center">
  <a href="LICENSE"><img alt="License" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/license-Apache%202.0-blue"></a>
  <img alt="Python 3.11+" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.11%2B-blue">
  <img alt="Agent Skill" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Agent%20Skill-Claude%20Code%20%7C%20Codex-8A2BE2">
  <img alt="Version" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/version-0.1.0-green">
</p>

<p align="center"><b>English</b> | <a href="README.zh-TW.md">繁體中文</a></p>

ReShield checks your game or app before release and tells you, in plain language, **where your code can be copied and how to fix it**. It runs as an Agent Skill in Claude Code or Codex, or as a standalone scanner.

```text
$ python reshield_cli.py scan ./build --format summary

**Your combat formulas are easy to copy right now.**

1. **Stop shipping source maps (files that contain your original code)**
   Where: `app.js.map` and 1 other file
   Why: Your original code, with your comments, ships with your release for anyone to read.
   Fix: Turn off source maps for production builds (Vite: build.sourcemap = false; webpack: devtool = false).
```

## Why

AI can now turn a shipped build back into readable code in hours. In [our first test](benchmarks/RESULTS.md#ai-recovery-first-run-2026-11-02), a fresh AI agent with no hints recovered the exact game formula from **6 of 7 builds**, including minified JavaScript and Godot binary tokens. ReShield shows you what is exposed before you ship.

## Highlights

- **Plain language.** No security background needed: each problem comes with where it is, why it matters, and the exact setting to change.
- **Every major platform.** Unity, Unreal, Godot, Web and Electron, Android, iOS, .NET and native apps, plus `.apk`, `.ipa` and `.zip` packages.
- **Leaked keys and debug files.** Finds API keys, private keys and symbol files that should never ship.
- **Honest.** Anything it cannot check is reported as not checked, never as safe.
- **Fixes it for you.** Ask your agent to apply a fix, and it changes the setting and scans again.
- **CI ready.** Exports SARIF for GitHub code scanning, tagged with [OWASP MASWE](https://mas.owasp.org/MASWE/) weaknesses.

## Quick start

Requires Python 3.11 or later. No other packages.

**Codex**

```bash
codex plugin marketplace add swimchicken777/reshield
codex plugin add reshield@reshield
```

**Claude Code**

```bash
git clone https://github.com/swimchicken777/reshield.git
cp -r reshield/skills/reshield ~/.claude/skills/
```

On Windows PowerShell: `Copy-Item -Recurse reshield\skills\reshield $HOME\.claude\skills\`

Then ask your agent:

```text
Before I release my game, check how well its code is protected and what I should fix
```

Or run the scanner directly:

```bash
python reshield/skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py scan <build> --format summary
```

Use `--format markdown` for the full report, or `--format sarif` for CI.

## What it checks

| Platform | Checks |
|---|---|
| Unity | Mono vs IL2CPP and readable IL2CPP metadata, in Windows, macOS, Linux, Android, iOS and WebGL builds |
| Unreal | Encrypted `.pak` / IoStore containers, C++ symbols |
| Godot | GDScript as text or binary tokens in `.pck` files |
| Web / Electron | Shipped source maps, readable JavaScript, code inside `app.asar` |
| Android | Whether R8 / ProGuard renamed your classes |
| Native and .NET | Symbols, debug info and class names in PE, ELF and Mach-O; readable .NET IL |
| Every build | Leaked keys (18 types), debug files, protection tools already applied |

Python, Flutter, React Native, Java, Lua, GameMaker, Ren'Py and Xamarin code is recognized and reported as not checked yet, with how to protect it. See the [remediation guides](skills/reshield/references/remediation/) for every rule.

## How it works

ReShield rates each part of your code on a scale from **L0** (readable almost as written) to **L5** (never ships; runs on your server), and recommends a level for each asset based on its value and the performance cost you accept. The rules are an open protocol, so any tool can produce or read the same reports.

- [Protocol](protocol/): protection levels, asset declaration and report format
- [Benchmarks](benchmarks/RESULTS.md): results on real Unity, Godot and Web builds
- [Example `reshield.toml`](examples/reshield.toml): mark what matters most in your project

ReShield measures and raises the cost of reverse engineering; it cannot make it impossible. It only scans projects you own or are allowed to test.

## Contributing

Bug reports, real-build results and new platform adapters are welcome. See [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md).

## License

[Apache 2.0](LICENSE) · [Privacy](PRIVACY.md) · [Terms](TERMS.md)

```

![ReShield banner](.github/assets/banner.png)

<p align="center"><b>在別人動手之前，先知道 AI 能從你的 build 裡挖出什麼。</b></p>

<p align="center">
  <a href="LICENSE"><img alt="License" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/license-Apache%202.0-blue"></a>
  <img alt="Python 3.11+" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.11%2B-blue">
  <img alt="Agent Skill" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Agent%20Skill-Claude%20Code%20%7C%20Codex-8A2BE2">

</p>

<p align="center"><a href="README.md">English</a> | <b>繁體中文</b></p>

ReShield 會在你發布遊戲或 App 之前做檢查，用白話告訴你**程式碼哪裡會被複製、該怎麼改**。它可以當作 Claude Code 或 Codex 的 Agent Skill 使用，也可以單獨執行掃描器。

```text
$ python reshield_cli.py scan ./build --format summary

**Your combat formulas are easy to copy right now.**

1. **Stop shipping source maps (files that contain your original code)**
   Where: `app.js.map` and 1 other file
   Why: Your original code, with your comments, ships with your release for anyone to read.
   Fix: Turn off source maps for production builds (Vite: build.sourcemap = false; webpack: devtool = false).
```

透過 Agent 使用時，它會用你的語言回答。

## 為什麼需要它

現在 AI 只要幾個小時，就能把發布出去的 build 還原成看得懂的程式碼。在[我們的第一次測試](benchmarks/RESULTS.md#ai-recovery-first-run-2026-11-02)中，一個沒有任何提示的全新 AI，從 **7 個 build 中的 6 個**還原出完全正確的遊戲公式，包括壓縮過的 JavaScript 和 Godot 的二進位 token。ReShield 讓你在發布前就知道哪些東西暴露了。

## 特色

- **白話說明**：不需要資安背景。每個問題都會告訴你在哪裡、為什麼重要，以及要改哪個設定。
- **涵蓋主要平台**：Unity、Unreal、Godot、Web 與 Electron、Android、iOS、.NET 和原生程式，也能直接掃描 `.apk`、`.ipa`、`.zip`。
- **外洩金鑰與除錯檔**：找出不該出貨的 API 金鑰、私鑰和符號檔。
- **誠實**：沒檢查到的部分會標示為「未檢查」，絕不說「安全」。
- **幫你修好**：請 Agent 套用修法，它會改好設定並重新掃描。
- **可接 CI**：輸出 SARIF 給 GitHub code scanning，並標上 [OWASP MASWE](https://mas.owasp.org/MASWE/) 弱點編號。

## 快速開始

需要 Python 3.11 以上，不需要其他套件。

**Codex**

```bash
codex plugin marketplace add swimchicken777/reshield
codex plugin add reshield@reshield
```

**Claude Code**

```bash
git clone https://github.com/swimchicken777/reshield.git
cp -r reshield/skills/reshield ~/.claude/skills/
```

Windows PowerShell：`Copy-Item -Recurse reshield\skills\reshield $HOME\.claude\skills\`

接著問你的 Agent：

```text
發布前幫我檢查我的遊戲程式碼保護得夠不夠，以及該怎麼加強
```

或直接執行掃描器：

```bash
python reshield/skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py scan <build> --format summary
```

要完整報告用 `--format markdown`，接 CI 用 `--format sarif`。

## 檢查項目

| 平台 | 檢查內容 |
|---|---|
| Unity | Mono 或 IL2CPP、IL2CPP metadata 是否可讀，支援 Windows、macOS、Linux、Android、iOS、WebGL build |
| Unreal | `.pak`／IoStore 是否加密、C++ 符號 |
| Godot | `.pck` 裡的 GDScript 是文字還是二進位 token |
| Web／Electron | 是否附帶 source map、JavaScript 可讀性、`app.asar` 內的程式碼 |
| Android | R8／ProGuard 是否把類別改名 |
| 原生與 .NET | PE、ELF、Mach-O 的符號、除錯資訊與類別名稱；.NET IL 是否可讀 |
| 所有 build | 外洩金鑰（18 種）、除錯檔、已套用的保護工具 |

Python、Flutter、React Native、Java、Lua、GameMaker、Ren'Py 和 Xamarin 的程式碼會被辨識出來，標示為「尚未檢查」並附上保護方法。每條規則的修法請見[修復指引](skills/reshield/references/remediation/)。

## 運作方式

ReShield 會把程式碼的每個部分評為 **L0**（幾乎跟原始碼一樣可讀）到 **L5**（不出貨，只在你的伺服器上執行），並依每項資產的價值與你能接受的效能代價，建議適合的等級。這些規則是開放協定，任何工具都能產生或讀取同樣的報告。

- [協定](protocol/)：防護等級、資產宣告與報告格式
- [基準測試](benchmarks/RESULTS.md)：真實 Unity、Godot、Web build 的結果
- [`reshield.toml` 範例](examples/reshield.toml)：標出專案裡最重要的部分

ReShield 能衡量並提高逆向工程的成本，但無法讓逆向變得不可能。它只掃描你自己擁有或獲得授權測試的專案。

## 參與貢獻

歡迎回報問題、提供真實 build 的檢查結果，以及新增平台轉接層。請見 [CONTRIBUTING.zh-TW.md](CONTRIBUTING.zh-TW.md)。

## 授權

[Apache 2.0](LICENSE) · [隱私政策](PRIVACY.md) · [使用條款](TERMS.md)

`benchmarks/README.md`:

```md
# Benchmarks

Spec: [`protocol/04-ai-resilience-measurement.md`](../protocol/04-ai-resilience-measurement.md)

Reference samples used to check that ReShield's levels and scores match reality.

## Samples

Three original minimal projects share the same combat formula and nine ground-truth cases.
Build and verify all three L0 samples first, then the four comparison variants.

| Project | Baseline first | Comparison variants |
|---|---|---|
| [Unity](unity/combat-formula/README.md) | Windows Mono: expected L0 | IL2CPP with readable metadata: expected L1 |
| [Godot](godot/combat-formula/README.md) | Text GDScript in unencrypted PCK: expected L0 | Binary tokens in unencrypted PCK: expected L1 |
| [Web](web/combat-formula/README.md) | Unminified JavaScript: expected L0 | Minified/no map: expected L1; minified/linked map: expected L0 |

Actual execution and evidence: [RESULTS.md](RESULTS.md).
These are expected classifications under the current protocol, not automatic claims of protection.
All variants are scanned end to end; see [RESULTS.md](RESULTS.md).

## Layout

```
benchmarks/
└── <platform>/
    └── <sample-name>/
        ├── src/            # original source, written by us
        ├── tests/          # behavior tests used to measure semantic recovery
        ├── reshield.toml   # asset declaration; target L2 is a desired goal
        ├── expected.toml   # expected level for each build variant
        └── README.md       # what the sample does and how to build each variant
```

## Rules

- Samples MUST be original code written for this repo.
- Do not commit build binaries; document how to build them instead.

Builds, engine caches, downloaded tools, dependencies and raw logs are ignored by the local `.gitignore`.
Keep Unity `.meta`/ProjectSettings/Packages and Godot `.gd.uid` files: they are source-project metadata, not caches.
Source projects and generated artifacts are our own; the optional downloaded engine/compiler binaries are not committed.

## Three distinct kinds of truth

- **Ground Truth** (`tests/ground-truth.json`): purpose, input domain, formula and correct outputs. Each platform keeps an identical copy, enforced by a test. Inputs are integers from 0 through 1,000,000 and a Boolean; out-of-domain behavior is not part of cross-platform recovery scoring.
- **Manifest** (`reshield.toml`): asset identity and desired target L2. It does not set the observed level.
- **Expected scenario** (`expected.toml`): per-variant evidence and expected level. It contains no actual score or pass claim.

Formula: `base = max(0, attack - defense); damage = critical ? base * 2 : base`.
The original five cases are preserved, plus three critical zero-result cases and an upper-bound case.
Build/runtime test harnesses only accept external inputs and return actual outputs; expected answers are not shipped.
Only `builds/<variant>/` should be given to a reference analyst. Do not expose source, answers, expected files or `results/` during recovery measurement. The Web map variant intentionally includes source code via its map.

## Provisional expected.toml format v1 (benchmark-local)

This is **not** a new shared protocol schema. No changes to `protocol/`, core or Adapters are required.

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `format_version` | Integer `1`; local benchmark format |
| `sample_id`, `platform` | Stable sample ID and registered platform name |
| `tool_version` | Pinned engine/runtime version; exact build dependencies are also locked/documented |
| `ground_truth` | Path relative to the sample directory |
| `[[variant]].id` | Unique build variant |
| `scan_target` | Publication directory relative to the sample; never `src/` or sample root |
| `asset_id` | ID present in `reshield.toml` |
| `expected_level`, `confidence` | Expected observed classification, not target_level or actual scan output |
| `evidence` | Human-readable artifact assertions implemented by local inspection helpers |
| `acceptance` | Functional/evidence acceptance criteria |

Do not pin numeric scores or invent rule IDs. Level definitions live in [`protocol/02-protection-levels.md`](../protocol/02-protection-levels.md).

## Quick checks without engines

From the Repository root (Python 3.11+, Node.js):

```powershell
python -B -m unittest discover -s benchmarks/tests -v
python -B -m unittest discover -s tests -v
node benchmarks/web/combat-formula/tests/test.mjs source
```

The existing CI only discovers root `tests/`. Benchmark contract tests run separately: `python -m unittest discover -s benchmarks/tests`.
Engine builds and execution use the commands in each sample README. `run_behavior.py` runs only these original samples; it must never be called from an Adapter, whose contract remains read-only.
Inspection helpers validate real output, not filename-only fixtures. AI recovery results are in [RESULTS.md](RESULTS.md).

```

`benchmarks/RESULTS.md`:

```md
# v0.1 end-to-end results and calibration

Date: 2026-11-01 (Asia/Taipei). ReShield `0.1.0.dev2`, Windows x64, Python 3.12.

Every benchmark variant that can be built here was built from source, passed its behavior tests (9/9 ground-truth cases), and was scanned with the CLI. Builds are not committed; rebuild them with each sample's README.

## End-to-end results

| Platform | Variant | Engine / tool | Expected | Scanned | Asset `combat-formulas` | Match |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Unity | mono | Unity 6000.3.8f1 | L0 observed | L0 observed | L0 observed, below target L2 | ✅ |
| Unity | il2cpp (metadata v39) | Unity 6000.3.8f1 | L1 observed | L1 observed | L1 observed, linked by name | ✅ (2026-10-31) |
| Unity | il2cpp (metadata v31) | Unity 2022.3.62f3 | L1 observed | L1 observed | L1 observed, method level | ✅ (2026-10-31) |
| Godot | text | Godot 4.5.1 | L0 observed | L0 observed | L0 observed | ✅ |
| Godot | tokens | Godot 4.5.1 | L1 observed | L1 observed | L1 observed | ✅ |
| Web | plain | esbuild 0.25.12 | L0 observed | L0 observed | L0 observed | ✅ (after the 2026-11-02 confidence decision) |
| Web | minified | esbuild 0.25.12 | L1 observed | L1 observed | L1 observed | ✅ (after the 2026-11-02 confidence decision) |
| Web | minified-map | esbuild 0.25.12 | L0 observed | L0 observed | L0 observed (source map) | ✅ |

All scans were complete (no `open` or `error`), read-only, and produced a developer summary, Markdown, JSON and SARIF.

## Added 2026-11-02

Built from a copy of the Unity sample outside the repository; scanned directly, without extracting.

| Build | Engine / tool | Scanned | Asset `combat-formulas` | Match |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Unity macOS Mono `.app` | Unity 6000.3.8f1 | L0 observed | L0 observed | ✅ after fixing the Mono runtime path (Unity 6 uses `Contents/Frameworks/libmonobdwgc-2.0.dylib`) |
| Unity Android IL2CPP `.apk` (arm64) | Unity 6000.3.8f1 | L1 observed; Unity, Android and native adapters ran | L1 observed | ✅ |
| Unity Android Mono `.apk` (armv7) | Unity 6000.3.8f1 | L0 observed; Unity, Android and native adapters ran | L0 observed | ✅ |
| Godot 4.5.1 editor executable | official release | L1: 2,907 C++ class names in RTTI | — | ✅ (was reported as L2 before GCC/Clang RTTI detection) |
| `pdftotext.exe` (GCC C++) | Git for Windows | L1: symbol table and 104 C++ class names | — | ✅ |

The real builds exposed three false results that synthetic tests had missed, all fixed with regression tests: a Java class file (`DebugProbesKt.bin`) read as a Mach-O binary, Android build-generated `R` classes reported as app code, and `JetBrains.Annotations` reported as a game namespace.

## Cross-platform calibration

The test for a level is what an analyst gets back (see `protocol/02-protection-levels.md`).

| Level | Unity | Godot | Web | Same protection? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| L0 | Managed IL decompiles to near-source C# | Original GDScript text | Original JavaScript or source map | **Yes.** Source-like code with original names on every platform |
| L1 | Native code; names and structure in metadata | Binary tokens that decompile back to readable script | Minified code; property names and strings remain | **Mostly.** Names and structure survive everywhere, but recovering Godot tokens takes much less effort than Unity IL2CPP native code |

Findings:

1. **Levels are consistent** across the three platforms for every variant.
2. **L1 covers a wide range of effort.** Godot tokens and minified JavaScript are easier to recover than IL2CPP. The level still describes the same kind of exposure (names and structure readable), but recovery cost differs. This is what `04-ai-resilience-measurement.md` is for; it is not resolved in v0.1.
3. **Web confidence, resolved 2026-11-02.** Web JavaScript is now `observed` when every parameter name agrees, like Unity and Godot, so Web assets can be scored. Mixed cases stay `open`.
4. **Unity reports one result per code area only when no assets are declared.** With a manifest, unlinked code is grouped. Both behaviors are intended.

## AI recovery (first run, 2026-11-02)

Each build was given to a fresh AI agent with no conversation history, in a folder outside the repository that contained only the shipped files. The prompt did not say what to look for. The recovered function was graded against the 9 ground-truth cases and 4 invalid inputs. Full records: [`measurement/ai-recovery-2026-11-02.json`](measurement/ai-recovery-2026-11-02.json).

| Build | Level | Recovered | Purpose and formula understood | Tool calls | Time |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Web plain | L0 | 9/9, rejects 4/4 | 5/5 | 3 | 22 s |
| Web minified | L1 | 9/9, rejects 4/4 | 5/5 | 3 | 22 s |
| Web with source map | L0 | 9/9, rejects 4/4 | 5/5 | 4 | 31 s |
| Godot text | L0 | 9/9, rejects 4/4 | 5/5 | 5 | 27 s |
| Godot tokens | L1 | 9/9, rejects 4/4 | 5/5 | 10 | 131 s |
| Unity Mono | L0 | 9/9, rejects 4/4 | 5/5 | 8 | 112 s |
| Unity Android IL2CPP | L1 | ERROR: the agent stopped itself at a safety check; not counted | — | 5 | 36 s |

What this shows:

1. **L0 and L1 give no real protection against an AI analyst for small, valuable logic.** Every completed run recovered the exact formula, including input validation, without being told what to look for.
2. **L1 raises cost, not outcome.** Godot tokens took about 5 times the time of Godot text, because the agent wrote a token-format decoder first. Minified JavaScript cost nothing extra.
3. **The agents found more than the scanner reports.** The Unity Mono agent noticed the debug-file path left in `Assembly-CSharp.dll`; several noticed the command-line benchmark entry point that returns results without decompiling.
4. This supports ReShield's recommendations: move valuable logic to a server (L5), and treat L2 and above as the minimum for anything that must stay private on the client.

Limits: one run per build, one reference analyst, a 40-tool-call budget, and a tiny formula. Larger code bases will cost more to recover. The IL2CPP run needs to be repeated.

## Not covered

- Unity desktop IL2CPP was not rebuilt on this machine (no Windows IL2CPP module); those rows come from a run on another machine on 2026-10-31. Android IL2CPP was built and scanned here.
- Linux desktop and iOS builds are covered by synthetic layouts only (no Linux module; iOS needs a Mac to build).
- Unreal containers and Android R8 renaming are covered by synthetic files; no real Unreal or native Android app was built here.
- AI recovery has one run per build; the Unity IL2CPP run ended in ERROR and must be repeated.

```

`benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/README.md`:

```md
# Godot combat-formula

**English** | [繁體中文](README.zh-TW.md)

Pinned to **Godot 4.5.1.stable.official.f62fdbde1**, the standard (non-.NET) edition.
The full project includes `project.godot`, a scene, an entry point and the GDScript formula module.
The main scene actually calls the formula and shows 7 for normal damage and 14 for a critical hit.

## Two export presets

| Preset | Variant | script_export_mode | Expected scenario |
|---|---|---|---|
| Text L0 | text | 0 | Unencrypted PCK with the original UTF-8 GDScript, expected L0 |
| Tokens L1 | tokens | 1 | Unencrypted PCK with uncompressed binary tokens, expected L1 |

Mode values follow the [Godot 4.5.1 source](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/4.5.1-stable/editor/export/editor_export_preset.h).
Mode 2 is compressed binary tokens and is not used in this batch. The scene text/binary conversion option is not the script export mode.

## Tools and build (from the repository root)

If you already have the same Godot version, pass its path with `-Godot`. Otherwise you can download the official portable build:

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/setup-godot.ps1
& ./benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant text
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py godot source --godot benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py godot text --godot benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe
python benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/tests/inspect_pck.py text
```

After L0 is done on all three platforms, run:

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant tokens
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py godot tokens --godot benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe
python benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/tests/inspect_pck.py tokens
```

The build output is `builds/text/CombatBenchmark.pck` and `builds/tokens/CombatBenchmark.pck`.
This batch ships standalone PCKs, run with the same Godot version using `--main-pack`. **It is not a standalone Windows EXE export.**
Exporting a PCK does not require downloading the full platform Export Templates, which avoids an unnecessary system installation.

## Manual steps on Windows

1. Open Godot 4.5.1 → Import → select `src/project.godot`.
2. Press F6 or F5 to run the main scene and confirm 7 and 14 on screen.
3. Project → Export → choose `Text L0` or `Tokens L1`. The script export modes are Text and Binary tokens respectively; turn off PCK and directory encryption.
4. Use **Export PCK/ZIP**, choose PCK and write to the path above. Do not pick Export Project, which builds a standalone EXE.
5. Run the actual PCK from PowerShell:

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64.exe --main-pack ./benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/builds/text/CombatBenchmark.pck
```

Then run the matching `run_behavior.py` and `inspect_pck.py` to verify all 9 cases and the resource contents.
If a sandbox blocks the engine's user data or temporary directory, run from a terminal with normal write access. Do not treat exit code 0 alone as a successful export.

## Acceptance and ground truth

- `tests/ground-truth.json` holds the formula, ranges and answers. It is not under `src/`, so it never ends up in the PCK.
- The `reshield.toml` asset target (L2) is kept separate from each variant's expected level (L0 or L1).
- `expected.toml` records the required evidence; `results/` keeps measured output.
- `inspect_pck.py` reads the real PCK directory, flags, entry hashes and contents instead of guessing from the source project.
- Text must contain a `damage_calculator.gd` identical to the source. Tokens must have a `GDSC` header, the uncompressed-token setting and a `.gd.remap`, and no original script text.
- PCK behavior tests use an isolated working directory, so the source project is never available as a fallback.

The current output uses PCK format **3**, which the Godot adapter supports.
`.godot/`, PCKs, tool downloads and logs are ignored. `.gd.uid` files are source identifiers and stay under version control.
For the latest measured status, see the [RESULTS.md](../../RESULTS.md).

```

# Godot combat-formula

[English](README.md) | **繁體中文**

固定 **Godot 4.5.1.stable.official.f62fdbde1**，標準非 .NET 版。
完整專案包含 `project.godot`、場景、入口與 GDScript 計算模組。
主場景實際呼叫公式，顯示一般傷害 7、暴擊 14。

## 兩個匯出設定

| Preset | 變體 | script_export_mode | 預期情境 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Text L0 | text | 0 | 未加密 PCK 含原始 UTF-8 GDScript，預期 L0 |
| Tokens L1 | tokens | 1 | 未加密 PCK 含未壓縮 binary tokens，預期 L1 |

模式值依 [Godot 4.5.1 官方原始碼](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/4.5.1-stable/editor/export/editor_export_preset.h)。
模式 2 是 compressed binary tokens，本批次不使用。不能把場景的文字／二進位轉換選項當成腳本匯出模式。

## 工具與建置（Repository 根目錄）

已有相同版本 Godot 可直接用 `-Godot` 指定路徑；否則可選擇下載官方可攜版：

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/setup-godot.ps1
& ./benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant text
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py godot source --godot benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe

python benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/tests/inspect_pck.py text
```

完成三平台 L0 後再執行：

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant tokens
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py godot tokens --godot benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe
python benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/tests/inspect_pck.py tokens
```

建置輸出是 `builds/text/CombatBenchmark.pck` 與 `builds/tokens/CombatBenchmark.pck`。
本批次交付獨立 PCK，以相同版本 Godot `--main-pack` 執行；**不是獨立 Windows EXE 匯出**。
PCK 匯出不需要下載整包平台 Export Templates；這避免不必要的系統安裝。

## Windows 手動操作

1. 開啟 Godot 4.5.1 → Import → 選 `src/project.godot`。
2. 按 F6／F5 執行主場景，確認畫面上的 7 和 14。
3. Project → Export → 選 `Text L0` 或 `Tokens L1`；Script 匯出模式分別是 Text／Binary tokens，關閉 PCK 與目錄加密。
4. 使用 **Export PCK/ZIP**，選 PCK，輸出至上述路徑；不要誤選獨立 EXE 的 Export Project。
5. 用 PowerShell 執行實際 PCK：

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/.tools/godot-4.5.1/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64.exe --main-pack ./benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/builds/text/CombatBenchmark.pck
```

再執行對應的 `run_behavior.py` 與 `inspect_pck.py`，驗證全部 9 案例及資源內容。
如果引擎被執行沙箱擋住 user data 或 temporary directory，應在有正常寫入權限的終端執行；不要只看退出碼 0 就判定匯出成功。

## 驗收與 Ground Truth

- `tests/ground-truth.json` 保存公式、範圍與正確答案；不在 `src/`，不會被 PCK 收入。
- `reshield.toml` 的資產目標 L2 與變體的預期 L0/L1 分開。
- `expected.toml` 記錄必要證據；`results/` 保存實測輸出。
- `inspect_pck.py` 讀取真正 PCK 目錄、旗標、entry hash 與內容，不透過來源專案猜測。
- Text 必須包含與來源相同的 `damage_calculator.gd`；Tokens 必須有 `GDSC` header、未壓縮 token 設定及 `.gd.remap`，且無原始腳本文字。
- PCK 行為測試以隔離目錄為工作目錄，不提供來源專案作為 fallback。

目前產物使用 PCK format **3**，Godot 轉接層已支援此格式。
`.godot/`、PCK、工具下載與日誌都忽略；`.gd.uid` 屬來源識別資料，保留版本控制。
最新實測狀態見 [RESULTS.md](../../RESULTS.md)。

`benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/build.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [ValidateSet('text','tokens')][string]$Variant = 'text',
    [string]$Godot = "$PSScriptRoot\..\..\.tools\godot-4.5.1\Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe"
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Godot = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Godot).Path
$version = (& $Godot --version | Out-String).Trim()
if (-not $version.StartsWith('4.5.1.stable')) { throw "Expected Godot 4.5.1.stable; got $version" }
$project = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'src'
$output = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "builds/$Variant"
$results = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'results'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $output,$results | Out-Null
$artifact = Join-Path $output 'CombatBenchmark.pck'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $artifact) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $artifact }
& $Godot --headless --path $project --editor --import 2>&1 | Tee-Object -FilePath (Join-Path $results 'import.log')
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Godot import failed' }
if (Select-String -LiteralPath (Join-Path $results 'import.log') -Pattern '^ERROR:|^SCRIPT ERROR:' -Quiet) { throw 'Godot import logged errors' }
$preset = if ($Variant -eq 'text') { 'Text L0' } else { 'Tokens L1' }
& $Godot --headless --path $project --export-pack $preset (Join-Path $output 'CombatBenchmark.pck') 2>&1 | Tee-Object -FilePath (Join-Path $results "$Variant-export.log")
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Godot PCK export failed' }
if (Select-String -LiteralPath (Join-Path $results "$Variant-export.log") -Pattern '^ERROR:|^SCRIPT ERROR:' -Quiet) { throw 'Godot export logged errors (even if exit code is zero)' }
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $artifact)) { throw 'Godot did not produce a PCK' }
Write-Output "PASS Godot $Variant PCK export ($version); run behavior/artifact checks."

```

`benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/src/damage_calculator.gd`:

```gd
extends RefCounted

static func calculate(attack: int, defense: int, critical: bool) -> int:
	# The shared benchmark domain is integers in [0, 1000000].
	if attack < 0 or defense < 0 or attack > 1000000 or defense > 1000000:
		push_error("Inputs must be integers in [0, 1000000].")
		return -1
	var base_damage: int = maxi(0, attack - defense)
	return base_damage * 2 if critical else base_damage

```

`benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/src/main.gd`:

```gd
extends Node

const Damage = preload("res://damage_calculator.gd")

func _ready() -> void:
	var label := Label.new()
	label.text = "ReShield: attack=12, defense=5\nNormal: %d\nCritical: %d" % [Damage.calculate(12, 5, false), Damage.calculate(12, 5, true)]
	add_child(label)
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	var index := args.find("--cases")
	if index < 0:
		return
	var result_index := args.find("--result")
	if result_index < 0 or index + 1 >= args.size() or result_index + 1 >= args.size():
		get_tree().quit(1)
		return
	var data = JSON.parse_string(FileAccess.get_file_as_string(args[index + 1]))
	if not data is Dictionary or not data.has("cases"):
		get_tree().quit(1)
		return
	var results: Array = []
	for item in data["cases"]:
		results.append({"id": item["id"], "damage": Damage.calculate(int(item["attack"]), int(item["defense"]), bool(item["critical"]))})
	var file := FileAccess.open(args[result_index + 1], FileAccess.WRITE)
	if file == null:
		get_tree().quit(1)
		return
	file.store_string(JSON.stringify({"results": results}, "  "))
	file.close()
	get_tree().quit(0)

```

`ground-truth.json` holds the expected behavior. It must be identical to the Unity and Web copies.

PCK tests do not use the source project as the working directory, so a fallback read from the source is never counted as a successful export.

`inspect_pck.py` verifies the unencrypted flags, entry hashes and the actual text or token contents.
The helpers in this directory are benchmark-only acceptance tools; the production Godot adapter is not modified.

`benchmarks/godot/combat-formula/tests/inspect_pck.py`:

```py
"""Read-only inspection of our unencrypted standalone Godot 4.5 PCKs, formats 2/3.

Layout reference: godotengine/godot 4.5.1-stable core/io/file_access_pack.cpp.
This is a benchmark assertion helper, not the production Godot Adapter.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct


def read_pack(path):
    data = path.read_bytes()
    cursor = 0

    def take(size):
        nonlocal cursor
        if size < 0 or cursor + size > len(data):
            raise ValueError("Truncated PCK")
        result = data[cursor:cursor + size]
        cursor += size
        return result

    def number(fmt):
        return struct.unpack(fmt, take(struct.calcsize(fmt)))[0]

    if take(4) != b"GDPC":
        raise ValueError("Expected standalone PCK magic")
    version = number("<I")
    engine = [number("<I") for _ in range(3)]
    flags = number("<I")
    if flags & 1:
        raise ValueError("Expected unencrypted directory")
    base = number("<Q")
    if version == 3:
        cursor = number("<Q")
    elif version == 2:
        take(64)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported PCK version {version}")
    count = number("<I")
    entries = {}
    for _ in range(count):
        name = take(number("<I")).rstrip(b"\0").decode("utf-8")
        offset, size = number("<Q"), number("<Q")
        digest = take(16)
        entry_flags = number("<I")
        if entry_flags:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected encrypted/removed entry: {name}")
        payload = data[base + offset:base + offset + size]
        if len(payload) != size or hashlib.md5(payload).digest() != digest:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid size/hash for {name}")
        entries[name.removeprefix("res://")] = payload
    return version, engine, entries


def inspect(sample, variant):
    pack = sample / "builds" / variant / "CombatBenchmark.pck"
    version, engine, entries = read_pack(pack)
    assert engine == [4, 5, 1], engine
    assert entries.get("project.binary", b"").startswith(b"ECFG"), "Missing valid project settings"
    assert not any("ground-truth" in p or p.startswith("tests/") for p in entries)
    source = (sample / "src/damage_calculator.gd").read_bytes()
    if variant == "text":
        assert entries["damage_calculator.gd"] == source, "Text export must retain exact source"
        assert b"maxi(0, attack - defense)" in entries["damage_calculator.gd"]
        evidence = "Unencrypted PCK contains exact damage_calculator.gd UTF-8 source"
    else:
        assert "damage_calculator.gd" not in entries, "Text source leaked into tokens variant"
        tokens = entries["damage_calculator.gdc"]
        assert tokens.startswith(b"GDSC"), "Expected real binary token header"
        assert struct.unpack_from("<I", tokens, 8)[0] == 0, "Expected uncompressed binary tokens"
        assert b"damage_calculator.gdc" in entries["damage_calculator.gd.remap"]
        assert not any(source in payload for payload in entries.values()), "Source leaked"
        evidence = "Unencrypted PCK contains GDSC binary tokens and .gd.remap, no original .gd"
    return {"status": "PASS", "variant": variant, "pck_format": version, "engine": engine,
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(pack.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
            "files": sorted(entries), "evidence": evidence}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("variant", choices=["text", "tokens"])
    args = parser.parse_args()
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    report = inspect(root, args.variant)
    (root / "results").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (root / "results" / (args.variant + "-inspection.json")).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

```

`benchmarks/measurement/MEASUREMENT.md`:

```md
# Measurement method

**English** | [繁體中文](MEASUREMENT.zh-TW.md)

How ReShield benchmarks record results so that levels and scores can be checked against evidence. The Chinese version holds the full field-by-field definitions; this page summarizes them. Status: v0.1 method; the JSON files in this folder are examples, not a formal schema.

## Four layers, recorded separately

1. **Build:** the sample builds and passes its 9 ground-truth cases.
2. **Scan:** the ReShield adapter's results for that build.
3. **AI recovery:** an isolated, budgeted AI session tries to recover the formula from the build alone.
4. **Evaluation:** the recovered code is run against the ground truth.

A 9/9 build is never counted as a 9/9 recovery. A stage that did not run is `NOT RUN` with `null` values, never zero or an empty list.

## Metrics

| Metric | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Scan success rate | Scans that finished with a valid report / scans started. An empty report can be a successful run but is not a successful analysis |
| Effective analysis rate | Scans where every pre-declared asset got an observed, determined level / scans started |
| Parsing mode | Whether metadata was parsed structurally or only read by name (for example Unity v31 vs v39), grouped by engine and version |
| Semantic recovery rate | Ground-truth cases the recovered code passes / 9. Missing or failing code counts as not passed |
| Intent identification | Whether the analyst correctly identified purpose, attack minus defense, the zero floor, the critical doubling and the input range (5 items) |
| Evidence completeness | Findings with impact, a valid location, evidence and a traceable artifact / all findings |
| Asset and rule coverage | Assets with an observed, determined level, and registered rules that returned any result |
| Cost | Start and end time, elapsed seconds, manual seconds, model tokens and tool calls per stage. Unmeasured values stay `null` |

Every ratio keeps its numerator and denominator. Results are only comparable with the same reference analyst, tools, prompt, budget, ground truth, assets and variant.

## Records

Each record has a unique `test_id`, its provenance (`HISTORICAL_ACTUAL`, `ILLUSTRATIVE` or `PLANNED`), the platform, variant and build hashes, the context (ReShield version, OS, tool versions, model and budget), one block per layer, and the metrics, costs and errors. See `historical-unity.json` and `ai-recovery-2026-11-02.json`.

## Status in v0.1

Build and scan layers have real results for all three platforms; see [../RESULTS.md](../RESULTS.md). The first AI recovery run is recorded in `ai-recovery-2026-11-02.json`: 6 of 7 builds fully recovered (9/9), and one run ended in ERROR.

```

`benchmarks/measurement/MEASUREMENT.zh-TW.md`:

```md
# 量測方法

[English](MEASUREMENT.md) | **繁體中文**

ReShield 基準樣本記錄結果的方式，讓等級與分數可以拿證據檢驗。狀態：v0.1 方法；本資料夾的 JSON 是紀錄範例，不是正式 schema。

## 四個層次，分開記錄

1. **建置：** 樣本能建置，並通過 9 個標準答案案例。
2. **掃描：** ReShield 轉接層對該 build 的結果。
3. **AI 還原：** 獨立、有預算限制的 AI 工作階段，只憑 build 嘗試還原公式。
4. **評分：** 用標準答案執行還原出的程式碼。

建置 9/9 絕不算成還原 9/9。沒有執行的階段記為 `NOT RUN`，數值為 `null`，不能記成 0 或空清單。

## 指標

| 指標 | 意義 |
|---|---|
| 掃描成功率 | 正常結束並產出有效報告的掃描次數／開始的掃描次數 |
| 有效分析率 | 每個預先宣告的資產都得到「觀察到、已判定」等級的掃描次數／開始的掃描次數 |
| 解析方式 | metadata 是結構化解析還是只讀名稱（例如 Unity v31 與 v39），依引擎與版本分組 |
| 語意還原率 | 還原出的程式碼通過的標準案例數／9 |
| 意圖辨識 | 是否正確說出用途、攻擊減防禦、下限為零、暴擊加倍、輸入範圍（5 項） |
| 證據完整率 | 具備危害、有效位置、證據與可追溯檔案的 Finding／全部 Finding |
| 資產與規則涵蓋率 | 取得觀察等級的資產，以及有回傳結果的已登記規則 |
| 成本 | 每個階段的起訖時間、秒數、人工秒數、模型 token 與工具呼叫次數；未量測者為 `null` |

每個比例都保留分子與分母。只有在相同的參考分析者、工具、提示、預算、標準答案、資產與變體下，結果才能比較。

## 狀態（v0.1）

三個平台的建置與掃描層都有真實結果，見 [../RESULTS.md](../RESULTS.md)。第一次 AI 還原紀錄在 `ai-recovery-2026-11-02.json`：7 個 build 中 6 個完整還原（9/9），1 次以 ERROR 結束。

```

`benchmarks/run_behavior.py`:

```py
"""Execute only our own benchmark player against external ground truth (not an Adapter)."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("platform", choices=["unity", "godot"])
    parser.add_argument("variant")
    parser.add_argument("--godot", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    allowed = {"unity": {"mono", "il2cpp"}, "godot": {"source", "text", "tokens"}}
    if args.variant not in allowed[args.platform]:
        parser.error("Unknown variant")
    sample = ROOT / args.platform / "combat-formula"
    truth = json.loads((sample / "tests/ground-truth.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    results = sample / "results"
    results.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    inputs = results / "inputs.json"
    inputs.write_text(json.dumps({"cases": [{k: v for k, v in c.items() if k != "expected"} for c in truth["cases"]]}), encoding="utf-8")
    output = results / (args.variant + "-behavior.json")
    # Never mistake a previous successful output for this run's result.
    output.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    if args.platform == "unity":
        command = [str(sample / "builds" / args.variant / "CombatBenchmark.exe"),
                   "-batchmode", "-nographics", "-logFile", str(results / (args.variant + "-player.log")),
                   "-benchmarkCases", str(inputs), "-benchmarkResult", str(output)]
    else:
        if not args.godot:
            parser.error("--godot is required")
        # Published-pack tests must not use the source project as a fallback.
        project = sample / "src" if args.variant == "source" else results
        command = [str(args.godot.resolve()), "--headless", "--path", str(project)]
        if args.variant != "source":
            command += ["--main-pack", str(sample / "builds" / args.variant / "CombatBenchmark.pck")]
        command += ["--", "--cases", str(inputs), "--result", str(output)]
    subprocess.run(command, check=True, timeout=120, cwd=results)
    actual = json.loads(output.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))["results"]
    expected = [{"id": c["id"], "damage": c["expected"]} for c in truth["cases"]]
    if actual != expected:
        raise AssertionError(f"Behavior mismatch: {actual!r} != {expected!r}")
    print(f"PASS {args.platform}/{args.variant}: {len(expected)} ground-truth cases")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`benchmarks/setup-godot.ps1`:

```ps1
# Optional portable setup only: no PATH changes, registry changes, or system installation.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$tools = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.tools'
$destination = Join-Path $tools 'godot-4.5.1'
$archive = Join-Path $tools 'godot-4.5.1.zip'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $tools | Out-Null
Invoke-WebRequest -Uri 'https://github.com/godotengine/godot-builds/releases/download/4.5.1-stable/Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64.exe.zip' -OutFile $archive
$expected = 'DEFCCC78669E644861B4247626B01AE362CD9F23975EDF19C8BFD2EB1F6A1783'
if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $archive -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $expected) {
    throw 'Archive differs from the official release downloaded for this benchmark. Do not run it.'
}
Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $archive -DestinationPath $destination -Force
# Official Godot self-contained editor mode stores editor settings beside the executable.
New-Item -ItemType File -Force -Path (Join-Path $destination '_sc_') | Out-Null
& (Join-Path $destination 'Godot_v4.5.1-stable_win64_console.exe') --version

`benchmarks/tests/test_contracts.py`:

```py
"""Benchmark-local checks. No engine, build artifact or production Adapter required."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tomllib
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

BENCHMARKS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(BENCHMARKS.parent / "skills/reshield/scripts"))
from reshield.manifest import load_manifest


class BenchmarkContracts(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_ground_truth_identical_and_required_answers(self):
        truths = [json.loads((BENCHMARKS / p / "combat-formula/tests/ground-truth.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                  for p in ("unity", "godot", "web")]
        self.assertEqual(truths[0], truths[1])
        self.assertEqual(truths[0], truths[2])
        cases = truths[0]["cases"]
        required = {
            "normal": (12, 5, False, 7),
            "critical": (12, 5, True, 14),
            "zero": (0, 0, False, 0),
            "equal": (10, 10, False, 0),
            "defense-higher": (5, 12, False, 0),
            "zero-critical": (0, 0, True, 0),
            "equal-critical": (10, 10, True, 0),
            "defense-higher-critical": (5, 12, True, 0),
            "upper-bound": (1000000, 0, True, 2000000),
        }
        self.assertEqual(len(cases), 9)
        self.assertEqual({c["id"] for c in cases}, set(required))
        self.assertEqual(len({c["id"] for c in cases}), len(cases))
        for c in cases:
            self.assertEqual((c["attack"], c["defense"], c["critical"], c["expected"]), required[c["id"]])
            self.assertIs(type(c["critical"]), bool)
            for key in ("attack", "defense"):
                self.assertIs(type(c[key]), int)
                self.assertTrue(0 <= c[key] <= 1000000)
            self.assertEqual(c["expected"], max(0, c["attack"] - c["defense"]) * (2 if c["critical"] else 1))

    def test_rejects_incomplete_or_replaced_cases(self):
        truth = json.loads((BENCHMARKS / "unity/combat-formula/tests/ground-truth.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        mutations = {"only-five": truth["cases"][:5]}
        for index, case in enumerate(truth["cases"]):
            mutations["missing-" + case["id"]] = truth["cases"][:index] + truth["cases"][index + 1:]
        mutations["duplicate-id"] = truth["cases"][:-1] + [truth["cases"][0]]
        mutations["renamed-id"] = truth["cases"][:-1] + [dict(truth["cases"][-1], id="replacement")]
        mutations["replaced-input"] = truth["cases"][:-1] + [dict(truth["cases"][-1], attack=12, defense=5, expected=14)]
        for name, cases in mutations.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                # Simulate the same accidental change in all three copies, in memory only.
                with patch.object(Path, "read_text", return_value=json.dumps(dict(truth, cases=cases))):
                    with self.assertRaises(AssertionError):
                        self.test_ground_truth_identical_and_required_answers()

    def test_manifest_and_expected_contract(self):
        expected_levels = {"unity": {"mono": "L0", "il2cpp": "L1"},
                           "godot": {"text": "L0", "tokens": "L1"},
                           "web": {"plain": "L0", "minified": "L1", "minified-map": "L0"}}
        for platform, levels in expected_levels.items():
            with self.subTest(platform=platform):
                sample = BENCHMARKS / platform / "combat-formula"
                manifest = load_manifest(sample / "reshield.toml")
                asset = manifest.asset("combat-formulas")
                self.assertIsNotNone(asset)
                self.assertEqual(str(asset.target_level), "L2")
                self.assertFalse(asset.server_side)
                for source in asset.paths:
                    self.assertTrue((sample / source).is_file())
                    self.assertEqual(manifest.match_asset(path=source).id, asset.id)
                with (sample / "expected.toml").open("rb") as f:
                    expected = tomllib.load(f)
                self.assertEqual(expected["format_version"], 1)
                self.assertEqual(expected["platform"], platform)
                self.assertTrue((sample / expected["ground_truth"]).is_file())
                self.assertEqual({v["id"]: v["expected_level"] for v in expected["variant"]}, levels)
                for variant in expected["variant"]:
                    self.assertEqual(variant["asset_id"], asset.id)
                    self.assertEqual(variant["confidence"], "observed")
                    self.assertEqual(variant["scan_target"], "builds/" + variant["id"])
                    self.assertTrue(variant["evidence"])
                    self.assertTrue(variant["acceptance"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`benchmarks/tests/test_mono_inspection.py`:

```py
"""Static inspector regression tests. Requires PowerShell 7, no Unity build required."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
INSPECTOR = ROOT / "unity/combat-formula/tests/inspect-mono.ps1"
PWSH = shutil.which("pwsh")
SOURCE = """
namespace Reshield.Benchmarks.Combat {
    public static class DamageCalculator {
        static DamageCalculator() { throw new System.Exception("Must never execute"); }
        public static int Calculate(int attack, int defense, bool critical) {
            int value = System.Math.Max(0, attack - defense);
            return critical ? value * 2 : value;
        }
    }
}
"""


@unittest.skipUnless(PWSH, "PowerShell 7 required for static Mono inspector")
class StaticMonoInspection(unittest.TestCase):
    def inspect(self, source=None, image=None):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="reshield-static-") as tmp:
            folder = Path(tmp)
            dll = folder / "target.dll"
            (folder / "fixture.cs").write_text(source or "", encoding="utf-8")
            if image is not None:
                dll.write_bytes(image)
            # Add-Type compiles our trusted fixture to disk only. The target must
            # remain absent from loaded assemblies before AND after inspection.
            script = folder / "check.ps1"
            script.write_text("""
param($Inspector, $Folder, $Compile)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$dll = Join-Path $Folder 'target.dll'
if ($Compile -eq 'yes') {
    Add-Type -Path (Join-Path $Folder 'fixture.cs') -OutputAssembly $dll
}
function Assert-NotLoaded {
    foreach ($assembly in [AppDomain]::CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()) {
        if (-not $assembly.IsDynamic -and $assembly.Location -eq $dll) {
            throw 'Target assembly was loaded'
        }
    }
}
Assert-NotLoaded
$before = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $dll -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
try {
    & $Inspector -Dll $dll -Report (Join-Path $Folder 'report.json') | Out-Null
} finally {
    Assert-NotLoaded
    if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $dll -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $before) {
        throw 'Inspector modified target'
    }
}
""", encoding="utf-8")
            before = dll.read_bytes() if dll.exists() else None
            result = subprocess.run(
                [PWSH, "-NoProfile", "-File", str(script), str(INSPECTOR), tmp,
                 "yes" if source else "no"], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=60)
            if before is not None:
                self.assertEqual(dll.read_bytes(), before, "Inspector modified target")
            report = folder / "report.json"
            return result, json.loads(report.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) if report.exists() else None

    def test_valid_fixture_without_loading_or_execution(self):
        result, report = self.inspect(source=SOURCE)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(report["status"], "PASS")
        for instruction in ("sub", "mul", "call System.Math.Max"):
            self.assertIn(instruction, report["instructions"])

    def test_rejects_missing_metadata_and_formula_evidence(self):
        mutations = {
            "type": SOURCE.replace("DamageCalculator", "OtherCalculator"),
            "method": SOURCE.replace("Calculate(", "OtherMethod("),
            "signature": SOURCE.replace("bool critical", "int critical").replace("critical ?", "critical != 0 ?"),
            "subtract": SOURCE.replace("attack - defense", "attack + defense"),
            "multiply": SOURCE.replace("value * 2", "value + 2"),
            "max": SOURCE.replace("Math.Max", "Math.Min"),
        }
        for name, source in mutations.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                result, report = self.inspect(source=source)
                self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
                self.assertIsNone(report)
                self.assertIn("Expected", result.stderr)
                self.assertNotIn("Target assembly was loaded", result.stderr)

    def test_rejects_invalid_pe(self):
        for image in (b"", b"MZ" + bytes(100)):
            with self.subTest(length=len(image)):
                result, report = self.inspect(image=image)
                self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
                self.assertIsNone(report)

    def test_existing_unity_dll_in_isolation(self):
        dll = ROOT / "unity/combat-formula/builds/mono/CombatBenchmark_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll"
        if not dll.exists():
            self.skipTest("Build Unity Mono first for artifact integration test")
        result, report = self.inspect(image=dll.read_bytes())
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(report["status"], "PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/README.md`:

```md
# Unity 2022.3.62f3 IL2CPP combat benchmark

This is an independent project for cross-version Adapter validation. The Unity
6000.3.8f1 sample remains at `../combat-formula` and must never be opened with 2022.
The damage formula, runtime test harness and nine ground-truth cases are identical.
Project settings are generated by the pinned 2022 Editor, not downgraded from Unity 6.

## Environment

- Unity **2022.3.62f3**, revision **96770f904ca7**.
- Matching Windows Build Support (IL2CPP), MSVC x64 tools and Windows SDK.
- Python 3.11+ and PowerShell.
- Official release: https://unity.com/releases/editor/whats-new/2022.3.62f3
- Hub CLI: https://docs.unity.com/en-us/hub/use-hub-cli

```powershell
& 'C:\Program Files\Unity Hub\Unity Hub.exe' -- --headless install --version 2022.3.62f3 --changeset 96770f904ca7 --module windows-il2cpp
```

If Windows requests elevation or Unity requests login/license activation, complete
that step interactively. Do not select another Editor version.

## Build and verify (repository root)

```powershell
pwsh -File benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/build.ps1 -Variant il2cpp
python benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/tests/run_behavior.py
python benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/tests/inspect_il2cpp.py
python -B skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py scan benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/builds/il2cpp --platform unity --manifest benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/reshield.toml --format json --output benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/results/adapter-v31.json
```

Use `-Unity '<path to Unity.exe>'` for a custom installation. The build entry point
rejects other Unity versions. Windows x64, IL2CPP, non-development and no debugging
are explicitly configured. The committed scene is never saved by the build script.
Expected answers stay outside the shipped Player. Metadata v31 is an expectation;
the inspector reads the actual little-endian version at byte offset 4 after checking
magic `0xFAB11BAF`. A successful build or scan exit code alone is not acceptance.

`results/` stores logs, behavior outputs, inspection evidence and Adapter reports.
`builds/`, `results/`, Library and caches are ignored by `benchmarks/.gitignore`.
Do not commit binaries or Unity backup/recovery material.

Adapter L1 means readable metadata was observed; it does **not** satisfy the
manifest's L2 protection target. Record findings and limitations, not just exit 0.

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/build.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [ValidateSet('mono','il2cpp')][string]$Variant = 'il2cpp',
    [string]$Unity = 'C:\Program Files\Unity\Hub\Editor\2022.3.62f3\Editor\Unity.exe'
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Unity)) { throw "Unity not found: $Unity" }
$project = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'src'
$results = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'results'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $results | Out-Null
$record = Join-Path $results "$Variant-build.txt"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $record) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $record }
$log = Join-Path $results "$Variant-editor.log"
$method = if ($Variant -eq 'mono') { 'BenchmarkBuild.Mono' } else { 'BenchmarkBuild.IL2CPP' }
$argsList = @('-batchmode', '-nographics', '-quit', '-projectPath', "`"$project`"", '-buildTarget', 'Win64', '-executeMethod', $method, '-logFile', "`"$log`"")
$process = Start-Process -FilePath $Unity -ArgumentList $argsList -PassThru -Wait -WindowStyle Hidden
if ($process.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Unity build failed ($($process.ExitCode)); see $log" }
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $record)) { throw "Unity exited without a successful build record; see $log" }
Write-Output "PASS Unity $Variant build; inspect $log and run behavior/artifact checks."

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/src/Assets/Editor/BenchmarkBuild.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEngine;

public static class BenchmarkBuild
{
    [MenuItem("ReShield/Build Mono L0")]
    public static void Mono() { Build(ScriptingImplementation.Mono2x, "mono"); }

    [MenuItem("ReShield/Build IL2CPP L1")]
    public static void IL2CPP() { Build(ScriptingImplementation.IL2CPP, "il2cpp"); }

    private static void Build(ScriptingImplementation backend, string variant)
    {
        if (Application.unityVersion != "2022.3.62f3")
            throw new Exception("Benchmark pins Unity 2022.3.62f3; do not silently upgrade.");
        const string scenePath = "Assets/Scenes/Main.unity";
        // Build the checked-in scene. Never create, replace or save source scenes here.
        if (!File.Exists(Path.Combine(Application.dataPath, "Scenes/Main.unity")))
            throw new FileNotFoundException("Restore the benchmark scene before building.", scenePath);
        for (int i = 0; i < UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.sceneCount; i++)
            if (UnityEngine.SceneManagement.SceneManager.GetSceneAt(i).isDirty)
                throw new Exception("Save or discard scene edits yourself before building; the benchmark will not save them.");
        string sample = Directory.GetParent(Application.dataPath).Parent.FullName;
        string output = Path.Combine(sample, "builds", variant);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
        PlayerSettings.companyName = "ReShield";
        PlayerSettings.productName = "CombatBenchmark";
        PlayerSettings.runInBackground = true;
        PlayerSettings.SetScriptingBackend(NamedBuildTarget.Standalone, backend);
        EditorUserBuildSettings.development = false;
        EditorUserBuildSettings.allowDebugging = false;
        EditorUserBuildSettings.connectProfiler = false;
        EditorBuildSettings.scenes = new[] { new EditorBuildSettingsScene(scenePath, true) };
        var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions {
            scenes = new[] { scenePath },
            locationPathName = Path.Combine(output, "CombatBenchmark.exe"),
            target = BuildTarget.StandaloneWindows64,
            options = BuildOptions.StrictMode
        });
        if (report.summary.result != BuildResult.Succeeded)
            throw new Exception("Build failed: " + report.summary.result);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(sample, "results"));
        // Unity puts recovery/debug material beside the player. Keep it out of the scan target.
        string backup = Path.Combine(output, "CombatBenchmark_BackUpThisFolder_ButDontShipItWithYourGame");
        if (Directory.Exists(backup))
            Directory.Move(backup, Path.Combine(sample, "results", variant + "-backup-" + DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfff")));
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(sample, "results", variant + "-build.txt"),
            "Unity=" + Application.unityVersion + "\nBackend=" + PlayerSettings.GetScriptingBackend(NamedBuildTarget.Standalone)
            + "\nTarget=StandaloneWindows64\nDevelopment=false\nResult=" + report.summary.result + "\n");
    }
}

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/src/Assets/Scripts/BenchmarkApp.cs`:

```cs
using System;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;
using Reshield.Benchmarks.Combat;

// The player accepts external inputs; expected answers are never embedded in the build.
public sealed class BenchmarkApp : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Serializable] public class InputCase { public string id; public int attack; public int defense; public bool critical; }
    [Serializable] public class InputSet { public InputCase[] cases; }
    [Serializable] public class OutputCase { public string id; public int damage; }
    [Serializable] public class OutputSet { public OutputCase[] results; }

    [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.AfterSceneLoad)]
    private static void Launch() { new GameObject("Combat benchmark").AddComponent<BenchmarkApp>(); }

    private void Start()
    {
        Debug.Log("Damage example: " + DamageCalculator.Calculate(12, 5, false));
        string[] args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
        int casesIndex = Array.IndexOf(args, "-benchmarkCases");
        if (casesIndex < 0) return;
        try
        {
            int resultIndex = Array.IndexOf(args, "-benchmarkResult");
            if (resultIndex < 0) throw new ArgumentException("-benchmarkResult required");
            InputSet input = JsonUtility.FromJson<InputSet>(File.ReadAllText(args[casesIndex + 1]));
            OutputSet output = new OutputSet { results = new OutputCase[input.cases.Length] };
            for (int i = 0; i < input.cases.Length; i++)
            {
                InputCase c = input.cases[i];
                output.results[i] = new OutputCase { id = c.id, damage = DamageCalculator.Calculate(c.attack, c.defense, c.critical) };
            }
            File.WriteAllText(args[resultIndex + 1], JsonUtility.ToJson(output, true));
            Application.Quit(0);
        }
        catch (Exception e) { Debug.LogException(e); Application.Quit(1); }
    }

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        GUI.Label(new Rect(20, 20, 600, 40), "ReShield combat benchmark: attack=12, defense=5");
        GUI.Label(new Rect(20, 60, 600, 40), "Normal: " + DamageCalculator.Calculate(12, 5, false));
        GUI.Label(new Rect(20, 100, 600, 40), "Critical: " + DamageCalculator.Calculate(12, 5, true));
    }
}

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/src/Assets/Scripts/DamageCalculator.cs`:

```cs
using System;

namespace Reshield.Benchmarks.Combat
{
    public static class DamageCalculator
    {
        public static int Calculate(int attack, int defense, bool critical)
        {
            if (attack < 0 || attack > 1000000 || defense < 0 || defense > 1000000)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("Inputs must be integers in [0, 1000000].");
            int baseDamage = Math.Max(0, attack - defense);
            return critical ? baseDamage * 2 : baseDamage;
        }
    }
}

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/tests/inspect_il2cpp.py`:

```py
"""Assert artifact evidence for our Windows x64 IL2CPP benchmark, without running it."""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct

sample = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
(sample / "results/il2cpp-inspection.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)
build = sample / "builds/il2cpp"
native = build / "GameAssembly.dll"
metadata = build / "CombatBenchmark_Data/il2cpp_data/Metadata/global-metadata.dat"
data = native.read_bytes()
assert data[:2] == b"MZ"
pe = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x3C)[0]
assert data[pe:pe + 4] == b"PE\0\0"
assert struct.unpack_from("<H", data, pe + 4)[0] == 0x8664, "Expected AMD64"
optional = pe + 24
assert struct.unpack_from("<H", data, optional)[0] == 0x20B, "Expected PE32+"
clr_rva, clr_size = struct.unpack_from("<II", data, optional + 112 + 14 * 8)
assert (clr_rva, clr_size) == (0, 0), "GameAssembly should be native, not managed IL"
raw = metadata.read_bytes()
magic, version = struct.unpack_from("<II", raw)
assert magic == 0xFAB11BAF, "Expected readable IL2CPP metadata header"
for name in (b"DamageCalculator\0", b"Calculate\0", b"Reshield.Benchmarks.Combat\0"):
    assert name in raw, f"Missing readable metadata name: {name}"
assert not list(build.rglob("Assembly-CSharp.dll")), "Managed game assembly accidentally shipped"
assert not list(build.glob("*_BackUpThisFolder_ButDontShipItWithYourGame")), "Do not scan/publish Unity's backup directory"
for required in ("CombatBenchmark.exe", "UnityPlayer.dll", "CombatBenchmark_Data/globalgamemanagers"):
    assert (build / required).is_file(), f"Incomplete player: {required}"
record = (sample / "results/il2cpp-build.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
assert "Unity=2022.3.62f3" in record
assert "Backend=IL2CPP" in record and "Development=false" in record
report = {"status": "PASS", "variant": "il2cpp", "unity_version": "2022.3.62f3", "metadata_version": version,
          "evidence": ["Native AMD64 GameAssembly.dll without CLR directory", "Readable IL2CPP metadata type/method/namespace names", "No Assembly-CSharp.dll shipped"],
          "sha256": {p.relative_to(build).as_posix(): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in sorted(build.rglob('*')) if p.is_file()}}
(sample / "results/il2cpp-inspection.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula-2022/tests/run_behavior.py`:

```py
"""Run this sample's real IL2CPP player against the shared nine cases."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

sample = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
truth = json.loads((sample / 'tests/ground-truth.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
results = sample / 'results'
results.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
inputs = results / 'inputs.json'
inputs.write_text(json.dumps({'cases': [
    {k: v for k, v in c.items() if k != 'expected'} for c in truth['cases']
]}), encoding='utf-8')
output = results / 'il2cpp-behavior.json'
summary = results / 'behavior-summary.json'
output.unlink(missing_ok=True)
summary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
subprocess.run([
    str(sample / 'builds/il2cpp/CombatBenchmark.exe'),
    '-batchmode', '-nographics', '-logFile', str(results / 'il2cpp-player.log'),
    '-benchmarkCases', str(inputs), '-benchmarkResult', str(output),
], check=True, timeout=120, cwd=results)
actual = json.loads(output.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))['results']
expected = [{'id': c['id'], 'damage': c['expected']} for c in truth['cases']]
if len(expected) != 9 or actual != expected:
    raise AssertionError(f'Behavior mismatch: {actual!r} != {expected!r}')
summary.write_text(json.dumps({'status': 'PASS', 'cases': 9, 'results': actual}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
print('PASS Unity 2022.3.62f3 IL2CPP: 9 ground-truth cases')

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/README.md`:

```md
# Unity combat-formula

**English** | [繁體中文](README.zh-TW.md)

A minimal, original Unity project. The Windows x64 Mono build is expected at L0, and the IL2CPP build (readable metadata) at L1.
These are acceptance scenarios defined by the current protocol. They are not assumed to pass a scan based on file names or settings.

## Pinned environment

- Unity **6000.3.8f1**, revision `1c7db571dde0`. Do not accept prompts to upgrade the project.
- Windows x64 Player, not a Development Build, no Script Debugging.
- Mono uses the Windows Standalone support bundled with the Editor.
- IL2CPP also needs Windows Build Support (IL2CPP) for the same version, the MSVC C++ tools and the Windows SDK.
- Python 3.11+ and PowerShell 7.2+ (the IL check on build output is verified with PowerShell 7.6.5).
- Only Unity's built-in imgui and jsonserialize modules are used; versions are locked under `Packages`.

## Behavior and data

`DamageCalculator.Calculate` implements the shared formula. `BenchmarkApp` is created when the scene starts, actually calls the formula and shows the normal and critical-hit results. It is not an unreferenced sample file.

- `tests/ground-truth.json`: the expected behavior and 9 answer cases, including 5 hand-picked edge cases.
- `reshield.toml`: the `combat-formulas` asset targets L2. It does not claim that either variant reaches L2.
- `expected.toml`: the expected scenarios and the required build evidence. The provisional format is described in `benchmarks/README.md`.
- Test inputs are passed to the Player from an external file; the answers are not shipped in the build.

## Rebuild from the command line (from the repository root)

Close any Editor that has this sample open first, so Unity does not lock the project.

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant mono
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py unity mono
pwsh -File benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/tests/inspect-mono.ps1
```

After L0 is done on all three platforms, run:

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant il2cpp
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py unity il2cpp
python benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/tests/inspect_il2cpp.py
```

If Unity is installed elsewhere, pass `-Unity '<full path to Unity.exe>'`.
The build script uses the committed `Assets/Scenes/Main.unity` directly and never creates, overwrites or saves scenes. It fails if the scene is missing. If the Editor has unsaved scene changes, save or discard them before building.
The script targets Windows x64, sets the backend explicitly, and records the actual backend after a successful build.
The backend serialized in the project may be whatever the last build used, so always build through the matching entry point rather than relying on the Editor's last setting.

## Manual steps on Windows

1. Unity Hub → Add project from disk → select this sample's `src/`.
2. Open it with **6000.3.8f1** and wait for import and compilation to finish.
3. Open `Assets/Scenes/Main.unity` and press Play. The screen shows 7 for a normal hit and 14 for a critical hit.
4. Use the menu **ReShield → Build Mono L0** or **Build IL2CPP L1**. This sets the backend and runs the build.
5. Alternatively, in Build Profiles choose Windows with Architecture x86_64, confirm Mono or IL2CPP under Player Settings → Other Settings → Scripting Backend, and clear Development Build. The project's build menu is less prone to settings drift.
6. Open the matching `builds/<variant>/CombatBenchmark.exe` for a demo, then run the automated tests above to verify every case.
7. If a module is missing: Hub → Installs → 6000.3.8f1 → Add modules → Windows Build Support (IL2CPP). This changes the system installation and needs the user's permission.

## Build output and evidence

| Variant | Scan root | Required evidence |
|---|---|---|
| mono | `builds/mono/` | `CombatBenchmark_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll` with a readable `DamageCalculator.Calculate`, whose IL contains sub, Math.Max and mul |
| il2cpp | `builds/il2cpp/` | Native `GameAssembly.dll`, readable class and method names in `CombatBenchmark_Data/il2cpp_data/Metadata/global-metadata.dat`, and no managed game assembly shipped |

`tests/inspect-mono.ps1` uses .NET `PEReader` and `MetadataReader`, bundled with PowerShell, to statically parse the DLL's PE/CLI metadata, classes, method signatures and IL. It does not load or execute the target DLL or load its dependencies.
The script compiles only this repository's `tests/StaticMonoInspector.cs` reader; no NuGet packages or additional installation are needed. It retains the `sub`, `call System.Math.Max` and `mul` checks, and also validates the managed DLL format, method signatures and IL bounds.
The IL2CPP check verifies an AMD64 PE without a CLR directory, the metadata magic number and the names, and checks the build record.
These are benchmark acceptance helpers. They are not the production adapter, and not a full reverse-engineering experiment.

`results/` keeps build and Player logs, actual output and SHA-256 hashes of the build output. `builds/`, Library, Temp and Logs are not committed.
IL2CPP may also produce `*_BackUpThisFolder_ButDontShipItWithYourGame`. The build script moves it to `results/<variant>-backup-<timestamp>` so recovery data is never mistaken for shipped output or a scan target.
Version control keeps the Assets `.meta` files, scenes, Packages and ProjectSettings so the project can be rebuilt.

For the latest measured status, see the [RESULTS.md](../../RESULTS.md). Adapter integration has not passed yet; an empty findings list is not a successful acceptance.

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/README.zh-TW.md`:

```md
# Unity combat-formula

[English](README.md) | **繁體中文**

原創最小 Unity 專案；Windows x64 Mono 預期 L0，IL2CPP（可讀 metadata）預期 L1。
這是依目前協定設定的驗收情境，不是由檔名或設定推定已通過掃描。

## 固定環境

- Unity **6000.3.8f1**，revision `1c7db571dde0`。不要接受升級專案的提示。
- Windows x64 Player；非 Development Build、無 Script Debugging。
- Mono 使用 Editor 隨附 Windows Standalone 支援。
- IL2CPP 另需同版本 Windows Build Support (IL2CPP)、MSVC C++ 工具及 Windows SDK。
- Python 3.11+、PowerShell 7（產物 IL 檢查以 PowerShell 7 驗證）。
- 僅使用 Unity 內建 imgui/jsonserialize 模組；版本鎖在 Packages 下。

## 功能與資料

`DamageCalculator.Calculate` 實作共同公式。`BenchmarkApp` 在場景啟動時建立，實際呼叫公式並顯示一般攻擊與暴擊結果；不是沒有被引用的範例檔。

- `tests/ground-truth.json`：正確行為與 9 組答案，含使用者指定的 5 組案例。
- `reshield.toml`：資產 `combat-formulas` 的目標為 L2，並不宣稱這兩個變體達到 L2。
- `expected.toml`：預期情境與必要產物證據；暫定格式見 benchmarks/README.md。
- 測試輸入由外部檔案傳入 Player；正確答案不包含在發布產物。

## 命令列重建（從 Repository 根目錄）

先關閉正在開啟此樣本的 Editor，避免 Unity 專案鎖定。

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant mono
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py unity mono
pwsh -File benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/tests/inspect-mono.ps1
```

完成三平台 L0 後，再執行：

```powershell
& ./benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/build.ps1 -Variant il2cpp
python benchmarks/run_behavior.py unity il2cpp
python benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/tests/inspect_il2cpp.py
```

若 Unity 安裝位置不同，傳入 `-Unity '完整的 Unity.exe 路徑'`。
建置腳本直接使用已提交的 `Assets/Scenes/Main.unity`，不建立、覆寫或儲存場景。場景遺失時會報錯；Editor 有尚未儲存的場景修改時，需自行儲存或捨棄後再建置。
腳本指定 Windows x64、明確設定 backend，並在建置成功後記錄實際 backend。
專案內目前序列化的 backend 可能是最近一次建置值；每次應使用對應建置入口，而非依 Editor 上次的選項。

## Windows 手動操作

1. Unity Hub → Add project from disk → 選本樣本 `src/`。
2. 使用 **6000.3.8f1** 開啟；等待 import／compile 完成。
3. 開啟 `Assets/Scenes/Main.unity`，按 Play，畫面顯示一般 7、暴擊 14。
4. 選單 **ReShield → Build Mono L0** 或 **Build IL2CPP L1**。這會設定 backend 並執行建置。
5. 也可在 Build Profiles 選 Windows、Architecture x86_64；Player Settings → Other Settings → Scripting Backend 確認 Mono／IL2CPP，取消 Development Build。使用專案建置選單較能避免設定漂移。
6. 打開對應的 `builds/<variant>/CombatBenchmark.exe` 看示範；再執行上方自動測試以驗證所有案例。
7. 若缺模組：Hub → Installs → 6000.3.8f1 → Add modules → Windows Build Support (IL2CPP)。此操作修改系統安裝，須由使用者授權。

## 發布產物與證據

| 變體 | 掃描根目錄 | 必要證據 |
|---|---|---|
| mono | `builds/mono/` | `CombatBenchmark_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll`，可讀 `DamageCalculator.Calculate`，IL 有 sub、Math.Max、mul |
| il2cpp | `builds/il2cpp/` | 原生 `GameAssembly.dll`、`CombatBenchmark_Data/il2cpp_data/Metadata/global-metadata.dat` 的可讀類別／方法名稱，沒有發布 managed 遊戲 assembly |

`tests/inspect-mono.ps1` 使用反射讀取自有 DLL 的方法與 IL；不只搜尋檔名。
IL2CPP 檢查驗證 AMD64 PE、無 CLR directory、metadata magic 與名稱，並核對建置紀錄。
這是基準驗收輔助程式，不是正式 Adapter，也不是完整逆向還原實驗。

`results/` 保存建置／Player 日誌、實際輸出及產物 SHA-256；`builds/`、Library、Temp、Logs 不提交。
IL2CPP 可能另產生 `*_BackUpThisFolder_ButDontShipItWithYourGame`；建置腳本會將其移至 `results/<variant>-backup-<timestamp>`，避免將恢復資料誤當發布或掃描目標。
版本控制保留 Assets 的 `.meta`、場景、Packages、ProjectSettings，確保可重建。

最新實測狀態見 [RESULTS.md](../../RESULTS.md)。Adapter 整合尚未通過；空 findings 不是驗收成功。

```

`benchmarks/unity/combat-formula/build.ps1`:

```ps1
param(
    [ValidateSet('mono','il2cpp')][string]$Variant = 'mono',
    [string]$Unity = 'C:\Program Files\Unity\Hub\Editor\6000.3.8f1\Editor\Unity.exe'
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Unity)) { throw "Unity not found: $Unity" }
$project = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'src'
$results = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'results'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $results | Out-Null
$log = Join-Path $results "$Variant-editor.log"
$method = if ($Variant -eq 'mono') { 'BenchmarkBuild.Mono' } else { 'BenchmarkBuild.IL2CPP' }
$argsList = @('-batchmode', '-nographics', '-quit', '-projectPath', "`"$project`"", '-buildTarget', 'Win64', '-executeMethod', $method, '-logFile', "`"$log`"")
$process = Start-Process -FilePath $Unity -ArgumentList $argsList -PassThru -Wait -WindowStyle Hidden
if ($process.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Unity build failed ($($process.ExitCode)); see $log" }
Write-Output "PASS Unity $Variant build; inspect $log and run behavior/artifact checks."

```
