```py
            offset = struct.unpack("<I", binary.read(60, 4))[0]
            symbols = struct.unpack("<I", binary.read(offset + 16, 4))[0]
            if symbols:
                exposed.append(f"a symbol table with {symbols} entries")
        elif magic == b"\x7fELF":
            sections = _elf_sections(binary)
            if ".symtab" in sections:
                exposed.append("a full symbol table (.symtab), so function names are readable")
            if any(s.startswith(".debug_") for s in sections):
                exposed.append("debug information (DWARF) with names, types and source lines")
            if ".gopclntab" in sections:
                exposed.append("Go function tables, which keep every function name")
    markers = _contains(path, (b".?AV", b"go:buildid", b"runtime.main"))
    if b".?AV" in markers and magic == b"MZ":
        exposed.append("C++ class names in run-time type information")
    elif (count := _itanium_class_names(path)) >= _RTTI_MINIMUM:
        exposed.append(f"{count} C++ class names in run-time type information (GCC/Clang)")
    if {b"go:buildid", b"runtime.main"} <= markers and not any("Go" in e for e in exposed):
        exposed.append("Go function tables, which keep every function name")
    if magic not in (b"MZ", b"\x7fELF"):
        head = _contains(path, (b"__debug_info", b"__objc_classname", b"__swift5_types"), HEAD)
        if b"__debug_info" in head:
            exposed.append("debug information (DWARF) with names, types and source lines")
        if head & {b"__objc_classname", b"__swift5_types"}:
            exposed.append("Objective-C or Swift class names")
    return exposed


def _dotnet_fix() -> Remediation:
    return Remediation("Compile .NET code ahead of time or obfuscate it", Level.L1, "low", [
        "Publish with Native AOT (.NET 7 or later), which ships machine code instead of IL. ReadyToRun alone is not enough: it still includes the IL.",
        "If Native AOT does not fit your app, run an obfuscator on your own assemblies before packaging.",
        "Build again and scan; your assemblies should no longer show up as readable code.",
    ])


def _native_fix() -> Remediation:
    return Remediation("Strip symbols and debug information from release binaries", Level.L2, "none", [
        "Build in release mode and strip symbols: MSVC without /DEBUG in the shipped binary, GCC/Clang with -s or strip, Rust with strip = true, Go with -ldflags=\"-s -w\".",
        "Keep the stripped-out symbols privately for crash reports.",
        "Names that must stay (C++ class names in RTTI, Go function tables, Swift and Objective-C classes) need an obfuscator to hide.",
    ])


class NativeAdapter(Adapter):
    """Fallback for any program not built with Unity, Godot or Web technology."""

    name = "native"
    display_name = "Native and .NET"
    checked_levels = frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1, Level.L2})

    def detect(self, target: Path) -> bool:
        return bool(_binaries(target))

    def scan(self, context: ScanContext) -> list[Finding]:
        root = context.target if context.target.is_dir() else context.target.parent
        findings = []
        for path in _binaries(context.target):
            rel = _rel(path, root)
            magic = _magic(path)
            try:
                findings.extend(self._scan_binary(path, rel, magic, context))
            except (_Uncertain, struct.error, OSError, ValueError, IndexError) as error:
                findings.append(Finding(
                    "RS-NATIVE-001", "Program names and structure", self.name, Severity.INFO, None,
                    Location(rel), f"Could not read this binary: {error}. Missing evidence does not mean protection.",
                    kind=Kind.ERROR if isinstance(error, OSError) else Kind.OPEN,
                ))
        return findings

    def _scan_binary(self, path: Path, rel: str, magic: bytes, context: ScanContext) -> list[Finding]:
        if magic == b"MZ":
            with _Binary(path) as binary:
                _, cli = _pe(binary)
            if cli != (0, 0):
                return self._scan_dotnet(path, rel, context)
        exposed = _native_names(path, magic)
        asset = context.manifest.match_asset(path=rel)
        if exposed:
            return [Finding(
                "RS-NATIVE-001", "Program names and structure", self.name, Severity.MEDIUM, Level.L1,
                Location(rel), "Readable names show an attacker what each part of the program does and where to look.",
                confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED,
                evidence=[Evidence(f"{rel} ships {item}", rel) for item in exposed],
                remediation=[_native_fix()], asset_id=asset.id if asset else None,
            )]
        return [Finding(
            "RS-NATIVE-001", "Program names and structure", self.name, Severity.INFO, Level.L2,
            Location(rel), "No symbol table, debug information or class-name metadata was found. "
            "The machine code and its text strings can still be read with a disassembler.",
            confidence=Confidence.INFERRED, kind=Kind.PASS,
            evidence=[Evidence(f"{rel}: symbols and debug information appear stripped", rel)],
            asset_id=asset.id if asset else None,
        )]

    def _scan_dotnet(self, path: Path, rel: str, context: ScanContext) -> list[Finding]:
        if path.name.casefold().startswith(_DOTNET_FRAMEWORK):
            return []
        symbols = _managed_symbols(path)
        groups = {}
        for symbol in symbols:
            asset = context.manifest.match_asset(symbol=symbol, path=rel)
            groups.setdefault(asset.id if asset else None, symbol)
        return [Finding(
            "RS-DOTNET-001", ".NET code ships as readable IL", self.name, Severity.HIGH, Level.L0,
            Location(rel, symbol=symbol), "Anyone with the files can open them in a free .NET decompiler and read your code almost as you wrote it.",
            confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED,
            evidence=[Evidence(f"{rel} contains {len(symbols)} methods with readable IL, for example {code_area(symbol)}", rel)],
            remediation=[_dotnet_fix()], asset_id=asset_id,
        ) for asset_id, symbol in groups.items()]

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/adapters/unity.py`:

```py
"""Unity desktop adapter: turns observed Mono/IL2CPP exposure evidence into findings."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from ..models import Confidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Remediation, Severity, code_area
from .base import Adapter, ScanContext
from .unity_layout import PlayerInspection, has_player, inspect_player

# Rule per backend. Every scan returns a result for both rules; an unevaluated rule is never silent.
_RULES = {
    "mono": ("RS-UNITY-001", "Mono managed code shipped"),
    "il2cpp": ("RS-UNITY-002", "IL2CPP metadata readable"),
}


class UnityAdapter(Adapter):
    """Unity platform entry point. No constructor arguments; detect returns bool, scan returns list[Finding]."""

    name = "unity"
    display_name = "Unity"
    # L2 checks need the evidence interface and analysis packages proposed for v0.2; only L0/L1 are implemented.
    checked_levels = frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1})

    def detect(self, target: Path) -> bool:
        """Check the desktop layout of target: Path and return bool. Binaries are not parsed during detection."""
        return has_player(target)

    def scan(self, context: ScanContext) -> list[Finding]:
        """Read the build in context: ScanContext and return one result per rule. Declarations never hide observed exposure."""
        declared = tuple(s for asset in context.manifest.assets for s in asset.symbols)
        inspection = inspect_player(context.target, declared)
        if inspection.status != "identified":
            return self._undetermined(inspection)

        mono = inspection.backend == "mono"
        other_rule, other_title = _RULES["il2cpp" if mono else "mono"]
        not_applicable = Finding(
            rule_id=other_rule,
            title=other_title,
            platform=self.name,
            severity=Severity.INFO,
            level=None,
            kind=Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE,
            location=Location(path=inspection.evidence[0].artifact or "."),
            impact=f"This build uses {'Mono' if mono else 'IL2CPP'}, so this rule does not apply.",
            evidence=list(inspection.evidence),
        )
        rule = "RS-UNITY-001" if mono else "RS-UNITY-002"
        level = Level.L0 if mono else Level.L1
        remediation = Remediation(
            summary="Build with IL2CPP instead of Mono" if mono else "Hide class and method names with an obfuscation tool",
            target_level=Level.L1 if mono else Level.L2,
            performance_cost="low" if mono else "moderate",
            steps=(
                ["In Unity: Edit > Project Settings > Player > Other Settings > Scripting Backend, choose IL2CPP (do it for each platform you release on).",
                 "Delete the old build folder, build again, and check that the game still works and runs as fast as before.",
                 "Your code becomes much harder to read, but class and method names stay visible. Hiding them needs an obfuscation tool."]
                if mono else
                ["Add a code obfuscation tool from the Unity Asset Store and turn it on for the scripts you care about.",
                 "Build again and check that the game still works, runs as fast, and ships without debug symbols or Development Build.",
                 "ReShield cannot confirm this protection yet, so treat it as not verified."]
            ),
        )
        findings = []
        for exposure in inspection.exposures:
            # One finding per file and asset. Different symbols can map to different assets; never broadcast to every declared asset.
            # Without declared assets, report one finding per code area so the developer can see what ships and pick what to protect.
            split_areas = not context.manifest.assets
            matched = {}
            for symbol in exposure.symbols:
                asset = context.manifest.match_asset(symbol=symbol, path=exposure.path)
                # server_side is a declaration, not an exclusion: keep the match so scoring reflects the exposure.
                asset_id = asset.id if asset else None
                matched.setdefault((asset_id, code_area(symbol) if split_areas else None), symbol)
            for (asset_id, _), symbol in matched.items():
                findings.append(Finding(
                    rule_id=rule,
                    title="Game managed code ships with the Mono build" if mono else "IL2CPP metadata exposes game type and method names",
                    platform=self.name,
                    severity=Severity.HIGH if mono else Severity.MEDIUM,
                    level=level,
                    confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED,
                    location=Location(path=exposure.path, symbol=symbol),
                    impact=("Anyone with the game files can open it in a free decompiler." if mono else
                            "Readable class and method names show an attacker exactly where to look."),
                    evidence=list(inspection.evidence),
                    remediation=[remediation],
                    asset_id=asset_id,
                ))
        return [*findings, not_applicable]

    def _undetermined(self, inspection: PlayerInspection) -> list[Finding]:
        """Turn an undetermined inspection into open/error results. If the backend is known, report only its rule."""
        kind = Kind.ERROR if inspection.status == "error" else Kind.OPEN
        backends = [inspection.backend] if inspection.backend else list(_RULES)
        artifact = next((e.artifact for e in inspection.evidence if e.artifact), None)
        results = []
        for backend in backends:
            rule, title = _RULES[backend]
            results.append(Finding(
                rule_id=rule,
                title=title,
                platform=self.name,
                severity=Severity.INFO,
                level=None,
                kind=kind,
                location=Location(path=artifact or "."),
                impact=f"Unity could not determine: {inspection.reason}. Missing evidence does not mean the build is protected.",
                evidence=list(inspection.evidence),
            ))
        return results

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/adapters/unity_layout.py`:

```py
"""Read-only evidence checks for Unity desktop builds. Format parsing does not depend on the host OS."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import gzip
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import stat
import struct

from ..models import Evidence


class _Uncertain(ValueError):
    """Evidence is incomplete. The message explains why; it must never become a protection level."""


@dataclass
class Exposure:
    """An exposure: path: str is relative to the target, symbols: tuple[str, ...] are actual names."""

    path: str
    symbols: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass
class PlayerInspection:
    """Internal result: status and reason are str, backend is str | None; evidence and exposures are lists."""

    status: str
    reason: str
    backend: str | None = None
    evidence: list[Evidence] = field(default_factory=list)
    exposures: list[Exposure] = field(default_factory=list)


class _Files:
    """Fixed-depth path lookup. root: Path is the only directory reads are allowed under."""

    def __init__(self, root: Path):
        # The explicit input root may be resolved; links inside it are rejected to avoid escaping the root or looping.
        self.root = root.resolve()
        self.cache: dict[Path, dict[str, Path]] = {}

    def children(self, parent: Path) -> dict[str, Path]:
        """Return a case-insensitive index of parent: Path as dict[str, Path]."""
        if parent not in self.cache:
            entries: dict[str, Path] = {}
            for path in parent.iterdir():
                key = path.name.casefold()
                if key in entries:
                    raise _Uncertain("file names differ only by case")
                entries[key] = path
                if len(entries) > 4096:
                    raise _Uncertain("directory has more entries than the inspection limit")
            self.cache[parent] = entries
        return self.cache[parent]

    def check(self, path: Path) -> Path:
        """Check the file type of path: Path and return it if safe. Links and special files are rejected."""
        mode = path.lstat().st_mode
        if not (stat.S_ISREG(mode) or stat.S_ISDIR(mode)) or path.is_symlink():
            raise _Uncertain("evidence path contains a symbolic link or special file")
        # A Windows junction is not a regular symlink, but must not be followed either.
        if getattr(path.lstat(), "st_file_attributes", 0) & stat.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT:
            raise _Uncertain("evidence path contains a reparse point")
        return path

    def find(self, parent: Path, relative: str) -> Path | None:
        """Look up the fixed relative: str under parent: Path and return Path | None."""
        current = parent
        for part in relative.split("/"):
            if not current.is_dir():
                return None
            found = self.children(current).get(part.casefold())
            if found is None:
                return None
            current = self.check(found)
        return current

    def relative(self, path: Path) -> str:
        """Convert path: Path to a relative str for evidence, so reports never leak absolute host paths."""
        return path.relative_to(self.root).as_posix()


def _android_libs(files: _Files, root: Path, name: str) -> list[str]:
    """Relative paths of lib/<abi>/<name> in an extracted Android package."""
    lib = files.find(root, "lib")
    if lib is None or not lib.is_dir():
        return []
    return [f"lib/{abi.name}/{name}" for abi in files.children(lib).values()
            if abi.is_dir() and files.find(abi, name) is not None]


def _ios_app(files: _Files, app: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path, str] | None:
    data = files.find(app, "Data")
    framework = files.find(app, "Frameworks/UnityFramework.framework/UnityFramework")
    return (app, data, "ios") if data is not None and framework is not None else None


def _layouts(files: _Files) -> list[tuple[Path, Path, str]]:
    """Locate Players in files: _Files; return a list of (root, data directory, kind).

    kind is "desktop" (Windows, Linux), "mac", "android" (extracted APK), "ios" (extracted IPA or .app)
    or "webgl" (data directory is the Build/*.data file).
    """
    if not files.root.is_dir():
        return []
    if webgl := _webgl_data(files):
        return [(files.root, webgl, "webgl")]
    candidates = []
    if files.root.suffix.casefold() == ".app":
        data = files.find(files.root, "Contents/Resources/Data")
        if data:
            candidates.append((files.root, data, "mac"))
        elif ios := _ios_app(files, files.root):
            return [ios]
    else:
        android_data = files.find(files.root, "assets/bin/Data")
        if android_data is not None and _android_libs(files, files.root, "libunity.so"):
            return [(files.root, android_data, "android")]
        payload = files.find(files.root, "Payload")
        if payload is not None and payload.is_dir():
            apps = [ios for app in files.children(payload).values()
                    if app.name.casefold().endswith(".app") and (ios := _ios_app(files, files.check(app)))]
            if apps:
                return apps
        for name, path in files.children(files.root).items():
            if name.endswith("_data"):
                candidates.append((files.root, files.check(path), "desktop"))
            elif name.endswith(".app"):
                app = files.check(path)
                data = files.find(app, "Contents/Resources/Data")
                if data:
                    candidates.append((app, data, "mac"))
    # Markers only locate the Player; their presence says nothing about the backend or protection.
    return [item for item in candidates if any(
        (marker := files.find(item[1], name)) is not None and marker.is_file()
        for name in ("globalgamemanagers", "data.unity3d")
    )]


WEBGL_DATA_SUFFIXES = (".data", ".data.gz", ".data.br", ".data.unityweb")
WEBGL_METADATA = "Il2CppData/Metadata/global-metadata.dat"
WEBGL_LIMIT = 1024 * 1024 * 1024


def _webgl_data(files: _Files) -> Path | None:
    """The Build/<name>.data file of a Unity WebGL build, found next to its <name>.loader.js."""
    build = files.find(files.root, "Build")
    if build is None or not build.is_dir():
        return None
    names = files.children(build)
    for name, path in names.items():
        if name.endswith(".loader.js"):
            stem = name[: -len(".loader.js")]
            for suffix in WEBGL_DATA_SUFFIXES:
                if (data := names.get(stem + suffix)) is not None:
                    return files.check(data)
    return None


def _webgl_metadata(data: Path) -> bytes:
    """Read global-metadata.dat out of a UnityWebData1.0 container (plain or gzip-compressed)."""
    with data.open("rb") as stream:
        head = stream.read(2)
        stream.seek(0)
        if head == b"\x1f\x8b":
            with gzip.open(stream) as unpacked:
                raw = unpacked.read(WEBGL_LIMIT + 1)
        elif data.name.endswith((".br", ".unityweb")):
            raise _Uncertain("the WebGL data is Brotli-compressed, which this check cannot read; "
                             "in Player Settings > Publishing Settings set Compression Format to Gzip or Disabled to check it")
        else:
            raw = stream.read(WEBGL_LIMIT + 1)
    if len(raw) > WEBGL_LIMIT:
        raise _Uncertain("WebGL data is larger than the parse limit")
    if not raw.startswith(b"UnityWebData1.0\x00"):
        raise _Uncertain("not a UnityWebData1.0 container")
    header_size = struct.unpack_from("<I", raw, 16)[0]
    pos = 20
    while pos < header_size:
        offset, size, length = struct.unpack_from("<III", raw, pos)
        name = raw[pos + 12: pos + 12 + length].decode("utf-8", "replace")
        pos += 12 + length
        if name == WEBGL_METADATA:
            if offset + size > len(raw):
                raise _Uncertain("WebGL data entry is out of range")
            return raw[offset: offset + size]
    raise _Uncertain("no IL2CPP metadata inside the WebGL data")


def has_player(target: Path) -> bool:
    """Quick check for a Unity layout in target: Path; returns bool. Access errors are left to the detector."""
    try:
        return bool(_layouts(_Files(target)))
    except _Uncertain:
        return False


class _Binary:
    """Bounded random-access reader. path: Path is only opened with "rb"; files are never loaded or executed."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path):
        self.stream = path.open("rb")
        self.size = os.fstat(self.stream.fileno()).st_size
        self.used = 0

    def read(self, offset: int, size: int) -> bytes:
        """Read size bytes at offset and return bytes. Out-of-range reads or exceeding the budget stop parsing."""
        self.used += size
        if offset < 0 or size < 0 or offset + size > self.size or size > 32 * 1024 * 1024 or self.used > 64 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise _Uncertain("file truncated, offset out of range, or parse limit exceeded")
        self.stream.seek(offset)
        data = self.stream.read(size)
        if len(data) != size:
            raise _Uncertain("file changed or was truncated while reading")
        return data

    def __enter__(self) -> _Binary:
        """Return this _Binary so every parsing path closes the file through the context manager."""
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *args) -> None:
        """Close the file and return None. Exceptions are not suppressed."""
        self.stream.close()


def _pe(binary: _Binary) -> tuple[list[tuple[int, int, int]], tuple[int, int]]:
    """Parse PE sections and the CLI directory of binary: _Binary; return (sections, CLI RVA and size)."""
    if binary.read(0, 2) != b"MZ":
        raise _Uncertain("not a PE file")
    offset = struct.unpack("<I", binary.read(60, 4))[0]
    header = binary.read(offset, 24)
    if header[:4] != b"PE\0\0":
        raise _Uncertain("invalid PE signature")
    count, optional_size = struct.unpack_from("<H", header, 6)[0], struct.unpack_from("<H", header, 20)[0]
    if not 1 <= count <= 96:
        raise _Uncertain("implausible PE section count")
    optional = binary.read(offset + 24, optional_size)
    magic = struct.unpack_from("<H", optional)[0]
    directory = {0x10B: 96, 0x20B: 112}.get(magic)
    if directory is None or len(optional) < directory:
        raise _Uncertain("unsupported PE optional header")
    directories = struct.unpack_from("<I", optional, directory - 4)[0]
    if directory + directories * 8 > len(optional):
        raise _Uncertain("PE data directories exceed the header")
    cli = struct.unpack_from("<II", optional, directory + 14 * 8) if directories > 14 else (0, 0)
    sections = []
    for index in range(count):
        section = binary.read(offset + 24 + optional_size + index * 40, 40)
        _, address, size, raw = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", section, 8)
        if raw + size > binary.size:
            raise _Uncertain("PE section extends past the end of the file")
        sections.append((address, raw, size))
    if not any(size for _, _, size in sections):
        raise _Uncertain("PE has no file-backed sections")
    return sections, cli


def _rva(sections: list[tuple[int, int, int]], address: int, size: int) -> int:
    """Map address and size to sections; return the single matching file offset as int."""
    matches = [raw + address - base for base, raw, length in sections if base <= address and address + size <= base + length]
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise _Uncertain("RVA is not inside exactly one valid section")
    return matches[0]


def _macho(binary: _Binary, base: int = 0, size: int | None = None, depth: int = 0) -> bool:
    """Check a Mach-O or universal binary; base, size and depth bound the check. Returns bool."""
    size = binary.size if size is None else size
    magic = binary.read(base, 4)
    if magic in (b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe", b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbf") and depth == 0:
        count = struct.unpack(">I", binary.read(base + 4, 4))[0]
        stride = 20 if magic[-1] == 0xBE else 32
        if not 1 <= count <= 8:
            return False
        for index in range(count):
            entry = binary.read(base + 8 + index * stride, stride)
            offset, length = struct.unpack_from(">II" if stride == 20 else ">QQ", entry, 8)
            if offset < 8 + count * stride or offset + length > size or not _macho(binary, base + offset, length, 1):
                return False
        return True
    layouts = {b"\xce\xfa\xed\xfe": ("<", 28), b"\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe": ("<", 32), b"\xfe\xed\xfa\xce": (">", 28), b"\xfe\xed\xfa\xcf": (">", 32)}
    if magic not in layouts:
        return False
    endian, header_size = layouts[magic]
    header = binary.read(base, header_size)
    kind, count, command_size = struct.unpack_from(endian + "III", header, 12)
    if kind != 6 or not 1 <= count <= 4096 or header_size + command_size > size:
        return False
    cursor, loaded = header_size, False
    for _ in range(count):
        command, length = struct.unpack(endian + "II", binary.read(base + cursor, 8))
        if length < 8 or cursor + length > header_size + command_size:
            return False
        if command in (1, 0x19):
            minimum, field_offset, fmt = (56, 32, "II") if command == 1 else (72, 40, "QQ")
            if length < minimum:
                return False
            offset, file_size = struct.unpack(endian + fmt, binary.read(base + cursor + field_offset, 8 if command == 1 else 16))
            if offset + file_size > size:
                return False
            loaded |= file_size > 0
        cursor += length
    return loaded and cursor == header_size + command_size


def _native(path: Path, format_name: str) -> bool:
    """Validate the native structure of path: Path for format_name: str; returns bool. Never executes it."""
    try:
        with _Binary(path) as binary:
            if format_name == "pe":
                _, cli = _pe(binary)
                return cli == (0, 0)
            if format_name == "macho":
                return _macho(binary)
            header = binary.read(0, 64)
            if header[:4] != b"\x7fELF" or header[4] not in (1, 2) or header[5] not in (1, 2) or header[6] != 1:
                return False
            endian = "<" if header[5] == 1 else ">"
            wide = header[4] == 2
            kind = struct.unpack_from(endian + "H", header, 16)[0]
            offset = struct.unpack_from(endian + ("Q" if wide else "I"), header, 32 if wide else 28)[0]
            entry_size, count = struct.unpack_from(endian + "HH", header, 54 if wide else 42)
            if kind != 3 or entry_size != (56 if wide else 32) or not 1 <= count <= 1024:
                return False
            loaded = False
            for index in range(count):
                entry = binary.read(offset + index * entry_size, entry_size)
                segment = struct.unpack_from(endian + "I", entry)[0]
                file_offset = struct.unpack_from(endian + ("Q" if wide else "I"), entry, 8 if wide else 4)[0]
                file_size = struct.unpack_from(endian + ("Q" if wide else "I"), entry, 32 if wide else 16)[0]
                if file_offset + file_size > binary.size:
                    return False
                loaded |= segment == 1 and file_size > 0
            return loaded
    except (_Uncertain, struct.error):
        return False


def _string(data: bytes, index: int) -> str:
    """Read a NUL-terminated string at index in data and return str. Rejects out-of-range indexes and control characters."""
    # The CLR #Strings heap can share string suffixes, so no NUL is required before the index; the end and UTF-8 must still be valid.
    if not 0 <= index < len(data):
        raise _Uncertain("name index out of range")
    end = data.find(b"\0", index, index + 2049)
    if end == -1:
        raise _Uncertain("name not terminated or too long")
    value = data[index:end].decode("utf-8")
    if value and not value.isprintable():
        raise _Uncertain("name contains invalid control characters")
    return value


def _managed_symbols(path: Path) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Parse CLI types and IL methods in path: Path statically; return tuple[str, ...]. The assembly is never loaded or executed."""
    with _Binary(path) as binary:
        sections, (cli_rva, cli_size) = _pe(binary)
        if cli_size < 72:
            raise _Uncertain("missing CLI header")
        cli = binary.read(_rva(sections, cli_rva, 72), 72)
        metadata_rva, size = struct.unpack_from("<II", cli, 8)
        metadata = binary.read(_rva(sections, metadata_rva, size), size)
        if metadata[:4] != b"BSJB":
            raise _Uncertain("missing CLI metadata")
        version_length = struct.unpack_from("<I", metadata, 12)[0]
        cursor = (16 + version_length + 3) & ~3
        stream_count = struct.unpack_from("<H", metadata, cursor + 2)[0]
        cursor += 4
        if not 1 <= stream_count <= 32:
            raise _Uncertain("implausible CLI stream count")
        streams, ranges = {}, []
        for _ in range(stream_count):
            offset, length = struct.unpack_from("<II", metadata, cursor)
            end = metadata.find(b"\0", cursor + 8, cursor + 40)
            if end < 0 or offset + length > size:
                raise _Uncertain("CLI stream out of range")
            name = metadata[cursor + 8:end]
            if name in streams:
                raise _Uncertain("duplicate CLI stream name")
            streams[name] = metadata[offset:offset + length]
            if length:
                ranges.append((offset, offset + length))
            cursor = (end + 4) & ~3
        ranges.sort()
        if any(start < cursor for start, _ in ranges) or any(a[1] > b[0] for a, b in zip(ranges, ranges[1:])):
            raise _Uncertain("CLI stream overlaps the header or another stream")
        tables, strings = streams.get(b"#~"), streams.get(b"#Strings")
        # shortcut: the unoptimized #- stream is not supported yet; report unknown instead of guessing.
        if tables is None or strings is None or len(tables) < 24:
            raise _Uncertain("unsupported CLI stream")
        heaps, valid = tables[6], struct.unpack_from("<Q", tables, 8)[0]
        rows, cursor = [0] * 64, 24
        for table in range(64):
            if valid & (1 << table):
                rows[table] = struct.unpack_from("<I", tables, cursor)[0]
                cursor += 4
        if rows[2] > 100000 or rows[6] > 1000000:
            raise _Uncertain("CLI row count exceeds the inspection limit")
        widths = [4 if value >= 65536 else 2 for value in rows]
        string_width, guid_width, blob_width = (4 if heaps & bit else 2 for bit in (1, 2, 4))
        resolution = 4 if max(rows[index] for index in (0, 26, 35, 1)) >= 16384 else 2
        type_ref = 4 if max(rows[index] for index in (2, 1, 27)) >= 16384 else 2
        sizes = [2 + string_width + 3 * guid_width, resolution + 2 * string_width,
                 4 + 2 * string_width + type_ref + widths[4] + widths[6], widths[4],
                 2 + string_width + blob_width, widths[6], 8 + string_width + blob_width + widths[8]]
        offsets = []
        for index, length in enumerate(sizes):
            offsets.append(cursor)
            cursor += rows[index] * length
        if cursor > len(tables) or rows[3] or rows[5]:
            raise _Uncertain("CLI table out of range or uses unsupported pointer tables")

        def index_at(offset: int, width: int) -> int:
            """Read a little-endian index of the given width at offset in tables; returns int."""
            return struct.unpack_from("<I" if width == 4 else "<H", tables, offset)[0]

        symbols = []
        for type_index in range(rows[2]):
            start = offsets[2] + type_index * sizes[2]
            name = _string(strings, index_at(start + 4, string_width))
            namespace = _string(strings, index_at(start + 4 + string_width, string_width))
            first = index_at(start + sizes[2] - widths[6], widths[6])
            last = index_at(start + 2 * sizes[2] - widths[6], widths[6]) if type_index + 1 < rows[2] else rows[6] + 1
            if not 1 <= first <= last <= rows[6] + 1:
                raise _Uncertain("invalid CLI method range")
            for method in range(first - 1, last - 1):
                position = offsets[6] + method * sizes[6]
                rva, impl = struct.unpack_from("<IH", tables, position)
                if not rva or impl & 3:
                    continue
                body = _rva(sections, rva, 1)
                prefix = binary.read(body, 1)[0]
                if prefix & 3 == 2:
                    code_size, header_size = prefix >> 2, 1
                elif prefix & 3 == 3:
                    header = binary.read(_rva(sections, rva, 12), 12)
                    header_size = (struct.unpack_from("<H", header)[0] >> 12) * 4
                    code_size = struct.unpack_from("<I", header, 4)[0]
                    if header_size < 12:
                        raise _Uncertain("incomplete IL method header")
                else:
                    raise _Uncertain("invalid IL method format")
                _rva(sections, rva, header_size + code_size)
                method_name = _string(strings, index_at(position + 8, string_width))
                if name and name != "<Module>" and method_name and code_size:
                    symbols.append(".".join(part for part in (namespace, name, method_name) if part))
        if not symbols:
            raise _Uncertain("no confirmable managed IL methods")
        return tuple(dict.fromkeys(symbols))


def _metadata_symbols(path: Path) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Validate name references in v31 metadata at path: Path; return game methods as tuple[str, ...]."""
    with _Binary(path) as binary:
        header = binary.read(0, 256)
        # Checked against GlobalMetadataFileInternals.h from local Unity 2022.3.62f2 and 6000.0.68f1 installs.
        if struct.unpack_from("<II", header) != (0xFAB11BAF, 31):
            raise _Uncertain("metadata is not in the standard format or its version is unsupported (v31 is supported)")
        sections = [struct.unpack_from("<II", header, 8 + index * 8) for index in range(31)]
        ranges = sorted((offset, offset + size) for offset, size in sections if size)
        if any(start < 256 or end > binary.size for start, end in ranges) or any(a[1] > b[0] for a, b in zip(ranges, ranges[1:])):
            raise _Uncertain("metadata sections out of range or overlapping")
        strings, methods, types, images = [binary.read(*sections[index]) for index in (2, 5, 19, 20)]
        if not strings or not methods or not types or not images or len(methods) % 36 or len(types) % 88 or len(images) % 40:
            raise _Uncertain("metadata name tables or rows are incomplete")
        if len(images) // 40 > 4096 or len(types) // 88 > 100000 or len(methods) // 36 > 1000000:
            raise _Uncertain("metadata row count exceeds the inspection limit")
        # Image ranges must not overlap; otherwise crafted input could make one large table be walked repeatedly.
        image_ranges = sorted((start, start + count) for offset in range(0, len(images), 40)
                              for start, count in [struct.unpack_from("<II", images, offset + 8)] if count)
        if any(a[1] > b[0] for a, b in zip(image_ranges, image_ranges[1:])):
            raise _Uncertain("metadata image type ranges overlap")
        symbols = []
        for image in range(0, len(images), 40):
            name_index, _, first, count = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", images, image)
            image_name = _string(strings, name_index)
            if first + count > len(types) // 88:
                raise _Uncertain("metadata image type range out of range")
            if not _game_assembly(image_name):
                continue
            for type_index in range(first, first + count):
                offset = type_index * 88
                name_index, namespace_index = struct.unpack_from("<II", types, offset)
                name, namespace = _string(strings, name_index), _string(strings, namespace_index)
                method_start = struct.unpack_from("<I", types, offset + 36)[0]
                method_count = struct.unpack_from("<H", types, offset + 64)[0]
                if method_count == 0:
                    continue
                if method_start + method_count > len(methods) // 36:
                    raise _Uncertain("metadata method range out of range")
                for index in range(method_start, method_start + method_count):
                    method_name_index, declaring_type = struct.unpack_from("<II", methods, index * 36)
                    method_name = _string(strings, method_name_index)
                    if declaring_type != type_index or not name or not method_name:
                        raise _Uncertain("metadata method and type references are inconsistent")
                    if name != "<Module>":
                        symbols.append(".".join(part for part in (namespace, name, method_name) if part))
        if not symbols:
            raise _Uncertain("metadata contains no confirmable game type and method names")
        return tuple(dict.fromkeys(symbols))


METADATA_MAGIC = 0xFAB11BAF  # Il2CppGlobalMetadataHeader.sanity; same value in the v31 and v39 files we verified
PARSED_METADATA_VERSIONS = frozenset({31})  # layouts verified field by field
_SCAN_LIMIT = 512 * 1024 * 1024
_SCAN_CHUNK = 4 * 1024 * 1024
# PascalCase first segment, as C# namespaces are by convention; skips literals such as "config.json".
_NAMESPACE = re.compile(r"[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)+")
_RUNTIME_NAMESPACES = ("system", "unity", "unityengine", "unityeditor", "mono", "microsoft", "internal", "jetbrains", "aot")


def _runtime_namespace(name: str) -> bool:
    """True for runtime namespaces, including ones read with up to 3 stray leading bytes.

    Newer metadata does not always NUL-separate strings, so a neighboring byte can stick to
    the front of a name (seen in a real v39 build as "HUnityEngine.TextCoreTextEngineModule").
    """
    first = name.split(".", 1)[0].casefold()
    return any(first[skip:] in _RUNTIME_NAMESPACES for skip in range(4))


def _metadata_strings(path: Path) -> set[str]:
    """Return every NUL-terminated printable string in the metadata file, reading in bounded chunks."""
    tokens: set[str] = set()
    carry = b""
    read = 0
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        while chunk := stream.read(_SCAN_CHUNK):
            read += len(chunk)
            if read > _SCAN_LIMIT:
                raise _Uncertain("metadata is larger than the inspection limit")
            parts = (carry + chunk).split(b"\0")
            carry = parts.pop()[-1024:]
            for part in parts:
                if 0 < len(part) <= 1024:
                    try:
                        text = part.decode("utf-8")
                    except UnicodeDecodeError:
                        continue
                    if text.isprintable():
                        tokens.add(text)
    return tokens


def _metadata_names(path: Path, declared_symbols: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str]:
    """Version-independent check: are game names stored as plain text, the way an analyst would first look?

    Returns (symbols for asset linking, evidence text). Raises _Uncertain when no game names can be confirmed.
    """
    tokens = _metadata_strings(path)
    images = sorted(t for t in tokens if _game_assembly(t))
    namespaces = sorted(
        t for t in tokens
        if _NAMESPACE.fullmatch(t) and not _runtime_namespace(t)
        and not _game_assembly(t)
    )
    symbols = [f"{ns}.*" for ns in namespaces]
    # A declared pattern such as "Game.Combat.Damage*" is confirmed when its literal part is in plain text.
    for pattern in declared_symbols:
        literal = re.split(r"[*?\[]", pattern, maxsplit=1)[0].rstrip(".")
        if literal and (literal in tokens or literal.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in tokens):
            symbols.append(pattern)
    if not images or not symbols:
        raise _Uncertain("could not confirm readable game names in the metadata; they may be protected, "
                         "or the game uses only the global namespace and declares no symbols")
    evidence = (f"game assembly names ({', '.join(images[:3])}{', ...' if len(images) > 3 else ''}) and "
                f"{len(namespaces)} game namespace name(s) are stored as plain text")
    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(symbols)), evidence


def _metadata_exposure(path: Path, declared_symbols: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str]:
    """Decide whether IL2CPP metadata exposes names. Returns (symbols, evidence text).

    The verdict needs no version-specific layout: names an analyst can read are exposed in any version.
    A verified layout only adds method-level symbols.
    """
    with _Binary(path) as binary:
        magic, version = struct.unpack("<II", binary.read(0, 8))
    if magic != METADATA_MAGIC:
        raise _Uncertain("metadata does not start with the standard IL2CPP header; it may be encrypted or customized")
    if version in PARSED_METADATA_VERSIONS:
        return (_metadata_symbols(path),
                f"metadata v{version} sections and string references are valid; game type and method names are readable")
    symbols, evidence = _metadata_names(path, declared_symbols)
    return symbols, f"metadata v{version}: {evidence} (layout not parsed; names checked independently of version)"


def _game_assembly(name: str) -> bool:
    """Exclude known runtime assemblies. name: str is the actual file name; returns bool. Does not assume only Assembly-CSharp."""
    name = name.casefold()
    return name.endswith(".dll") and name not in ("mscorlib.dll", "netstandard.dll", "system.dll") and not name.startswith(("system.", "unityengine.", "unityeditor.", "mono.", "microsoft.")) and name not in ("unityengine.dll", "unityeditor.dll")


def inspect_player(target: Path, declared_symbols: tuple[str, ...] = ()) -> PlayerInspection:
    """Inspect target: Path read-only and return PlayerInspection. Unknown results and access failures never produce an invented level.

    declared_symbols are the manifest's symbol patterns, used to confirm names in metadata versions without a parsed layout.
    """
    evidence: list[Evidence] = []
    backend = None
    try:
        files = _Files(target)
        layouts = _layouts(files)
        if len(layouts) != 1:
            raise _Uncertain("multiple builds found; scan a single Player" if layouts else "no supported Unity Player layout found")
        root, data, kind = layouts[0]
        metadata_path = "il2cpp_data/Metadata/global-metadata.dat"
        if kind == "mac":
            # Unity 6 places the Mono runtime directly in Frameworks (verified on a real 6000.3 build).
            mono_paths = [("Contents/Frameworks/libmonobdwgc-2.0.dylib", "macho"),
                          ("Contents/Frameworks/MonoBleedingEdge/MonoEmbedRuntime/osx/libmonobdwgc-2.0.dylib", "macho")]
            il2cpp_paths = [("Contents/Frameworks/GameAssembly.dylib", "macho")]
        elif kind == "android":
            mono_paths = [(path, "elf") for path in _android_libs(files, root, "libmonobdwgc-2.0.so")]
            il2cpp_paths = [(path, "elf") for path in _android_libs(files, root, "libil2cpp.so")]
            metadata_path = "Managed/Metadata/global-metadata.dat"
        elif kind == "webgl":
            # WebGL is always IL2CPP: the code is WebAssembly and its names live in the packed metadata.
            import tempfile
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="reshield-webgl-") as tmp:
                extracted = Path(tmp) / "global-metadata.dat"
                extracted.write_bytes(_webgl_metadata(data))
                symbols, description = _metadata_exposure(extracted, declared_symbols)
            relative = f"{files.relative(data)}!/{WEBGL_METADATA}"
            evidence.append(Evidence(description, relative))
            return PlayerInspection("identified", "exposure evidence confirmed", "il2cpp", evidence,
                                    [Exposure(relative, symbols)])
        elif kind == "ios":
            # iOS always uses IL2CPP; the game code is compiled into UnityFramework.
            mono_paths = []
            il2cpp_paths = [("Frameworks/UnityFramework.framework/UnityFramework", "macho")]
            metadata_path = "Managed/Metadata/global-metadata.dat"
        else:
            # Windows keeps the Mono runtime beside the player; Linux keeps it inside <Product>_Data
            # (verified on a real 2022.3 Linux build).
            linux_mono = f"{data.relative_to(root).as_posix()}/MonoBleedingEdge/x86_64/libmonobdwgc-2.0.so"
            mono_paths = [("MonoBleedingEdge/EmbedRuntime/mono-2.0-bdwgc.dll", "pe"),
                          ("MonoBleedingEdge/x86_64/libmonobdwgc-2.0.so", "elf"), (linux_mono, "elf")]
            il2cpp_paths = [("GameAssembly.dll", "pe"), ("GameAssembly.so", "elf")]

        def libraries(candidates: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[Path]:
            """Validate native files from the candidates list of (path, format); returns list[Path]."""
            found = []
            for relative, format_name in candidates:
                path = files.find(root, relative)
                if path is not None and path.is_file() and _native(path, format_name):
                    found.append(path)
                    evidence.append(Evidence(f"Valid {format_name} native file structure: {files.relative(path)}", files.relative(path)))
            return found

        mono, il2cpp = libraries(mono_paths), libraries(il2cpp_paths)
        if mono and il2cpp:
            raise _Uncertain("conflicting Mono and IL2CPP evidence; the folder may contain stale build output")
        # One runtime per ABI is normal on Android; mixing Mono and IL2CPP is not.
        if not (mono or il2cpp):
            raise _Uncertain("no valid scripting runtime found")
        exposures = []
        if il2cpp:
            backend = "il2cpp"
            metadata = files.find(data, metadata_path)
            if metadata is None or not metadata.is_file():
                raise _Uncertain("metadata missing; this does not show that it is encrypted or that L2 is reached")
            symbols, description = _metadata_exposure(metadata, declared_symbols)
            relative = files.relative(metadata)
            evidence.append(Evidence(description, relative))
            exposures.append(Exposure(relative, symbols))
        else:
            backend = "mono"
            managed = files.find(data, "Managed")
            if managed is None or not managed.is_dir():
                raise _Uncertain("Managed directory missing")
            for path in sorted(files.children(managed).values(), key=lambda item: item.name.casefold()):
                if not _game_assembly(path.name):
                    continue
                files.check(path)
                if not path.is_file():
                    continue
                # A damaged game assembly must not be hidden by other valid ones, or the scan would falsely look complete.
                symbols = _managed_symbols(path)
                relative = files.relative(path)
                evidence.append(Evidence("PE/CLI metadata and IL method bodies are valid; game managed code ships with the build", relative))
                exposures.append(Exposure(relative, symbols))
            if not exposures:
                raise _Uncertain("no confirmable game managed assemblies")
        return PlayerInspection("identified", "exposure evidence confirmed", backend, evidence, exposures)
    except OSError as error:
        # An access failure means the check did not complete (XCCDF error); report it apart from missing evidence.
        return PlayerInspection("error", str(error), backend, evidence)
    except (ValueError, struct.error) as error:
        # Format limits or missing evidence (XCCDF unknown / SARIF open) must never raise the protection score.
        return PlayerInspection("unknown", str(error), backend, evidence)

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/adapters/unreal.py`:

```py
"""Unreal Engine packaged content: .pak files and IoStore .utoc containers.

Checks whether the index of each container is encrypted. Without encryption, free
tools list and extract every asset and Blueprint. Game C++ code is checked by the
native adapter, which runs alongside this one.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import struct
from pathlib import Path

from ..models import Confidence, Evidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Remediation, Severity
from .base import Adapter, ScanContext

PAK_MAGIC = struct.pack("<I", 0x5A6F12E1)
UTOC_MAGIC = b"-==--==--==--==-"
UTOC_FLAGS_OFFSET = 80  # FIoStoreTocHeader: magic, version, sizes, container id and key GUID come first
UTOC_ENCRYPTED = 1 << 1
MAX_CONTAINERS = 500
MAX_DEPTH = 5


def pak_encrypted(path: Path) -> bool:
    """Whether a .pak file's index is encrypted, read from its footer."""
    size = path.stat().st_size
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        f.seek(max(0, size - 4096))
        tail = f.read()
    pos = tail.rfind(PAK_MAGIC)
    if pos < 1 or pos + 8 > len(tail):
        raise ValueError("no Unreal pak footer")
    version = struct.unpack_from("<I", tail, pos + 4)[0]
    if not 1 <= version <= 32:
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported pak version {version}")
    # bEncryptedIndex sits right before the magic from pak version 4 onwards.
    return version >= 4 and tail[pos - 1] == 1


def utoc_encrypted(path: Path) -> bool:
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        header = f.read(UTOC_FLAGS_OFFSET + 1)
    if not header.startswith(UTOC_MAGIC) or len(header) <= UTOC_FLAGS_OFFSET:
        raise ValueError("not an IoStore table of contents")
    return bool(header[UTOC_FLAGS_OFFSET] & UTOC_ENCRYPTED)


def _is_unreal(path: Path) -> bool:
    """Whether a .pak or .utoc file is Unreal's. Chromium and Electron also ship .pak files."""
    suffix = path.suffix.casefold()
    if suffix not in (".pak", ".utoc"):
        return False
    try:
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            if suffix == ".utoc":
                return f.read(len(UTOC_MAGIC)) == UTOC_MAGIC
            f.seek(max(0, path.stat().st_size - 4096))
            return PAK_MAGIC in f.read()
    except OSError:
        # Unreadable: count it only where Unreal keeps its containers, so the scan reports it.
        return "paks" in (part.casefold() for part in path.parts)


def _containers(target: Path) -> list[Path]:
    if target.is_file():
        return [target] if _is_unreal(target) else []
    found, base = [], len(target.parts)
    for directory, dirs, names in os.walk(target, followlinks=False):
        if len(Path(directory).parts) - base >= MAX_DEPTH:
            dirs.clear()
        found += [Path(directory) / n for n in sorted(names) if _is_unreal(Path(directory) / n)]
        if len(found) >= MAX_CONTAINERS:
            break
    return found[:MAX_CONTAINERS]


def _fix() -> Remediation:
    return Remediation("Encrypt your packaged Unreal content", Level.L2, "small load-time cost", [
        "In Project Settings, Crypto, generate an encryption key and turn on Encrypt Pak Index and Encrypt Assets (also Sign Pak Files).",
        "Package the game again with the Shipping configuration.",
        "The key ships inside the game executable, so this slows extraction down but does not stop a determined attacker.",
    ])


class UnrealAdapter(Adapter):
    name = "unreal"
    display_name = "Unreal Engine"
    checked_levels = frozenset({Level.L1, Level.L2})

    def detect(self, target: Path) -> bool:
        return bool(_containers(target))

    def scan(self, context: ScanContext) -> list[Finding]:
        root = context.target if context.target.is_dir() else context.target.parent
        findings = []
        for path in _containers(context.target):
            rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
            try:
                encrypted = pak_encrypted(path) if path.suffix.casefold() == ".pak" else utoc_encrypted(path)
            except (ValueError, OSError, struct.error) as error:
                findings.append(Finding("RS-UNREAL-001", "Packaged Unreal content", self.name, Severity.INFO, None,
                                        Location(rel), f"Could not read this container: {error}. Missing evidence does not mean protection.",
                                        kind=Kind.ERROR if isinstance(error, OSError) else Kind.OPEN))
                continue
            asset = context.manifest.match_asset(path=rel)
            if encrypted:
                findings.append(Finding("RS-UNREAL-001", "Packaged Unreal content", self.name, Severity.INFO, Level.L2,
                                        Location(rel), "The container index is encrypted. The key ships in the game executable, "
                                        "so this slows extraction but does not stop it.", confidence=Confidence.INFERRED,
                                        kind=Kind.PASS, evidence=[Evidence(f"{rel}: encrypted index", rel)],
                                        asset_id=asset.id if asset else None))
            else:
                findings.append(Finding("RS-UNREAL-001", "Packaged Unreal content", self.name, Severity.HIGH, Level.L1,
                                        Location(rel), "Free tools can list and extract every asset and Blueprint in this file.",
                                        confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED, evidence=[Evidence(f"{rel}: index is not encrypted", rel)],
                                        remediation=[_fix()], asset_id=asset.id if asset else None))
        return findings

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/adapters/web.py`:

```py
"""Static evidence collection for Web and Electron publications."""

import json
import os
import re
import stat
from html.parser import HTMLParser
from itertools import islice
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit

from ..models import Confidence, Evidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Remediation, Severity
from .base import Adapter, ScanContext
from .web_asar import THIRD_PARTY, BudgetExceeded, LIMIT, SUFFIXES, ScanBudget, archive_entries, read_exact

IDENT = r"[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*"
JS = {".js", ".mjs", ".cjs"}
TOKEN = re.compile(r"""//[^\r\n]*|/\*[\s\S]*?(?:\*/|\Z)|"(?:\\[\s\S]|[^"\\]++)*+(?:"|$)|'(?:\\[\s\S]|[^'\\]++)*+(?:'|$)|\x60(?:\\[\s\S]|[^\x60\\]++)*+(?:\x60|$)|""" + IDENT + r"|\s+|.")
MAP_REF = re.compile(r"(?:/[/*][#@])\s*sourceMappingURL=([^\s*]+)")
BASE64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"


def _fix(is_map, level):
    """Plain-language fix for an exposure; see references/remediation/web.md."""
    if is_map:
        return Remediation("Stop shipping source maps (files that contain your original code)", Level.L1, "none", [
            "Turn off source maps for production builds (Vite: build.sourcemap = false; webpack: devtool = false; esbuild: no --sourcemap).",
            "If you need them for error reports, upload them to your error tracker instead of shipping them.",
            "Build again and check that no .map files or sourceMappingURL comments are left in the output.",
        ])
    if level == Level.L0:
        return Remediation("Minify your JavaScript for release", Level.L1, "none", [
            "Turn on minification in your bundler for production builds.",
            "Build again and check the game or app still works.",
            "Minified code is harder to read but can still be reformatted; move secrets and paid unlock checks to a server.",
        ])
    return Remediation("Move valuable logic to a server", Level.L5, "network latency and server cost", [
        "Keep prices, rewards, unlock checks and API keys on your server, and have the app ask the server for results.",
        "Anything that runs in the browser or Electron can be read, even when minified.",
    ])


def result(path, message, *, kind=Kind.OPEN, level=None, asset=None, symbol=None, line=None, inferred=False):
    is_map = path.lower().endswith(".map")
    return Finding(
        rule_id="RS-WEB-002" if is_map else "RS-WEB-001",
        title="Published source map" if is_map else "Published application code",
        platform="web", kind=kind, level=level, severity=Severity.MEDIUM if level is not None else Severity.INFO,
        location=Location(path, symbol, line), asset_id=asset.id if asset else None,
        confidence=Confidence.INFERRED if inferred else Confidence.OBSERVED,
        impact=message, evidence=[Evidence(message, path)],
        remediation=[_fix(is_map, level)] if kind is Kind.FAIL else [],
    )


def inspect_text(context, path, code, budget, source=None, first_line=1):
    """Infer readability from named implementations; formatting is never level evidence."""
    words, lines, refs = [], [], []
    line = first_line
    for token in TOKEN.finditer(code):
        budget.charge("work")
        value = token[0]
        if value.startswith("/*") and not value.endswith("*/"):
            return [result(path, "Unterminated JavaScript comment.")], refs
        if value.startswith(("//", "/*")):
            reference = MAP_REF.match(value)
            if reference:
                refs.append(reference[1])
        elif not value.isspace():
            if value[0] in "\"'\x60" and (len(value) == 1 or value[-1] != value[0]):
                return [result(path, "Unterminated JavaScript literal.")], refs
            if value == "/" and (not words or words[-1] in ("=", "(", "return", ",", ":", "[", ";")):
                return [result(path, "Regular expression literal requires unsupported syntax analysis.")], refs
            words.append(value if len(value) <= 200 else "<long token>")
            lines.append(line)
        line += value.count("\n")
    findings = {}
    braces, ends = [], {}
    for index, token in enumerate(words):
        if token == "{":
            braces.append(index)
        elif token == "}":
            if not braces:
                return [result(path, "Unmatched JavaScript closing brace.")], refs
            ends[braces.pop()] = index
    if braces:
        return [result(path, "Unclosed JavaScript body.")], refs
    for i, word in enumerate(words):
        if word == "function" and words[i + 2:i + 3] == ["("]:
            symbol, start = words[i + 1], i + 3
        elif words[i + 1:i + 3] == ["=", "{"] and words[i + 4:i + 5] == ["("]:
            symbol, start = word + "." + words[i + 3], i + 5
        else:
            continue
        if not all(re.fullmatch(IDENT, part) for part in symbol.split(".")):
            continue
        try:
            close = words.index(")", start, start + 64)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        params = words[start:close:2]
        if (ends.get(close + 1, 0) <= close + 2 or not params
                or not all(re.fullmatch(IDENT, p) for p in params)
                or any(t != "," for t in words[start + 1:close:2])):
            continue
        if all(len(p) > 1 for p in params):
            level = Level.L0
        elif all(len(p) == 1 for p in params):
            level = Level.L1
        else:
            continue
        asset = context.manifest.match_asset(symbol=symbol, path=source or path)
        origin = f"Embedded sourcesContent ({source})" if source is not None else "JavaScript"
        detail = f"{origin} contains {symbol}({', '.join(params)}) at line {lines[i]}."
        # The shipped code is read directly. A level is only assigned when every parameter
        # name agrees (all descriptive or all single letters); mixed cases stay open.
        # Decision recorded in protocol/02-protection-levels.md (v0.1, 2026-11-02).
        inferred = False
        key = (asset.id if asset else None, level)
        if key not in findings:
            findings[key] = result(path, detail, kind=Kind.FAIL, level=level, asset=asset,
                                   symbol=symbol, line=lines[i] if source is None else None, inferred=inferred)
    if not findings:
        findings[None] = result(path, "No supported named implementation with decisive parameter evidence.")
    return list(findings.values()), refs


def check_mappings(mappings, budget):
    """Check VLQ termination and segment shape, without claiming positional accuracy."""
    if not isinstance(mappings, str):
        return False
    fields = digits = 0
    for char in mappings:
        budget.charge("work")
        if char in ";,":
            if digits or fields not in (0, 1, 4, 5):
                return False
            fields = 0
        else:
            value = BASE64.find(char)
            if value < 0 or digits >= 7:
                return False
            if value < 32:
                fields += 1
                digits = 0
            else:
                digits += 1
    return not digits and fields in (0, 1, 4, 5)


def inspect_map(context, path, text, budget):
    data = json.loads(text)
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("version") != 3 or not isinstance(data.get("sources"), list):
        raise ValueError("Unsupported source map structure or version")
    sources, contents = data["sources"], data.get("sourcesContent")
    if not isinstance(contents, list) or len(contents) != len(sources) or not contents:
        raise ValueError("Missing or inconsistent sourcesContent")
    findings = []
    if not check_mappings(data.get("mappings"), budget):
        findings.append(result(path, "Invalid source map VLQ segments; positions were not established."))
    for name, code in zip(sources, contents):
        budget.charge("entries")
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(code, str) or not code.strip():
            findings.append(result(path, "Missing source name or embedded source text."))
        else:
            findings.extend(inspect_text(context, path, code, budget, name)[0])
    return findings


class Scripts(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self, analyze, reference, unsupported, budget):
        super().__init__()
        self.analyze = analyze
        self.reference = reference
        self.unsupported = unsupported
        self.budget = budget
        self.active = False

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        self.budget.charge("work")
        if tag == "base" and "href" in dict(attrs):
            self.unsupported("Custom HTML base URL was not resolved.")
        if tag != "script":
            return
        self.budget.charge("entries")
        attrs = dict(attrs)
        media = (attrs.get("type") or "").split(";")[0].strip().lower()
        if media not in ("", "module", "text/javascript", "application/javascript", "text/ecmascript",
                         "application/ecmascript"):
            self.unsupported(f"Script type {media[:80]!r} was not analyzed.")
        elif "src" in attrs:
            self.reference(attrs["src"] or "")
        else:
            self.active = True

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if self.active and data.strip():
            self.analyze(data, self.getpos()[0])

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag == "script":
            self.active = False


def resolve_ref(path, ref):
    """Resolve within a virtual publication root, without accessing the filesystem."""
    url = urlsplit(ref)
    name = unquote(url.path, errors="strict")
    if not name or url.scheme or url.netloc or "\\" in name or "\0" in name:
        raise ValueError("non-local or unsupported reference")
    parts = [] if name.startswith("/") else path.split("/")[:-1]
    for part in name.split("/"):
        if part == "..":
            if not parts:
                raise ValueError("reference escapes publication root")
            parts.pop()
        elif part not in ("", "."):
            parts.append(part)
    return "/".join(parts)


class WebAdapter(Adapter):
    name = "web"
    display_name = "Web / Electron"
    checked_levels = frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1})

    def detect(self, target: Path) -> bool:
        if target.is_file():
            return target.suffix.lower() in JS | {".asar"}
        if not target.is_dir():
            return False
        if (target / "index.html").is_file():
            return True
        # Electron keeps the app in resources/; VS Code and Squirrel installs nest it one
        # level down, in a version folder such as 2c33b50b16/ or app-1.0.9/.
        with os.scandir(target) as entries:
            folders = [target] + [Path(e.path) for e in islice(entries, 200) if e.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False)]
        if any((f / "resources/app").is_dir() or (f / "resources/app.asar").is_file() for f in folders):
            return True
        with os.scandir(target) as entries:
            return any(Path(entry.name).suffix.lower() in JS for entry in islice(entries, 12000))

    def scan(self, context: ScanContext) -> list[Finding]:
        target = context.target
        findings, seen, refs = [], set(), {}
        scripts = 0
        budget = ScanBudget()

        def inspect(root, member, raw):
            nonlocal scripts
            path = root + member
            seen.add(path)
            if raw is None:
                findings.append(result(path, "Oversized, linked or unpacked entry was not analyzed."))
                return

            def reference(ref):
                budget.charge("entries")
                try:
                    linked = root + resolve_ref(member, ref)
                    refs.setdefault((path, linked), ref[:160])
                except ValueError as exc:
                    findings.append(result(path, f"Unsupported reference {ref[:160]}: {exc}"))

            def analyze(code, line):
                nonlocal scripts
                budget.charge("entries")
                scripts += 1
                evaluated, links = inspect_text(context, path, code, budget, first_line=line)
                findings.extend(evaluated)
                for link in links:
                    reference(link)

            try:
                if path.lower().endswith((".html", ".htm")):
                    if len(raw) > 256 * 1024:
                        raise ValueError("HTML analysis size limit reached")
                    parser = Scripts(analyze, reference, lambda msg: findings.append(result(path, msg)), budget)
                    parser.feed(raw.decode("utf-8-sig"))
                    parser.close()
                    if parser.cdata_elem or parser.active:
                        raise ValueError("Unclosed HTML script element")
                elif path.lower().endswith(".map"):
                    findings.extend(inspect_map(context, path, raw.decode("utf-8-sig"), budget))
                else:
                    analyze(raw.decode("utf-8-sig"), 1)
            except (AssertionError, ValueError, RecursionError) as exc:
                findings.append(result(path, f"Cannot interpret artifact: {exc}"))

        def visit(file, path):
            try:
                info = file.lstat()
                if (file.is_symlink() or getattr(info, "st_file_attributes", 0) & stat.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT
                        or not file.resolve().is_relative_to(target.resolve())):
                    findings.append(result(path, "Linked entry was not followed."))
                elif not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
                    findings.append(result(path, "Non-regular publication entry was not analyzed."))
                elif file.suffix.lower() == ".asar":
                    for member, raw in archive_entries(file, budget):
                        inspect(path + "/", member, raw)
                else:
                    root = "resources/app/" if path.startswith("resources/app/") else ""
                    raw = None
                    if info.st_size <= LIMIT:
                        with file.open("rb") as stream:
                            raw = read_exact(stream, info.st_size, budget)
                    inspect(root, path[len(root):], raw)
            except OSError as exc:
                findings.append(result(path, f"Artifact read failed (errno {exc.errno}).", kind=Kind.ERROR))
            except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError, RecursionError) as exc:
                findings.append(result(path, f"Unsupported or malformed publication: {exc}"))

        try:
            if target.is_dir():
                pending = [target]
                while pending:
                    directory = pending.pop()
                    try:
                        with os.scandir(directory) as entries:
                            for entry in entries:
                                budget.charge("entries")
                                file = Path(entry.path)
                                path = file.relative_to(target).as_posix()
                                info = entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False)
                                if (entry.is_symlink() or getattr(info, "st_file_attributes", 0)
                                        & stat.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT):
                                    findings.append(result(path, "Linked entry was not followed."))
                                elif stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):
                                    if entry.name not in THIRD_PARTY:
                                        pending.append(file)
                                elif file.suffix.lower() in SUFFIXES or file.name.lower() == "app.asar":
                                    visit(file, path)
                    except OSError as exc:
                        findings.append(result(directory.relative_to(target).as_posix(),
                                               f"Directory enumeration failed (errno {exc.errno}).", kind=Kind.ERROR))
            else:
                visit(target, target.name)
        except BudgetExceeded as exc:
            findings.append(result(".", str(exc)))
        for (path, linked), ref in refs.items():
            if linked not in seen:
                findings.append(result(path, f"Referenced artifact unavailable: {ref}"))
        if not scripts:
            findings.append(result(".", "No readable JavaScript publication entry was found."))
        return findings

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/adapters/web_asar.py`:

```py
"""Bounded, read-only traversal of Electron ASAR publications."""

import json
import re
import struct
from pathlib import Path

LIMIT = 8 * 1024 * 1024
HEADER_LIMIT = 32 * 1024 * 1024  # the file list of a large app (875 MB) is about 2 MB
SUFFIXES = {".js", ".mjs", ".cjs", ".map", ".html", ".htm"}
# Open-source packages the app depends on: public already, and not the developer's code.
THIRD_PARTY = {"node_modules", "bower_components"}


class BudgetExceeded(Exception):
    """The rest of this scan must be reported as incomplete."""


class ScanBudget:
    def __init__(self):
        self.remaining = {"bytes": 64 * 1024 * 1024, "entries": 12000, "work": 1_000_000}

    def charge(self, key, count=1):
        self.remaining[key] -= count
        if self.remaining[key] < 0:
            raise BudgetExceeded(f"Scan {key} limit reached; remaining publication was not analyzed.")


def read_exact(stream, size, budget):
    budget.charge("bytes", size)
    data = stream.read(size)
    if len(data) != size:
        raise ValueError("Truncated publication entry")
    return data


def archive_entries(path, budget):
    """Validate ranges before reading payloads; never extract archive members."""
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        size = stream.seek(0, 2)
        stream.seek(0)
        tag, block, payload, length = struct.unpack("<4I", read_exact(stream, 16, budget))
        if (tag != 4 or block != payload + 4 or payload != 4 + (length + 3) // 4 * 4
                or length > HEADER_LIMIT or 8 + block > size):
            raise ValueError("Invalid or oversized ASAR header")
        tree = json.loads(read_exact(stream, length, budget))
        pending = [("", tree["files"])]
        ranges = []
        skipped = []
        while pending:
            prefix, entries = pending.pop()
            for name, entry in entries.items():
                if (not isinstance(entry, dict) or not name or name in (".", "..")
                        or any(c in name for c in "/\\:\0") or len(prefix) + len(name) > 4096):
                    raise ValueError("Invalid ASAR member")
                member = prefix + name
                if name in THIRD_PARTY and "files" in entry:
                    continue
                if "link" in entry or entry.get("unpacked"):
                    skipped.append(member)
                elif "files" in entry:
                    pending.append((member + "/", entry["files"]))
                elif Path(name).suffix.lower() in SUFFIXES:
                    budget.charge("entries")  # only code counts; images and data are just names here
                    offset, length = entry["offset"], entry["size"]
                    if not isinstance(offset, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,20}", offset):
                        raise ValueError("Invalid ASAR offset")
                    offset = int(offset)
                    if type(length) is not int or length < 0 or 8 + block + offset + length > size:
                        raise ValueError("Invalid ASAR member range")
                    ranges.append((offset, length, member))
        ranges.sort()
        end = 0
        for offset, length, member in ranges:
            if length and offset < end:
                raise ValueError("Overlapping ASAR payloads; archive was not analyzed")
            end = max(end, offset + length)
        for member in skipped:
            yield member, None
        for offset, length, member in ranges:
            if length > LIMIT:
                yield member, None
            else:
                stream.seek(8 + block + offset)
                yield member, read_exact(stream, length, budget)

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/archive.py`:

```py
"""Read packaged builds (.apk, .aab, .ipa, .zip, .xapk) by extracting a private copy.

Spec: protocol/proposals/v0.2-containers.md

The original file is only read. Entries are extracted into a temporary folder that
is deleted after the scan, with limits against zip bombs and path tricks.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

ARCHIVE_SUFFIXES = {".apk", ".aab", ".ipa", ".zip", ".xapk", ".apks"}
MAX_ENTRIES = 200_000
MAX_TOTAL = 8 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_RATIO = 200  # uncompressed / compressed, per entry


class ArchiveError(ValueError):
    pass


def is_archive(target: Path) -> bool:
    return target.is_file() and target.suffix.casefold() in ARCHIVE_SUFFIXES and zipfile.is_zipfile(target)


def _safe_name(name: str) -> PurePosixPath | None:
    path = PurePosixPath(name.replace("\\", "/"))
    if path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts or not path.parts or ":" in path.parts[0]:
        return None
    return path


def extract(target: Path, into: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Extract target into the folder `into`. Returns entry names that were skipped and why."""
    skipped = []
    total = 0
    with zipfile.ZipFile(target) as archive:
        entries = archive.infolist()
        if len(entries) > MAX_ENTRIES:
            raise ArchiveError(f"more than {MAX_ENTRIES} entries")
        for info in entries:
            if info.is_dir():
                continue
            name = _safe_name(info.filename)
            # Symbolic links in zip files carry the link flag in the upper bits of external_attr.
            if name is None or (info.external_attr >> 16) & 0o170000 == 0o120000:
                skipped.append(f"{info.filename}: unsafe path or link")
                continue
            if info.compress_size and info.file_size / info.compress_size > MAX_RATIO:
                skipped.append(f"{info.filename}: suspicious compression ratio")
                continue
            total += info.file_size
            if total > MAX_TOTAL:
                raise ArchiveError(f"more than {MAX_TOTAL // (1024 ** 3)} GB when extracted")
            destination = into.joinpath(*name.parts)
            destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with archive.open(info) as source, destination.open("wb") as sink:
                shutil.copyfileobj(source, sink, 1024 * 1024)
    # Split packages (.xapk, .apks) contain more packages: open those one level down.
    for inner in list(into.rglob("*.apk")):
        if zipfile.is_zipfile(inner):
            folder = inner.with_suffix(inner.suffix + ".contents")
            folder.mkdir()
            skipped += extract(inner, folder)
    return skipped

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/cli.py`:

```py
"""Command-line entry point.

    python reshield_cli.py scan <target> [--platform unity|godot|web]
                                         [--manifest reshield.toml]
                                         [--format markdown|sarif|json]
                                         [--output FILE]
    python reshield_cli.py platforms
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import tempfile
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from . import __version__
from .adapters import ADAPTERS
from .detection import detect_target
from .manifest import ManifestError
from .report import WRITERS
from .scanner import NoPlatformDetected, run_scan


def _report_destination(target: Path, output: Path) -> Path:
    """Reject destinations that would modify the resolved scan target."""
    original_target = Path(os.path.abspath(target))
    original_output = Path(os.path.abspath(output))
    target = target.resolve()
    destination = output.resolve()
    # Reject paths named inside the build even if a symlink points outward.
    # Resolve the parent separately to also cover aliases of the build root.
    output_entry = output.parent.resolve() / output.name
    if target.is_dir() and any(
        path.is_relative_to(root)
        for path in (original_output, output_entry)
        for root in (original_target, target)
    ):
        raise ValueError("Report output must be outside the scan target.")
    if destination == target or (target.is_dir() and destination.is_relative_to(target)):
        raise ValueError("Report output must be outside the scan target.")
    if target.is_file() and destination.exists() and destination.samefile(target):
        raise ValueError("Report output refers to the scan target file.")
    if destination.exists() and not destination.is_file():
        raise ValueError("Report output must be a regular file.")
    return destination


def _write_report(target: Path, output: Path, report: str) -> None:
    """Replace the destination rather than truncate a potentially shared inode.

    An external report path may be a hard link to a file inside a build
    directory. Replacing its directory entry preserves the build's bytes.
    This assumes filesystem paths are not concurrently changed by others.
    """
```
