```py
    destination = _report_destination(target, output)
    temporary = None
    try:
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
            mode="w", encoding="utf-8", dir=destination.parent,
            prefix=".reshield-report-", delete=False,
        ) as stream:
            temporary = Path(stream.name)
            stream.write(report)
        os.replace(temporary, destination)
    finally:
        if temporary is not None:
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Parse argv and return an exit code. Only scan inputs in the frozen contract are offered."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="reshield", description="Measure reverse-engineering resistance.")
    parser.add_argument("--version", action="version", version=f"reshield {__version__}")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    detect = sub.add_parser("detect", help="Detect the build platform without scanning; outputs JSON")
    detect.add_argument("target", type=Path)

    scan = sub.add_parser("scan", help="Scan a build directory or file")
    scan.add_argument("target", type=Path)
    scan.add_argument("--platform", choices=[a.name for a in ADAPTERS])
    scan.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, help="Path to reshield.toml (auto-detected if omitted)")
    scan.add_argument("--format", choices=sorted(WRITERS), default="markdown")
    scan.add_argument("--output", type=Path, help="Write the report to a file instead of stdout")

    sub.add_parser("platforms", help="List supported platforms")

    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    # Paths can hold any character; a Windows console or pipe defaults to a local code page (cp950, cp1252)
    # that cannot print them, and agents read the output as UTF-8.
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
            stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

    if args.command == "platforms":
        for a in ADAPTERS:
            print(f"{a.name}\t{a.display_name}")
        return 0

    if args.command == "detect":
        result = detect_target(args.target)
        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 2 if result["status"] == "error" else 0

    if not args.target.exists():
        print(f"error: {args.target} does not exist. Check the path and try again.", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    if args.output:
        try:
            _report_destination(args.target, args.output)
        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as error:
            print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2

    try:
        result = run_scan(args.target, manifest_path=args.manifest, platform=args.platform)
    except (NoPlatformDetected, ManifestError) as e:
        print(f"error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    report = WRITERS[args.format](result)
    if args.output:
        try:
            _write_report(args.target, args.output, report)
        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as error:
            print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        print(f"Report written to {args.output}")
    else:
        print(report)
    return 0

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/core_checks.py`:

```py
"""Checks that apply to every build, whatever the platform.

Spec: protocol/02-protection-levels.md#cross-platform-findings

- RS-CORE-001: credentials shipped in the build (API keys, private keys, tokens, key stores).
- RS-CORE-002: debug files shipped in the build (symbol files, Unity "do not ship" folders).
- RS-CORE-004: code in a format no adapter checks yet (Python, Flutter, React Native, Java, Lua, ...).
- RS-CORE-005: a known protection tool was applied (VMProtect, Themida, ConfuserEx, ...).

The scan is read-only and bounded. Secrets are never written out in full.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
from pathlib import Path

from .manifest import Manifest
from .models import Confidence, Evidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Remediation, Severity

# High-precision patterns only: a false alarm about a leaked key wastes the developer's time.
_SECRETS = [
    ("Anthropic API key", rb"sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{32,}"),
    # Legacy keys are 48 plain characters; project and service keys are long and prefixed.
    ("OpenAI API key", rb"sk-(?:[A-Za-z0-9]{48}(?![A-Za-z0-9])|(?:proj|svcacct|admin)-[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{80,})"),
    # Key ids are base32: digits 0, 1, 8 and 9 never appear.
    ("AWS access key", rb"(?<![A-Z0-9])(?:AKIA|ASIA)[A-Z2-7]{16}(?![A-Z0-9])"),
    ("Google API key", rb"AIza[0-9A-Za-z_\-]{35}"),
    ("GitHub token", rb"gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{36,}"),
    ("Slack token", rb"xox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9\-]{10,}"),
    ("Stripe secret key", rb"[sr]k_live_[0-9A-Za-z]{24,}"),
    ("GitLab token", rb"glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{20}"),
    ("npm token", rb"npm_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}(?![A-Za-z0-9])"),
    ("Hugging Face token", rb"hf_[A-Za-z0-9]{34}(?![A-Za-z0-9])"),
    ("SendGrid API key", rb"SG\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{22}\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{43}"),
    ("Azure storage key", rb"AccountKey=[A-Za-z0-9+/]{86}=="),
    ("Firebase Cloud Messaging server key", rb"AAAA[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{7}:APA91b[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{134}"),
    ("Google OAuth client secret", rb"GOCSPX-[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{28}"),
    # Starts at ":AA" so the search does not try every digit in the file.
    ("Telegram bot token", rb"(?<=[0-9]{8}):AA[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{33}(?![A-Za-z0-9_\-])"),
    ("Slack webhook", rb"https://hooks\.slack\.com/services/T[A-Z0-9]{8,}/B[A-Z0-9]{8,}/[A-Za-z0-9]{24}"),
    ("Discord webhook", rb"https://(?:ptb\.|canary\.)?discord(?:app)?\.com/api/webhooks/[0-9]{17,20}/[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{60,68}"),
    # The header alone appears in every TLS library (OpenSSL, mbedTLS), so require real key data after it.
    ("Private key", rb"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |DSA |OPENSSH |PGP |ENCRYPTED )?PRIVATE KEY-----\r?\n[A-Za-z0-9+/=\r\n]{64,}"),
]
# Text every match must contain. A plain substring search is far faster than a regex,
# so a pattern only runs on data that holds one of its hints.
_SECRET_HINTS = {
    "Anthropic API key": (b"sk-ant-",), "OpenAI API key": (b"sk-",), "AWS access key": (b"AKIA", b"ASIA"),
    "Google API key": (b"AIza",), "GitHub token": (b"ghp_", b"gho_", b"ghu_", b"ghs_", b"ghr_"),
    "Slack token": (b"xox",), "Stripe secret key": (b"k_live_",), "GitLab token": (b"glpat-",),
    "npm token": (b"npm_",), "Hugging Face token": (b"hf_",), "SendGrid API key": (b"SG.",),
    "Azure storage key": (b"AccountKey=",), "Firebase Cloud Messaging server key": (b":APA91b",),
    "Google OAuth client secret": (b"GOCSPX-",), "Telegram bot token": (b":AA",),
    "Slack webhook": (b"hooks.slack.com",), "Discord webhook": (b"/api/webhooks/",),
    "Private key": (b"PRIVATE KEY-----",),
}
# One search per pattern is several times faster than one large alternation.
_SECRET_PATTERNS = [(name, re.compile(pattern), _SECRET_HINTS[name],
                     tuple(h.decode().encode("utf-16-le") for h in _SECRET_HINTS[name]))
                    for name, pattern in _SECRETS]

# Files whose presence alone ships a credential.
_CREDENTIAL_FILES = {".p12": "certificate with private key", ".pfx": "certificate with private key",
                     ".keystore": "Android signing key store", ".jks": "Java key store"}
_CREDENTIAL_NAMES = {".env": "environment file with settings and keys"}

# Debug files: what an analyst gets from them, and the level that implies.
_DEBUG_SUFFIXES = {".pdb": "Windows debug symbols", ".mdb": "Mono debug symbols",
                   ".debug": "separate debug information", ".sym": "symbol file"}
_DEBUG_DIR_SUFFIXES = {".dsym": "Apple debug symbols"}
_DO_NOT_SHIP = {
    "_backupthisfolder_butdontshipitwithyourgame": ("Unity IL2CPP backup with the generated C++ source of your game", Level.L0),
    "_burstdebuginformation_donotship": ("Unity Burst debug information", Level.L1),
}

# Code that runs inside its own runtime. The native check only sees the runtime's executable,
# so without this a Python or Flutter app could look protected. Name: (what an attacker gets, how to fix).
_RUNTIMES = {
    "Python": ("free tools turn the shipped .pyc files back into readable Python.",
               ["Compile the code you care about with Nuitka or Cython instead of shipping .pyc files.",
                "Keep keys and important rules on your server."]),
    "Flutter (Dart)": ("free tools can recover your Dart class and function names from the app.",
                       ["Build with: flutter build <platform> --obfuscate --split-debug-info=<private folder>.",
                        "Keep the debug-info folder private; you need it to read crash reports."]),
    "React Native": ("the JavaScript bundle can be read, or decompiled if it is Hermes bytecode.",
                     ["Turn on Hermes and do not ship source maps.",
                      "Keep keys and important rules on your server."]),
    "Java": ("Java .jar and .class files can be opened with a free tool and read almost as written.",
             ["Run ProGuard or another Java obfuscator on the release .jar.",
              "Keep keys and important rules on your server."]),
    "Lua": ("Lua scripts and Lua bytecode can be read or decompiled with free tools.",
            ["Do not ship plain .lua files; compile and encrypt them with your engine's tools.",
             "Keep keys and important rules on your server."]),
    "GameMaker": ("free tools can open data.win and show your GML code.",
                  ["In GameMaker, build with the YYC (compiled) target instead of VM."]),
    "Ren'Py": ("free tools turn .rpyc and .rpa files back into readable scripts.",
               ["Keep important rules and keys on a server; Ren'Py scripts cannot be fully hidden."]),
    "Xamarin / .NET MAUI": ("the .NET code inside the app can be extracted and read almost as written.",
                            ["Turn on AOT compilation and trimming, and use a .NET obfuscator for the code you care about."]),
}
_RUNTIME_NAMES = {"libapp.so": "Flutter (Dart)", "app.so": "Flutter (Dart)", "index.android.bundle": "React Native",
                  "main.jsbundle": "React Native", "base_library.zip": "Python", "data.win": "GameMaker",
                  "game.unx": "GameMaker", "game.ios": "GameMaker", "game.droid": "GameMaker",
                  "assemblies.blob": "Xamarin / .NET MAUI"}
_RUNTIME_SUFFIXES = {".pyc": "Python", ".jar": "Java", ".class": "Java", ".lua": "Lua", ".luac": "Lua",
                     ".rpyc": "Ren'Py", ".rpa": "Ren'Py"}
_PYINSTALLER_COOKIE = b"MEI\x0c\x0b\x0a\x0b\x0e"
_EXECUTABLE_MAGIC = (b"MZ", b"\x7fELF", b"\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe", b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe")


def _runtime(path: Path, name: str, suffix: str, size: int) -> str | None:
    lower = name.casefold()
    if lower in _RUNTIME_NAMES:
        return _RUNTIME_NAMES[lower]
    if suffix in _RUNTIME_SUFFIXES:
        return _RUNTIME_SUFFIXES[suffix]
    if lower.startswith("libassemblies.") and lower.endswith(".blob.so"):
        return "Xamarin / .NET MAUI"
    if size > 1024 * 1024:  # PyInstaller appends its archive to the executable
        with path.open("rb") as stream:
            if stream.read(4).startswith(_EXECUTABLE_MAGIC):
                stream.seek(-min(size, 64 * 1024), os.SEEK_END)
                if _PYINSTALLER_COOKIE in stream.read():
                    return "Python"
    return None


def _runtime_findings(runtimes: dict[str, str]) -> list[Finding]:
    findings = []
    for runtime, path in runtimes.items():
        hint, steps = _RUNTIMES[runtime]
        findings.append(Finding(
            rule_id="RS-CORE-004", title=f"{runtime} code", platform="core", severity=Severity.INFO, level=None,
            location=Location(path), kind=Kind.OPEN,
            impact=f"This build contains {runtime} code, which ReShield cannot check yet. Usually {hint}",
            evidence=[Evidence(f"{runtime} code found: {path}", path)],
            remediation=[Remediation(f"Protect the {runtime} code", Level.L2, "depends on the tool", steps)],
        ))
    return findings


# Known protection tools and the marks they leave. Section names are only matched in a PE header;
# the other marks are attribute or version strings the tools write into the file.
_PE_SECTION_TOOLS = re.compile(rb"\.(vmp[0-9])\x00|\.(themida)|\.(winlice)|\.(enigma[12])\x00")
_PE_SECTION_NAMES = {"vmp": "VMProtect", "themida": "Themida", "winlice": "WinLicense", "enigma": "Enigma Protector"}
_TOOL_MARKS = [
    ("ConfuserEx", b"ConfusedByAttribute"),
    ("Dotfuscator", b"DotfuscatorAttribute"),
    ("SmartAssembly", b"SmartAssembly.Attributes"),
    ("Babel Obfuscator", b"BabelObfuscatorAttribute"),
    ("Obfuscator-LLVM", b"Obfuscator-LLVM"),
]
_TOOL_RE = re.compile(b"|".join(re.escape(mark) for _, mark in _TOOL_MARKS))
_TOOL_BY_MARK = {mark: name for name, mark in _TOOL_MARKS}
_JS_OBFUSCATOR = re.compile(rb"\b_0x[0-9a-f]{4,6}\b")
JS_OBFUSCATOR_MIN = 200  # javascript-obfuscator renames everything to _0x...; plain minifiers never do


def _tool_finding(tool: str, path: str, asset_id: str | None) -> Finding:
    return Finding(
        rule_id="RS-CORE-005", title=f"{tool} protection", platform="core", severity=Severity.INFO,
        level=Level.L2, location=Location(path), confidence=Confidence.INFERRED, kind=Kind.PASS,
        impact=f"{tool} was applied to this file. Most tools protect only the code you select, "
               "so check that your important code is included.",
        evidence=[Evidence(f"{tool} marks found in {path}", path)], asset_id=asset_id,
    )


_UTF16 = re.compile(rb"([\x20-\x7e])\x00")
CHUNK = 8 * 1024 * 1024
OVERLAP = 512
MAX_FILES = 50_000
MAX_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024


def _mask(value: bytes) -> str:
    text = value.decode("ascii", "replace")
    return text[: min(8, len(text) // 3)] + "****"


def _rel(path: Path, root: Path) -> str:
    return path.relative_to(root).as_posix() if path != root else path.name


def _secret_fix() -> Remediation:
    return Remediation("Remove the key from the build and replace it", Level.L5, "a server endpoint for calls that need it", [
        "Cancel this key in the website or service that gave it to you, and create a new one. Removing it from the release is not enough.",
        "Move calls that need the key to your own server, and let the game or app call that server instead.",
        "Build again and scan to check the key is gone.",
    ])


def _debug_fix() -> Remediation:
    return Remediation("Do not ship debug files", Level.L2, "none", [
        "Delete these files or folders from the copy you upload. Keep a private copy if you use them for crash reports.",
        "For Unity, delete folders whose names end in DontShipItWithYourGame or DoNotShip before uploading.",
        "Build or package again and scan to check they are gone.",
    ])


def _secret_finding(path: str, kind_name: str, sample: str | None, asset_id: str | None) -> Finding:
    shown = f" ({sample})" if sample else ""
    return Finding(
        rule_id="RS-CORE-001", title="A key or password ships with the build", platform="core",
        severity=Severity.CRITICAL, level=Level.L0, location=Location(path),
        impact=f"Anyone with the files can copy this {kind_name}{shown} and use it as you.",
        confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED, evidence=[Evidence(f"{kind_name} found in {path}{shown}", path)],
        remediation=[_secret_fix()], asset_id=asset_id,
    )


def _debug_finding(path: str, what: str, level: Level, asset_id: str | None) -> Finding:
    return Finding(
        rule_id="RS-CORE-002", title="Debug files ship with the build", platform="core",
        severity=Severity.HIGH if level is Level.L0 else Severity.MEDIUM, level=level, location=Location(path),
        impact=f"This {what} gives an attacker your code's names and structure for free.",
        confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED, evidence=[Evidence(f"{what}: {path}", path)],
        remediation=[_debug_fix()], asset_id=asset_id,
    )


def _scan_secrets(path: Path, tools: set[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    """Return {secret kind: first match} found in the file, reading in bounded, overlapping chunks.

    Protection tools found on the way are added to tools.
    """
    found: dict[str, bytes] = {}
    tools = set() if tools is None else tools
    tail = b""
    first = True
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        while chunk := stream.read(CHUNK):
            if first:
                first = False
                if chunk.startswith(b"MZ"):
                    for match in _PE_SECTION_TOOLS.finditer(chunk[:4096]):
                        section = next(g for g in match.groups() if g).decode()
                        tools.add(next(v for k, v in _PE_SECTION_NAMES.items() if section.startswith(k)))
                if path.suffix.casefold() in (".js", ".mjs", ".cjs"):
                    if len(_JS_OBFUSCATOR.findall(chunk)) >= JS_OBFUSCATOR_MIN:
                        tools.add("javascript-obfuscator")
            data = tail + chunk
            tools.update(_TOOL_BY_MARK[m.group()] for m in _TOOL_RE.finditer(data))
            # .NET and Windows store many strings as UTF-16. The UTF-16 view removes only the NUL
            # after each character, so the double NUL between strings still keeps them apart.
            utf16 = None
            for name, pattern, hints, wide in _SECRET_PATTERNS:
                if name in found:
                    continue
                if any(h in data for h in hints) and (match := pattern.search(data)):
                    found[name] = match.group()
                elif any(h in data for h in wide):
                    if utf16 is None:
                        utf16 = _UTF16.sub(rb"\1", data)
                    if match := pattern.search(utf16):
                        found[name] = match.group()
            tail = data[-OVERLAP:]
    return found


def scan(target: Path, manifest: Manifest) -> tuple[list[Finding], str | None]:
    """Run the cross-platform checks. Returns (findings, reason the check stopped early or None)."""
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    root = target if target.is_dir() else target.parent
    files = [target] if target.is_file() else None
    budget, count = MAX_BYTES, 0
    runtimes: dict[str, str] = {}
    unreadable: list[str] = []

    def link(rel: str) -> str | None:
        asset = manifest.match_asset(path=rel)
        return asset.id if asset else None

    walker = [(str(root), [], [target.name])] if files else os.walk(target, followlinks=False)
    for directory, dirs, names in walker:
        base = Path(directory)
        # File systems list entries in different orders; sort so every OS gives the same report.
        dirs.sort()
        for d in list(dirs):
            lower = d.casefold()
            match = next((v for k, v in _DO_NOT_SHIP.items() if lower.endswith(k)), None)
            if match is None and Path(lower).suffix in _DEBUG_DIR_SUFFIXES:
                match = (_DEBUG_DIR_SUFFIXES[Path(lower).suffix], Level.L1)
            if match is not None:
                rel = _rel(base / d, root)
                findings.append(_debug_finding(rel, match[0], match[1], link(rel)))
                dirs.remove(d)  # reported as a whole; do not descend
        for name in sorted(names):
            path = base / name
            if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
                continue
            count += 1
            if count > MAX_FILES:
                return findings + _runtime_findings(runtimes), f"more than {MAX_FILES} files"
            rel = _rel(path, root)
            suffix = path.suffix.casefold()
            if suffix in _DEBUG_SUFFIXES:
                findings.append(_debug_finding(rel, _DEBUG_SUFFIXES[suffix], Level.L1, link(rel)))
                continue
            if suffix in _CREDENTIAL_FILES or name.casefold() in _CREDENTIAL_NAMES:
                what = _CREDENTIAL_FILES.get(suffix) or _CREDENTIAL_NAMES[name.casefold()]
                findings.append(_secret_finding(rel, what, None, link(rel)))
                continue
            try:
                size = path.stat().st_size
                runtime = _runtime(path, name, suffix, size)
                if runtime and runtime not in runtimes:
                    runtimes[runtime] = rel
                budget -= size
                if budget < 0:
                    return findings + _runtime_findings(runtimes), f"more than {MAX_BYTES // (1024 ** 3)} GB of files"
                tools: set[str] = set()
                secrets = _scan_secrets(path, tools)
            except OSError:  # locked, no permission, or removed while scanning
                unreadable.append(rel)
                continue
            for kind_name, value in secrets.items():
                findings.append(_secret_finding(rel, kind_name, _mask(value), link(rel)))
            findings += [_tool_finding(tool, rel, link(rel)) for tool in sorted(tools)]
    if unreadable:
        more = f" and {len(unreadable) - 1} more" if len(unreadable) > 1 else ""
        return findings + _runtime_findings(runtimes), f"could not read {unreadable[0]}{more}"
    return findings + _runtime_findings(runtimes), None


def incomplete_result(reason: str) -> Finding:
    """An open result when the credential check could not cover the whole build."""
    return Finding(
        rule_id="RS-CORE-001", title="A key or password ships with the build", platform="core",
        severity=Severity.INFO, level=None, location=Location("."), kind=Kind.OPEN,
        impact=f"The credential check stopped early ({reason}). Files after that point were not checked.",
    )

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/detection.py`:

```py
"""Read-only platform detection for agents, without scanning or scoring."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from .adapters import ADAPTERS


def detect_target(target: Path) -> dict:
    """Evaluate registered detectors in priority order and expose all matches.

    The adapter v0 contract returns only a boolean. Do not invent file-level
    evidence or confidence scores from that result. Errors block selection
    because an unavailable higher-priority detector may change the outcome.
    """
    target = target.resolve()
    result = {
        "target": str(target),
        "status": "unknown",
        "selected_platform": None,
        "matches": [],
        "errors": [],
        "selection_policy": "first matching adapter in registry order",
        "limitations": [
            "Detection identifies a candidate platform, not protection or scan coverage.",
            "Adapter detect() returns a boolean; file-level evidence is unavailable.",
        ],
    }
    if not target.is_file() and not target.is_dir():
        result["status"] = "error"
        result["errors"].append({"platform": None, "message": "Target must be an existing file or directory."})
        return result

    for adapter in ADAPTERS:
        try:
            if adapter.detect(target):
                result["matches"].append({
                    "platform": adapter.name,
                    "display_name": adapter.display_name,
                    "basis": "adapter.detect(target) returned true",
                })
        except OSError as error:
            result["errors"].append({"platform": adapter.name, "message": str(error)})

    if result["errors"]:
        result["status"] = "error"
    elif result["matches"]:
        result["status"] = "detected"
        result["selected_platform"] = result["matches"][0]["platform"]
    return result

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/manifest.py`:

```py
"""Load the asset declaration file (reshield.toml).

Spec: protocol/01-asset-declaration.md
Example: examples/reshield.toml
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import tomllib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from fnmatch import fnmatchcase
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

from .models import AssetValue, Level

MANIFEST_NAME = "reshield.toml"

ASSET_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$")
PERFORMANCE_BUDGETS = ("none", "low", "moderate", "high")

# Used when an asset omits target_level. See protocol/01-asset-declaration.md.
DEFAULT_TARGET_LEVEL = {
    AssetValue.CRITICAL: Level.L3,
    AssetValue.HIGH: Level.L2,
    AssetValue.MEDIUM: Level.L2,
    AssetValue.LOW: Level.L1,
}


@lru_cache(maxsize=None)
def _path_glob(pattern: str) -> re.Pattern[str]:
    """Compile a path glob: `*` and `?` stay within one folder, `**` spans folders."""
    regex = ""
    i = 0
    while i < len(pattern):
        if pattern.startswith("**/", i):
            regex += "(?:.*/)?"
            i += 3
        elif pattern.startswith("**", i):
            regex += ".*"
            i += 2
        elif pattern[i] == "*":
            regex += "[^/]*"
            i += 1
        elif pattern[i] == "?":
            regex += "[^/]"
            i += 1
        else:
            regex += re.escape(pattern[i])
            i += 1
    return re.compile(regex + r"\Z")


@dataclass
class Asset:
    id: str
    description: str
    value: AssetValue
    target_level: Level
    paths: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # source globs, relative to the project root
    symbols: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # names as they appear in the build
    performance_budget: str = "low"
    server_side: bool = False

    def matches(self, symbol: str | None = None, path: str | None = None) -> bool:
        """True if a build symbol or path belongs to this asset."""
        if symbol and any(fnmatchcase(symbol, p) for p in self.symbols):
            return True
        if path:
            path = path.replace("\\", "/")
            return any(_path_glob(p).match(path) for p in self.paths)
        return False


@dataclass
class Manifest:
    project_name: str | None = None
    assets: list[Asset] = field(default_factory=list)
    source: Path | None = None

    def asset(self, asset_id: str) -> Asset | None:
        return next((a for a in self.assets if a.id == asset_id), None)

    def match_asset(self, symbol: str | None = None, path: str | None = None) -> Asset | None:
        """Find the asset a finding belongs to. Adapters use this to set Finding.asset_id."""
        return next((a for a in self.assets if a.matches(symbol=symbol, path=path)), None)


class ManifestError(ValueError):
    pass


def _str_list(raw: dict, key: str) -> list[str]:
    items = raw.get(key, [])
    if not isinstance(items, list) or not all(isinstance(i, str) for i in items):
        raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a list of strings")
    return items


def _string(raw: dict, key: str, default: str) -> str:
    value = raw.get(key, default)
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a string")
    return value


# id, value, target_level, paths, symbols, performance_budget, server_side
def _parse_asset(raw: dict) -> Asset:
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ValueError("expected a table; declare each asset with [[asset]]")
    if "id" not in raw:
        raise ValueError("id is required")
    asset_id = raw["id"]
    if not isinstance(asset_id, str) or not ASSET_ID_PATTERN.match(asset_id):
        raise ValueError(f"id must be kebab-case: {asset_id!r}")

    value_name = _string(raw, "value", "medium")
    try:
        value = AssetValue(value_name)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise ValueError("value must be one of critical, high, medium, low") from error

    server_side = raw.get("server_side", False)
    if not isinstance(server_side, bool):
        raise ValueError("server_side must be true or false")

    if "target_level" in raw:
        level_name = _string(raw, "target_level", "")
        try:
            target_level = Level[level_name]
        except KeyError as error:
            raise ValueError("target_level must be one of L0, L1, L2, L3, L4, L5") from error
    else:
        target_level = Level.L5 if server_side else DEFAULT_TARGET_LEVEL[value]

    budget = _string(raw, "performance_budget", "low")
    if budget not in PERFORMANCE_BUDGETS:
        raise ValueError(f"performance_budget must be one of {', '.join(PERFORMANCE_BUDGETS)}")

    paths = _str_list(raw, "paths")
    symbols = _str_list(raw, "symbols")
    if not paths and not symbols:
        raise ValueError("at least one of paths or symbols is required")

    return Asset(
        id=asset_id,
        description=_string(raw, "description", ""),
        value=value,
        target_level=target_level,
        paths=paths,
        symbols=symbols,
        performance_budget=budget,
        server_side=server_side,
    )


def load_manifest(path: Path) -> Manifest:
    try:
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            data = tomllib.load(f)
    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as e:
        raise ManifestError(f"{path}: invalid TOML: {e}") from e
    except UnicodeDecodeError as e:
        raise ManifestError(f"{path}: manifest must be valid UTF-8") from e
    except OSError as e:
        reason = e.strerror or type(e).__name__
        raise ManifestError(f"{path}: unable to read manifest: {reason}") from e

    project = data.get("project", {})
    if not isinstance(project, dict):
        raise ManifestError(f"{path}: project must be a table")
    if "name" in project and not isinstance(project["name"], str):
        raise ManifestError(f"{path}: project.name must be a string")

    entries = data.get("asset", [])
    if not isinstance(entries, list):
        raise ManifestError(f"{path}: declare assets with [[asset]], not [asset]")

    assets = []
    seen = set()
    for i, raw in enumerate(entries):
        try:
            asset = _parse_asset(raw)
        except ValueError as e:
            raise ManifestError(f"{path}: invalid [[asset]] entry #{i + 1}: {e}") from e
        if asset.id in seen:
            raise ManifestError(f"{path}: duplicate asset id {asset.id!r}")
        seen.add(asset.id)
        assets.append(asset)

    return Manifest(
        project_name=project.get("name"),
        assets=assets,
        source=path,
    )


def find_manifest(target: Path) -> Path | None:
    """Look for reshield.toml in the target directory and its parents.

    The search stops at the first directory containing .git, so a manifest
    outside the project's repository is never picked up.
    """
    start = target if target.is_dir() else target.parent
    for directory in [start, *start.parents]:
        candidate = directory / MANIFEST_NAME
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate
        if (directory / ".git").exists():
            break
    return None

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/models.py`:

```py
"""Core data model shared by every adapter and report writer.

Spec: protocol/02-protection-levels.md, protocol/05-report-format.md
Schema: protocol/schemas/finding.schema.json
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, IntEnum
from pathlib import PurePosixPath, PureWindowsPath

RULE_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^RS-[A-Z]+-[0-9]{3}$")


def code_area(symbol: str | None) -> str | None:
    """The code area a build symbol belongs to: its namespace, or its type for global-namespace code.

    "Game.Combat.Damage.Calculate" -> "Game.Combat"; "Player..ctor" -> "Player"; "Game.Combat.*" -> "Game.Combat".
    """
    if not symbol:
        return None
    if symbol.endswith(".*"):
        return symbol[:-2]
    # Constructors are named ".ctor"/".cctor", so "Type..ctor" splits at the double dot.
    type_name = symbol.split("..", 1)[0] if ".." in symbol else symbol.rsplit(".", 1)[0]
    return type_name.rsplit(".", 1)[0] if "." in type_name else type_name


class Level(IntEnum):
    """Protection level. Higher means more expensive to reverse engineer."""

    L0 = 0  # Shipped as-is
    L1 = 1  # Compiled, but symbols/metadata expose names and structure
    L2 = 2  # Symbols/metadata stripped or encrypted
    L3 = 3  # Control flow and data transformed
    L4 = 4  # Critical code virtualized
    L5 = 5  # Asset never ships to the client

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.name


class Severity(str, Enum):
    CRITICAL = "critical"
    HIGH = "high"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    LOW = "low"
    INFO = "info"


class Confidence(str, Enum):
    """How the reported level was established."""

    OBSERVED = "observed"  # seen directly in the build artifact
    INFERRED = "inferred"  # concluded from heuristics; may be wrong
    DECLARED = "declared"  # stated in reshield.toml; cannot be verified from the build


class AssetValue(str, Enum):
    CRITICAL = "critical"
    HIGH = "high"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    LOW = "low"


@dataclass
class Location:
    """Where a finding applies. `path` is relative to the scan target."""

    path: str
    symbol: str | None = None
    line: int | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if PurePosixPath(self.path).is_absolute() or PureWindowsPath(self.path).is_absolute():
            raise ValueError(f"Location.path must be relative to the scan target: {self.path!r}")
        self.path = self.path.replace("\\", "/")


@dataclass
class Evidence:
    """What was observed, so the developer can verify the finding."""

    description: str
    artifact: str | None = None


@dataclass
class Remediation:
    summary: str
    target_level: Level
    performance_cost: str  # e.g. "none", "low", "2-5x on affected code"
    steps: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


class Kind(str, Enum):
    """Outcome of evaluating one rule. Mirrors SARIF 2.1.0 result.kind (3.27.9).

    See protocol/05-report-format.md for the SARIF and XCCDF 1.2 mapping.
    """

    FAIL = "fail"  # exposure found; `level` is the level observed
    PASS = "pass"  # rule's protection conditions confirmed; `level` is the level supported
    OPEN = "open"  # evaluated, but not enough information to decide
    ERROR = "error"  # evaluation could not complete (access, I/O)
    NOT_APPLICABLE = "notapplicable"  # rule does not apply to this target


#: Kinds that leave the protection of an asset undetermined.
UNDETERMINED_KINDS = frozenset({Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR})


@dataclass
class Finding:
    """The result of evaluating one rule against the build.

    The name is kept for compatibility; with `kind` other than "fail" a Finding
    is not a problem but a statement about what was or was not established.
    """

    rule_id: str  # e.g. "RS-UNITY-001"
    title: str
    platform: str
    severity: Severity
    level: Level | None  # required for fail/pass, None for every other kind
    location: Location
    impact: str  # fail: what happens if the asset is recovered; otherwise: what this result means
    confidence: Confidence = Confidence.OBSERVED
    evidence: list[Evidence] = field(default_factory=list)
    remediation: list[Remediation] = field(default_factory=list)
    asset_id: str | None = None  # matches an asset id from reshield.toml; None means the whole build
    kind: Kind = Kind.FAIL  # SARIF default

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """Validate field consistency; raises ValueError."""
        if not RULE_ID_PATTERN.match(self.rule_id):
            raise ValueError(f"rule_id must look like RS-<PLATFORM>-NNN: {self.rule_id!r}")
        has_level = self.kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS)
        if has_level and self.level is None:
            raise ValueError(f"{self.rule_id}: kind {self.kind.value!r} requires a level")
        if not has_level and self.level is not None:
            raise ValueError(f"{self.rule_id}: kind {self.kind.value!r} must not carry a level")
        # SARIF 3.27.10: only "fail" results carry a problem severity.
        if self.kind is not Kind.FAIL and self.severity is not Severity.INFO:
            raise ValueError(f"{self.rule_id}: kind {self.kind.value!r} must use severity 'info'")

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        """Return a JSON-compatible dict with the frozen contract fields only."""
        data = asdict(self)
        data["kind"] = self.kind.value
        data["severity"] = self.severity.value
        data["level"] = str(self.level) if self.level is not None else None
        data["confidence"] = self.confidence.value
        for r in data["remediation"]:
            r["target_level"] = str(r["target_level"])
        return data

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/report.py`:

```py
"""Report writers: Markdown (for people), SARIF (for tools), JSON (for the skill).

Spec: protocol/05-report-format.md
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

from . import __version__
from .actions import developer_actions, to_summary
from .models import UNDETERMINED_KINDS, AssetValue, Confidence, Kind, Location, Severity
from .scanner import ScanResult
from .scoring import ASSET_WEIGHTS, SCORE_POLICY
from .severity import SEVERITY_POLICY

INFORMATION_URI = "https://github.com/swimchicken777/reshield"

_SARIF_KIND = {
    Kind.FAIL: "fail",
    Kind.PASS: "pass",
    Kind.OPEN: "open",
    Kind.ERROR: "open",  # plus a toolExecutionNotification; see protocol/05-report-format.md
    Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE: "notApplicable",
}

_SARIF_LEVEL = {
    Severity.CRITICAL: "error",
    Severity.HIGH: "error",
    Severity.MEDIUM: "warning",
    Severity.LOW: "note",
    Severity.INFO: "note",
}


def to_json(result: ScanResult) -> str:
    """Render result as JSON with the frozen contract fields, so consumers stay compatible."""
    s = result.summary
    data = {
        "tool": {"name": "reshield", "version": __version__},
        "target": str(result.target),
        "manifest": str(result.manifest.source) if result.manifest.source else None,
        "platforms": [a.name for a in result.platforms],
        "summary": {
            "complete": s.complete,
            "overall_level": str(s.overall_level) if s.overall_level is not None else None,
            "overall_level_is_upper_bound": not s.complete and s.overall_level is not None,
            "score": s.score,
            "severity_counts": s.severity_counts,
            "kind_counts": s.kind_counts,
            "not_checked_levels": [str(lv) for lv in s.not_checked_levels],
            "assets": [
                {
                    "id": a.asset_id,
                    "target_level": str(a.target_level),
                    "observed_level": str(a.observed_level) if a.observed_level is not None else None,
                    "confidence": a.confidence.value if a.confidence else None,
                    "status": a.status,
                    "meets_target": a.meets_target,
                }
                for a in s.assets
            ],
        },
        "findings": [f.to_dict() for f in result.findings],
        "actions": [a.to_dict() for a in developer_actions(result)],
    }
    return json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)


# OWASP MASWE weakness each rule checks, so results line up with MASVS-RESILIENCE reviews.
MASWE = {
    "RS-CORE-001": "MASWE-0004",  # sensitive data hardcoded in the app package
    "RS-CORE-002": "MASWE-0061",  # debug artifacts not removed
    "RS-WEB-002": "MASWE-0061",
    "RS-UNREAL-001": "MASWE-0060",  # resource obfuscation not implemented
    **{rule: "MASWE-0059" for rule in (  # code obfuscation not implemented
        "RS-UNITY-001", "RS-UNITY-002", "RS-GODOT-001", "RS-GODOT-002", "RS-WEB-001",
        "RS-ANDROID-001", "RS-NATIVE-001", "RS-DOTNET-001", "RS-CORE-004", "RS-CORE-005")},
}
MASWE_URI = "https://mas.owasp.org/MASWE/"


def _sarif_rule(f) -> dict:
    rule = {"id": f.rule_id, "shortDescription": {"text": f.title}}
    if f.rule_id in MASWE:
        rule["properties"] = {"tags": ["security", MASWE[f.rule_id]]}
        rule["helpUri"] = MASWE_URI
    return rule


def to_sarif(result: ScanResult) -> str:
    """Render result as SARIF 2.1.0, keeping the existing result and property mapping."""
    rules = {}
    results = []
    for f in result.findings:
        rules.setdefault(f.rule_id, _sarif_rule(f))
        region = {"startLine": f.location.line} if f.location.line else None
        physical = {"artifactLocation": {"uri": f.location.path}}
        if region:
            physical["region"] = region
        results.append(
            {
                "ruleId": f.rule_id,
                "kind": _SARIF_KIND[f.kind],
                # SARIF 3.27.10: only "fail" results carry a problem level.
                "level": _SARIF_LEVEL[f.severity] if f.kind is Kind.FAIL else "none",
                "message": {"text": f"{f.title}. {f.impact}"},
                "locations": [{"physicalLocation": physical}],
                "properties": {
                    "reshield.kind": f.kind.value,
                    "reshield.level": str(f.level) if f.level is not None else None,
                    "reshield.confidence": f.confidence.value,
                    "reshield.platform": f.platform,
                    "reshield.asset": f.asset_id,
                },
            }
        )

    errors = [f for f in result.findings if f.kind is Kind.ERROR]
    invocation = {
        "executionSuccessful": not errors,
        "toolExecutionNotifications": [
            {
                "level": "error",
                "message": {"text": f"{f.title}. {f.impact}"},
                "associatedRule": {"id": f.rule_id},
            }
            for f in errors
        ],
    }

    sarif = {
        "$schema": "https://json.schemastore.org/sarif-2.1.0.json",
        "version": "2.1.0",
        "runs": [
            {
                "tool": {
                    "driver": {
                        "name": "ReShield",
                        "version": __version__,
                        "informationUri": INFORMATION_URI,
                        "rules": list(rules.values()),
                    }
                },
                "invocations": [invocation],
                "results": results,
                "properties": {
                    "reshield.complete": result.summary.complete,
                    "reshield.notCheckedLevels": [str(lv) for lv in result.summary.not_checked_levels],
                },
            }
        ],
    }
    return json.dumps(sarif, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)


def to_markdown(result: ScanResult) -> str:
    """Render the technical Markdown report without changing scan or scoring data.

    Takes the scan result with its asset summary.
    Returns the report with sections in the order defined in protocol/05-report-format.md.
    """
    s = result.summary
    decisions = {id(d.finding): d for d in (result.severity_decisions or [])}
    policy_applied = result.severity_decisions is not None and all(
        id(f) in decisions and decisions[id(f)].matches(f, result.manifest)
        for f in result.findings
    )
    severity_policy = (SEVERITY_POLICY + " (contextual exposure consequences)" if policy_applied
                       else "not applied by this result; supplied severities are retained")
    platforms = ", ".join(a.display_name for a in result.platforms)
    lines = [
        "# ReShield Report",
        "",
        f"- **Target:** `{result.target}`",
        f"- **Manifest:** {f'`{result.manifest.source}`' if result.manifest.source else 'none'}",
        f"- **Platform:** {platforms}",
        f"- **Assessment:** {_assessment(s)}",
        f"- **Overall level:** {_overall(s)}",
        f"- **Score:** {_score(s)}",
        f"- **Score policy:** {SCORE_POLICY} (value-weighted asset target attainment)",
        f"- **Severity policy:** {severity_policy}",
    ]
    if s.not_checked_levels:
        lines.append(
            "- **Not checked:** " + ", ".join(str(lv) for lv in s.not_checked_levels)
            + " (the adapter does not evaluate these levels yet)"
        )
    lines.append("")
    lines += [
        "The score measures attainment of the declared asset targets, not absolute "
        "reverse-engineering resistance. 100% can reflect a deliberately low target. "
        "Compare scores only with the same asset scope, values, targets and policy.",
        "",
    ]
    # Keep the full declared denominator when scoring is withheld, so unknown assets never drop out silently.
    if result.manifest.assets:
        weights = {a.id: ASSET_WEIGHTS[a.value] for a in result.manifest.assets}
        total = sum(weights.values())
        if s.score is not None:
            attained = sum(weights[a.asset_id] for a in s.assets if a.meets_target is True)
            lines += [f"- **Attained asset weight:** {attained}/{total}", ""]
        else:
            lines += [
                f"- **Attained asset weight:** {_score(s)}; total declared weight: {total}",
                "",
            ]
    critical_ids = {a.id for a in result.manifest.assets if a.value is AssetValue.CRITICAL}
    critical_gaps = [a.asset_id for a in s.assets if a.asset_id in critical_ids and a.meets_target is False]
    if critical_gaps:
        lines += [
            "- **Critical assets below target:** " + ", ".join(critical_gaps),
            "- An aggregate percentage does not clear these gaps or authorize release.",
            "",
        ]

    fails = [f for f in result.findings if f.kind is Kind.FAIL]
    undetermined = [f for f in result.findings if f.kind in UNDETERMINED_KINDS]
    passes = [f for f in result.findings if f.kind is Kind.PASS]
    not_applicable = [f for f in result.findings if f.kind is Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE]

    if s.declaration_only:
        lines += [
            "The available levels in the scoring scope are developer declarations, "
            "not independently verified protection. No numeric score is assigned. "
            "Any target comparison based on these declarations is not a verified pass.",
            "",
        ]

    declared_ids = {asset.id for asset in result.manifest.assets}
    if declared_ids:
        lines += [
            "- **Overall level basis:** declared assets with available levels; "
            "findings not associated with a declared asset are excluded.",
            "",
        ]
    else:
        lines += [
            "- **Overall level basis:** all findings; no assets are declared. "
            "Asset target compliance is not assessed.",
            "",
        ]

    # Association checks apply to every result kind; diagnostics without a level are never dropped.
    lines += _coverage_limits(result)

    if not result.findings:
        lines += [
            "No results. The adapter reported nothing, so this scan establishes "
            "nothing about protection.",
            "",
        ]

    if s.assets:
        lines += ["## Assets", "", "| Asset | Target | Observed | Meets target |", "|---|---|---|---|"]
        for a in s.assets:
            if a.observed_level is not None:
                observed = f"{a.observed_level} ({a.confidence.value})"
            else:
                observed = "undetermined" if a.status == "undetermined" else "n/a"
            meets = {True: "yes", False: "**no**", None: "n/a"}[a.meets_target]
            if a.confidence in (Confidence.DECLARED, Confidence.INFERRED) and a.meets_target is not None:
                meets += f" ({a.confidence.value}; unverified)"
            lines.append(f"| {a.asset_id} | {a.target_level} | {observed} | {meets} |")
        lines.append("")

    if fails:
        lines += ["## Findings", ""]
        order = list(Severity)
        for f in sorted(fails, key=lambda f: (order.index(f.severity), f.level)):
            where = _location(f.location)
            lines += [
                f"### [{f.severity.value.upper()}] {f.title}",
                "",
                f"- **Rule:** {f.rule_id}",
                f"- **Location:** {where}",
                f"- **Current level:** {f.level} ({f.confidence.value})",
                f"- **Impact:** {f.impact}",
            ]
            decision = decisions.get(id(f))
            if policy_applied and decision is not None:
                lines.append(f"- **Severity basis:** {decision.basis}")
            else:
                lines.append("- **Severity basis:** supplied result; contextual normalization is not recorded.")
            if f.asset_id in declared_ids:
                lines.append(f"- **Asset:** {f.asset_id}")
            elif f.asset_id is not None:
                lines.append(f"- **Asset:** {f.asset_id!r} (undeclared asset ID)")
            else:
                lines.append("- **Asset:** no asset association")
            for e in f.evidence:
                lines.append(f"- **Evidence:** {e.description}")
            # An empty field means the tool provided nothing; the report must not invent evidence or fixes.
            if not f.evidence:
                lines.append("- **Evidence:** not provided")
            if not f.remediation:
                lines.append("- **Fix:** not provided")
            for i, r in enumerate(f.remediation, 1):
                lines.append(
                    f"- **Fix {i}:** {r.summary} (→ {r.target_level}, cost: {r.performance_cost})"
                )
                lines += [f"  - {step}" for step in r.steps]
            lines.append("")

    if undetermined:
        lines += [
            "## Not assessed",
            "",
            "These rules could not be decided. Missing evidence is never counted as protection.",
            "",
        ]
        for f in undetermined:
            label = "could not complete" if f.kind is Kind.ERROR else "not enough information"
            # An empty string is an invalid asset id, not the whole-build None.
            scope = "whole build" if f.asset_id is None else f.asset_id
            if f.asset_id is not None and f.asset_id not in declared_ids:
                scope = f"{f.asset_id!r} (undeclared asset ID)"
            lines += [
                f"### {f.rule_id}: {f.title} ({label})",
                "",
                f"- **Location:** {_location(f.location)}",
                f"- **Reason:** {f.impact}",
                f"- **Scope:** {scope}",
            ]
            lines += [f"- **Evidence:** {e.description}" for e in f.evidence]
            lines.append("")

    if passes:
        lines += ["## Confirmed protection", ""]
        for f in passes:
            lines.append(f"- **{f.rule_id}** {f.title}: {f.level} ({f.confidence.value}), {_location(f.location)}")
        lines.append("")

    if not_applicable:
        rules = ", ".join(sorted({f.rule_id for f in not_applicable}))
        lines += [f"Rules not applicable to this build: {rules}", ""]

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _location(location: Location) -> str:
    """Format a location with all available detail, so no section loses evidence.

    Takes a build-relative path with an optional line and symbol.
    Returns a readable string; missing lines and symbols are never guessed.
    """
    where = location.path
    if location.line is not None:
        where += f":{location.line}"
    if location.symbol:
        where += f" → `{location.symbol}`"
    return where


def _coverage_limits(result: ScanResult) -> list[str]:
    """Lines for the association and coverage section; empty when there are no gaps."""
    declared_ids = {asset.id for asset in result.manifest.assets}
    # Without declared assets there is nothing to match. A notapplicable result
    # belongs to no asset by definition, so it is not an association gap; a
    # reference to an undeclared id is still shown for every kind.
    unassociated = [
        f for f in result.findings if f.asset_id is None and f.kind is not Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE
    ] if declared_ids else []
    invalid_references = [
        f for f in result.findings
        if f.asset_id is not None and f.asset_id not in declared_ids
    ]
    unlinked = unassociated + invalid_references
    unknown = [a.asset_id for a in result.summary.assets if a.status == "no_results"]
    undetermined = [a.asset_id for a in result.summary.assets if a.status == "undetermined"]
    if not (unlinked or unknown or undetermined):
        return []
    lines = ["## Asset association and coverage limits", ""]
    if unlinked:
        lines.append(f"- **Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** {len(unlinked)}")
        # open/error/notapplicable carry no level and never enter the lowest-level calculation.
        leveled = [f for f in unlinked if f.level is not None]
        if leveled:
            lowest = min(f.level for f in leveled)
            confidences = sorted({f.confidence.value for f in leveled if f.level == lowest})
            lines.append(
                f"- **Lowest reported level among these findings:** {lowest} ({', '.join(confidences)})"
            )
        lines.append(
            "- These results do not supply a declared asset level or attainment weight. "
            "Whole-build open/error results still make the assessment incomplete and all assets undetermined."
        )
        if unassociated:
            lines += [
                f"- **Findings without an asset association:** {len(unassociated)}",
                "- Review the manifest's `paths` and `symbols` to determine whether association "
                "is missing or the code is outside the intended asset scope. "
                "An unassociated open/error result has whole-build scope.",
            ]
        if invalid_references:
            lines += [
                f"- **Findings referencing undeclared asset IDs:** {len(invalid_references)}",
                "- Investigate the inconsistent asset references shown below; "
                "do not assume they belong to a declared asset.",
            ]
        for f in unlinked:
            asset = "no asset association" if f.asset_id is None else f"{f.asset_id!r} (undeclared asset ID)"
            lines.append(f"- **{f.rule_id}** ({f.kind.value}), asset: {asset}; {_location(f.location)}")
    if unknown:
        lines += [
            f"- **Declared assets with unknown levels:** {len(unknown)} ({', '.join(unknown)})",
            "- These declared assets have no available level and are not covered by the overall level. "
            "Their protection remains unknown.",
        ]
    if undetermined:
        lines += [
            f"- **Declared assets with undetermined levels:** {len(undetermined)} ({', '.join(undetermined)})",
            "- An asset-specific or whole-build open/error result prevents a level determination. "
            "Unknown protection is not a failed target comparison.",
        ]
    return lines + [""]


def _assessment(s) -> str:
    if s.complete:
        if s.declaration_only:
            return "complete (no open/error results); scoring basis is declared only and unverified"
        return "complete"
    return "**incomplete**: some rules could not be decided (see Not assessed)"


def _overall(s) -> str:
    if s.overall_level is None:
        return "n/a"
    if not s.complete:
        return f"at most {s.overall_level} (assessment incomplete)"
    if s.declaration_only:
        return f"{s.overall_level} (declared only; unverified)"
    return str(s.overall_level)


def _score(s) -> str:
    if s.score is not None:
        return f"{s.score}% of declared asset target weight"
    if s.score_reason:
        return f"withheld ({s.score_reason})"
    return "n/a"


WRITERS = {"markdown": to_markdown, "sarif": to_sarif, "json": to_json, "summary": to_summary}

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/scanner.py`:

```py
"""Scan orchestration: pick adapters, collect findings, summarize."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path

from . import archive, core_checks
from .adapters import ADAPTERS, Adapter, ScanContext, detect_adapters, get_adapter
from .manifest import Manifest, find_manifest, load_manifest
from .models import Evidence, Finding, Kind, Location, Severity
from .scoring import Summary, summarize
from .severity import SeverityDecision, normalize_severities


@dataclass
class ScanResult:
    target: Path
    platforms: list[Adapter]
    manifest: Manifest
    findings: list[Finding]
    summary: Summary
    severity_decisions: list[SeverityDecision] | None = None  # internal; not exported


class NoPlatformDetected(Exception):
    pass


def _source_project(target: Path) -> str | None:
    """Return a plain explanation when target is an engine project rather than a build players get.

    Projects hold editor caches and debug files that never ship, which would produce wrong advice.
    """
    if not target.is_dir():
        return None
    after = "Your AI assistant can also review the project settings without a build."
    if (target / "ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt").is_file() and (target / "Assets").is_dir():
        return ("This is a Unity project, not a build. Build the game first (File > Build Settings > Build), "
                f"then scan the folder it creates. {after}")
    if (target / "project.godot").is_file():
        return ("This is a Godot project, not a build. Export the game first (Project > Export), "
                f"then scan the exported .pck file or folder. {after}")
    if any(target.glob("*.uproject")):
        return ("This is an Unreal Engine project, not a build. Package the game first (Platforms > Package Project), "
                f"then scan the folder it creates. {after}")
    return None


def run_scan(
    target: Path,
    manifest_path: Path | None = None,
    platform: str | None = None,
) -> ScanResult:
    """Scan target with an optional manifest path and platform; returns ScanResult.

    Inputs and the adapter boundary follow the frozen contract; evidence mapping awaits the v0.2 proposal.
    """
    target = target.resolve()

    manifest_path = manifest_path or find_manifest(target)
    manifest = load_manifest(manifest_path) if manifest_path else Manifest()

    if archive.is_archive(target):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="reshield-") as tmp:
            return _scan_archive(target, Path(tmp), manifest, platform)

    if not platform and (project := _source_project(target)):
        raise NoPlatformDetected(project)

    adapters = [get_adapter(platform)] if platform else detect_adapters(target)
    # Cross-platform checks (credentials, debug files, unchecked runtimes) run on every build.
    core, stopped = core_checks.scan(target, manifest)
    if not adapters and not any(f.rule_id == "RS-CORE-004" for f in core):
        if target.is_file() and target.suffix.casefold() in archive.ARCHIVE_SUFFIXES:
            raise NoPlatformDetected(f"{target.name} could not be opened. The file may be damaged or "
                                     "incomplete; download or export it again.")
        raise NoPlatformDetected(
            f"No game or app build was found in {target}. Point ReShield at the folder players get "
            "(for example the one with your game's .exe, index.html or .pck file), or at an .apk, .ipa "
            "or .zip file. If you know the platform, set it with --platform."
        )

    context = ScanContext(target=target, manifest=manifest)
    findings = [f for adapter in adapters for f in adapter.scan(context)]
    findings += core + ([core_checks.incomplete_result(stopped)] if stopped else [])
    return _result(target, adapters, manifest, findings)


def _result(target: Path, adapters: list[Adapter], manifest: Manifest, findings: list[Finding]) -> ScanResult:
    findings, decisions = normalize_severities(findings, manifest)
    return ScanResult(
        target=target,
        platforms=adapters,
        manifest=manifest,
        findings=findings,
        severity_decisions=decisions,
        summary=summarize(
            findings,
            manifest,
            checked_levels=frozenset().union(*(a.checked_levels for a in adapters)),
        ),
    )


def _inside(finding: Finding, prefix: str) -> Finding:
    """Point a finding at the entry inside the package, for example game.apk!/lib/arm64-v8a/libil2cpp.so."""
    path = finding.location.path
    location = replace(finding.location, path=prefix if path in ("", ".") else f"{prefix}/{path}")
    evidence = [replace(e, artifact=f"{prefix}/{e.artifact}") if e.artifact else e for e in finding.evidence]
    return replace(finding, location=location, evidence=evidence)


def _scan_archive(target: Path, folder: Path, manifest: Manifest, platform: str | None) -> ScanResult:
    """Scan a packaged build. Packages often combine technologies, so every matching adapter runs."""
    prefix = f"{target.name}!"
    try:
        skipped = archive.extract(target, folder)
    except (archive.ArchiveError, OSError) as error:
        skipped, folder = [f"could not open the package: {error}"], None
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    adapters: list[Adapter] = []
    if folder is not None:
        adapters = [get_adapter(platform)] if platform else [a for a in ADAPTERS if a.detect(folder)]
        context = ScanContext(target=folder, manifest=manifest)
        for adapter in adapters:
            findings += adapter.scan(context)
        core, stopped = core_checks.scan(folder, manifest)
        findings += core + ([core_checks.incomplete_result(stopped)] if stopped else [])
    findings = [_inside(f, prefix) for f in findings]
    if skipped or not adapters:
        reason = "; ".join(skipped[:5]) if skipped else "no supported code was found inside the package"
        findings.append(Finding(
            "RS-CORE-003", "Package contents", "core", Severity.INFO, None, Location(prefix),
            f"Part of the package was not checked: {reason}. Missing evidence does not mean protection.",
            kind=Kind.OPEN, evidence=[Evidence(f"{len(skipped)} entries skipped", prefix)],
        ))
    return _result(target, adapters, manifest, findings)

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/scoring.py`:

```py
"""Turn findings into a summary and score.

Spec: protocol/02-protection-levels.md
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from .manifest import Asset, Manifest
from .models import UNDETERMINED_KINDS, AssetValue, Confidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Severity

# Levels an adapter can evaluate in a build. L5 comes from the manifest.
BUILD_LEVELS = (Level.L0, Level.L1, Level.L2, Level.L3, Level.L4)

SCORE_POLICY = "asset-target-v1"
ASSET_WEIGHTS = {
    AssetValue.CRITICAL: 4,
    AssetValue.HIGH: 3,
    AssetValue.MEDIUM: 2,
    AssetValue.LOW: 1,
}


@dataclass
class AssetResult:
    """Per-asset summary. A level or confidence of None means it is not determined."""

    asset_id: str
    target_level: Level
    observed_level: Level | None  # None: undetermined, or no results linked to this asset
    confidence: Confidence | None = None  # how observed_level was established
    # "determined", "undetermined" (an open/error result applies), or "no_results"
    status: str = "no_results"

    @property
    def meets_target(self) -> bool | None:
        if self.observed_level is None:
            return None
        return self.observed_level >= self.target_level


@dataclass
class Summary:
    overall_level: Level | None  # an upper bound when the assessment is incomplete
    score: int | None  # observed-only, value-weighted target attainment percentage
    severity_counts: dict[str, int]  # "fail" results only
    assets: list[AssetResult] = field(default_factory=list)
    complete: bool = True  # False when any result is "open" or "error"
    kind_counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    not_checked_levels: list[Level] = field(default_factory=list)
    declaration_only: bool = False  # internal scoring basis, not scan completeness
    score_reason: str | None = None  # internal withholding reason; not a new JSON field


_CONFIDENCE_ORDER = [Confidence.OBSERVED, Confidence.INFERRED, Confidence.DECLARED]


def _weakest(results: list[Finding]) -> tuple[Level, Confidence]:
    level = min(f.level for f in results)
    # Among results at the weakest level, report the strongest evidence.
    confidence = min(
        (f.confidence for f in results if f.level == level),
        key=_CONFIDENCE_ORDER.index,
    )
    return level, confidence


def _asset_result(asset: Asset, findings: list[Finding], build_undetermined: bool) -> AssetResult:
    """Return the AssetResult for one asset. Observations take precedence over declarations."""
    linked = [f for f in findings if f.asset_id == asset.id]
    # Fail-safe default: missing evidence never counts as protection, so an
    # open/error result for this asset or for the whole build leaves it undetermined.
    if build_undetermined or any(f.kind in UNDETERMINED_KINDS for f in linked):
        return AssetResult(asset.id, asset.target_level, None, None, "undetermined")
    for kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS):
        # Use linked results first; fall back to the server_side declaration only when nothing matched.
        results = [f for f in linked if f.kind is kind]
        if results:
            return AssetResult(asset.id, asset.target_level, *_weakest(results), "determined")
    if asset.server_side:
        # Declared server-side and not found in a completely assessed build.
        return AssetResult(asset.id, asset.target_level, Level.L5, Confidence.DECLARED, "determined")
    return AssetResult(asset.id, asset.target_level, None, None, "no_results")


def _target_score(
    assets: list[AssetResult], manifest: Manifest, complete: bool,
) -> tuple[int | None, str | None]:
    """Compute the asset-target-v1 score; return the reason instead of a partial score when evidence is insufficient."""
    if not complete:
        return None, "assessment incomplete"
    if not manifest.assets:
        return None, "no declared assets"
    if any(a.observed_level is None for a in assets):
        return None, "some declared asset levels are unknown"
    if all(a.confidence is Confidence.DECLARED for a in assets):
        return None, "declared only; unverified"
    if any(a.confidence is not Confidence.OBSERVED for a in assets):
        return None, "some asset levels are inferred or declared; unverified"

    weights = {a.id: ASSET_WEIGHTS[a.value] for a in manifest.assets}
    total = sum(weights[a.asset_id] for a in assets)
    attained = sum(weights[a.asset_id] for a in assets if a.meets_target is True)
    return round(100 * attained / total), None


def summarize(
    findings: list[Finding],
    manifest: Manifest,
    checked_levels: frozenset[Level] = frozenset(BUILD_LEVELS),
) -> Summary:
    """Summarize findings for the manifest and the levels the adapters checked; returns Summary.

    Completeness and weakest-level rules follow the frozen contract; nothing may bypass open/error results.
    """
    fails = [f for f in findings if f.kind is Kind.FAIL]
    counts = Counter(f.severity.value for f in fails)
    severity_counts = {s.value: counts.get(s.value, 0) for s in Severity}
    kinds = Counter(f.kind.value for f in findings)
    kind_counts = {k.value: kinds.get(k.value, 0) for k in Kind}

    complete = not any(f.kind in UNDETERMINED_KINDS for f in findings)
    build_undetermined = any(f.kind in UNDETERMINED_KINDS and f.asset_id is None for f in findings)
    assets = [_asset_result(a, findings, build_undetermined) for a in manifest.assets]

    # Spec 02: the weakest declared asset sets the overall level.
    # Without declared assets, fall back to the weakest fail/pass result.
    if manifest.assets:
        overall = min((a.observed_level for a in assets if a.observed_level is not None), default=None)
    else:
        leveled = [f.level for f in findings if f.kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS)]
        overall = min(leveled, default=None)

    # Only consider evidence in the same scope as the overall-level calculation.
    # Unlinked observations cannot verify declared assets. Unknown assets do not
    # supply evidence; completeness remains governed by the result-kind contract.
    if manifest.assets:
        confidences = [a.confidence for a in assets if a.observed_level is not None]
    else:
        confidences = [f.confidence for f in findings if f.kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS)]
    declaration_only = bool(confidences) and all(c is Confidence.DECLARED for c in confidences)

    score, score_reason = _target_score(assets, manifest, complete)

    return Summary(
        overall_level=overall,
        score=score,
        score_reason=score_reason,
        severity_counts=severity_counts,
        assets=assets,
        complete=complete,
        declaration_only=declaration_only,
        kind_counts=kind_counts,
        not_checked_levels=[lv for lv in BUILD_LEVELS if lv not in checked_levels],
    )

```

`skills/reshield/scripts/reshield/severity.py`:

```py
"""Shared contextual severity for registered implementation-exposure rules."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from enum import Enum
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Mapping

from .manifest import Manifest
from .models import AssetValue, Finding, Kind, Severity

SEVERITY_POLICY = "severity-v1"


class ExposureClass(str, Enum):
    SOURCE = "source-like-implementation"
    NAMES = "readable-names-and-structure"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RulePolicy:
    platform: str
    exposure: ExposureClass
    baseline: Severity


RULE_POLICIES: Mapping[str, RulePolicy] = MappingProxyType({
    "RS-UNITY-001": RulePolicy("unity", ExposureClass.SOURCE, Severity.HIGH),
    "RS-UNITY-002": RulePolicy("unity", ExposureClass.NAMES, Severity.MEDIUM),
})

_MATRIX: Mapping[ExposureClass, Mapping[AssetValue, Severity]] = MappingProxyType({
    ExposureClass.SOURCE: MappingProxyType({
        AssetValue.LOW: Severity.LOW,
        AssetValue.MEDIUM: Severity.MEDIUM,
        AssetValue.HIGH: Severity.HIGH,
        AssetValue.CRITICAL: Severity.CRITICAL,
    }),
    ExposureClass.NAMES: MappingProxyType({
        AssetValue.LOW: Severity.LOW,
        AssetValue.MEDIUM: Severity.LOW,
        AssetValue.HIGH: Severity.MEDIUM,
        AssetValue.CRITICAL: Severity.HIGH,
    }),
})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeverityDecision:
    """Internal rationale, tied to the exact normalized finding, never serialized."""

    finding: Finding
    severity: Severity
    basis: str
    inputs: tuple

    def matches(self, finding: Finding, manifest: Manifest) -> bool:
        """Reject stale rationale after callers change decision inputs."""
        return (self.finding is finding and self.severity is finding.severity
                and self.inputs == _decision_inputs(finding, manifest))


def _decision_inputs(finding: Finding, manifest: Manifest) -> tuple:
    asset = manifest.asset(finding.asset_id) if finding.asset_id is not None else None
    value = None if asset is None else (type(asset.value), repr(asset.value))
    return (finding.kind, finding.rule_id, finding.platform, finding.asset_id, value)


def _select(
    finding: Finding, manifest: Manifest, registry: Mapping[str, RulePolicy],
) -> tuple[Severity, str]:
    if finding.kind is not Kind.FAIL:
        return Severity.INFO, "Non-fail result; no exposure severity escalation."
    policy = registry.get(finding.rule_id)
    if policy is None:
        return finding.severity, "Adapter severity retained: no registered contextual policy for this rule."
    if finding.platform != policy.platform:
        return finding.severity, "Adapter severity retained: rule/platform mismatch; investigate result consistency."
    if finding.asset_id is None:
        return policy.baseline, (
            f"{policy.exposure.value}; rule baseline used because no asset value is associated."
        )
    asset = manifest.asset(finding.asset_id)
    if asset is None:
        return finding.severity, "Adapter severity retained: asset ID is not declared; investigate association consistency."
    if not isinstance(asset.value, AssetValue):
        return finding.severity, "Adapter severity retained: invalid internal asset value; investigate declaration consistency."
    return _MATRIX[policy.exposure][asset.value], (
        f"{policy.exposure.value}; declared asset value {asset.value.value}. "
        "Contextual consequence, not proof of exploitation; confidence is reported separately."
    )


def normalize_severities(
    findings: list[Finding], manifest: Manifest,
    *, registry: Mapping[str, RulePolicy] | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[Finding], list[SeverityDecision]]:
    """Return copied findings and matching rationale without changing adapter inputs.

    A registry override supports isolated policy tests; production uses only
    registered rule IDs. No platform is selected by host OS or rule-ID prefix.
    """
    policies = RULE_POLICIES if registry is None else registry
    normalized = []
    decisions = []
    for finding in findings:
        severity, basis = _select(finding, manifest, policies)
        updated = replace(finding, severity=severity)
        normalized.append(updated)
        decisions.append(SeverityDecision(updated, severity, basis,
                                          _decision_inputs(updated, manifest)))
    return normalized, decisions

```

```py
"""Entry point used by the skill. Works without installing the package."""

import sys

if sys.version_info < (3, 11):
    # Plain message instead of an import error on older Pythons (for example macOS's built-in 3.9).
    sys.stderr.write("ReShield needs Python 3.11 or later, but this is Python %d.%d. "
                     "Install a newer Python from https://www.python.org/downloads/ "
                     "or ask your AI assistant to set it up.\n" % sys.version_info[:2])
    sys.exit(2)

from pathlib import Path  # noqa: E402

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from reshield.cli import main  # noqa: E402

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```
