```py
"""Packaged builds, Android DEX, Unreal containers, Unity mobile layouts and C++ RTTI names."""

import contextlib
import json
import struct
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from reshield import archive  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.android import AndroidAdapter, dex_classes  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.base import ScanContext  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.native import NativeAdapter, _magic  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.unity_layout import has_player, inspect_player  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.unreal import UnrealAdapter  # noqa: E402
from reshield.cli import main  # noqa: E402
from reshield.manifest import Manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import Kind, Level  # noqa: E402
from test_native_adapter import elf  # noqa: E402
from test_unity_adapter import _Output, make_player, metadata_image, native_image, pe_image  # noqa: E402


def dex(descriptors):
    """Minimal DEX file defining the given classes."""
    strings = b""
    offsets = []
    base = 0x70 + len(descriptors) * (4 + 4 + 32)
    for d in descriptors:
        offsets.append(base + len(strings))
        strings += bytes([len(d)]) + d.encode() + b"\0"
    n = len(descriptors)
    header = bytearray(0x70)
    header[:8] = b"dex\n035\0"
    string_ids_off, type_ids_off, class_defs_off = 0x70, 0x70 + 4 * n, 0x70 + 8 * n
    struct.pack_into("<II", header, 0x38, n, string_ids_off)
    struct.pack_into("<II", header, 0x40, n, type_ids_off)
    struct.pack_into("<II", header, 0x60, n, class_defs_off)
    body = b"".join(struct.pack("<I", o) for o in offsets) + b"".join(struct.pack("<I", i) for i in range(n))
    body += b"".join(struct.pack("<I", i) + bytes(28) for i in range(n))
    return bytes(header) + body + strings


def pak(encrypted, version=11):
    footer = bytes(16) + bytes([1 if encrypted else 0]) + struct.pack("<II", 0x5A6F12E1, version) + bytes(36)
    return b"\x00" * 100 + footer


def utoc(encrypted):
    header = bytearray(144)
    header[:16] = b"-==--==--==--==-"
    header[16] = 3
    header[80] = 0b1001 | (0b10 if encrypted else 0)
    return bytes(header)


class ArchiveTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def zip(self, name, entries):
        path = self.root / name
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
            for entry, data in entries.items():
                z.writestr(entry, data)
        return path

    def run_cli(self, target):
        out = _Output()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(_Output()):
            self.assertEqual(main(["scan", str(target), "--format", "json"]), 0)
        return json.loads(out.getvalue())

    def test_unsafe_entries_are_skipped(self):
        path = self.zip("evil.zip", {"../escape.txt": b"x", "ok.txt": b"y"})
        out = self.root / "out"
        out.mkdir()
        skipped = archive.extract(path, out)
        self.assertTrue(any("escape" in s for s in skipped))
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "escape.txt").exists())
        self.assertTrue((out / "ok.txt").exists())

    def test_apk_runs_every_matching_adapter_and_points_inside(self):
        apk = self.zip("game.apk", {
            "classes.dex": dex(["Lcom/mygame/CombatSystem;", "Lcom/mygame/ShopManager;"]),
            "lib/arm64-v8a/libgame.so": elf([".text", ".symtab"]),
            "assets/config.json": b'{"key": "AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE"}',
        })
        report = self.run_cli(apk)
        self.assertEqual(set(report["platforms"]), {"android", "native"})
        paths = {f["rule_id"]: f["location"]["path"] for f in report["findings"]}
        self.assertEqual(paths["RS-ANDROID-001"], "game.apk!/classes.dex")
        self.assertEqual(paths["RS-NATIVE-001"], "game.apk!/lib/arm64-v8a/libgame.so")
        self.assertEqual(paths["RS-CORE-001"], "game.apk!/assets/config.json")

    def test_package_without_code_is_open(self):
        report = self.run_cli(self.zip("art.zip", {"readme.txt": b"hello"}))
        self.assertFalse(report["summary"]["complete"])
        self.assertIn("RS-CORE-003", {f["rule_id"] for f in report["findings"]})

    def test_original_package_is_not_modified(self):
        apk = self.zip("game.apk", {"classes.dex": dex(["Lcom/mygame/Combat;"])})
        before = (apk.read_bytes(), apk.stat().st_mtime_ns)
        self.run_cli(apk)
        self.assertEqual(before, (apk.read_bytes(), apk.stat().st_mtime_ns))


class AndroidTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def scan(self, classes):
        (self.root / "classes.dex").write_bytes(dex(classes))
        return AndroidAdapter().scan(ScanContext(self.root, Manifest()))

    def test_dex_parser(self):
        self.assertEqual(dex_classes(dex(["La/b/C;"])), ["La/b/C;"])

    def test_readable_names_are_l0(self):
        [f] = self.scan(["Lcom/mygame/CombatSystem;", "Lcom/mygame/Shop;", "Landroidx/core/App;"])
        self.assertEqual((f.kind, f.level), (Kind.FAIL, Level.L0))

    def test_r8_renamed_code_passes_l2(self):
        [f] = self.scan(["Lcom/mygame/a;", "Lcom/mygame/b;", "Lcom/mygame/c;", "Lcom/mygame/MainActivity;"])
        self.assertEqual((f.kind, f.level), (Kind.PASS, Level.L2))

    def test_library_and_generated_classes_only_is_not_applicable(self):
        # A Unity APK's DEX holds only Unity glue and resource classes (seen in a real build).
        [f] = self.scan(["Lcom/unity3d/player/UnityPlayerActivity;", "Lcom/mygame/R$mipmap;", "Lcom/mygame/BuildConfig;"])
        self.assertEqual(f.kind, Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE)


class UnrealTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def scan(self):
        return {f.location.path: (f.kind, f.level) for f in UnrealAdapter().scan(ScanContext(self.root, Manifest()))}

    def test_pak_and_utoc_encryption(self):
        paks = self.root / "Game/Content/Paks"
        paks.mkdir(parents=True)
        (paks / "open.pak").write_bytes(pak(False))
        (paks / "locked.pak").write_bytes(pak(True))
        (paks / "open.utoc").write_bytes(utoc(False))
        (paks / "locked.utoc").write_bytes(utoc(True))
        result = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual(result["Game/Content/Paks/open.pak"], (Kind.FAIL, Level.L1))
        self.assertEqual(result["Game/Content/Paks/locked.pak"], (Kind.PASS, Level.L2))
        self.assertEqual(result["Game/Content/Paks/open.utoc"], (Kind.FAIL, Level.L1))
        self.assertEqual(result["Game/Content/Paks/locked.utoc"], (Kind.PASS, Level.L2))

    def test_unsupported_unreal_pak_is_open(self):
        (self.root / "future.pak").write_bytes(b"\x00" * 64 + struct.pack("<II", 0x5A6F12E1, 99) + b"\x00" * 32)
        self.assertEqual(self.scan()["future.pak"], (Kind.OPEN, None))

    def test_chromium_pak_is_not_unreal(self):
        # Electron and Chromium ship locale and resource .pak files (seen in a real Electron app).
        (self.root / "locales").mkdir()
        (self.root / "locales/en-US.pak").write_bytes(b"\x05\x00\x00\x00" + b"\x01" * 200)
        (self.root / "chrome_100_percent.pak").write_bytes(b"\x05\x00\x00\x00" + b"\x02" * 200)
        self.assertFalse(UnrealAdapter().detect(self.root))
        self.assertEqual(self.scan(), {})


class UnityMobileTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def write(self, rel, data):
        path = self.root / rel
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_bytes(data)

    def test_android_il2cpp_layout(self):
        # Layout verified against a real Unity 6000.3.8f1 Android build.
        self.write("lib/arm64-v8a/libunity.so", native_image("linux"))
        self.write("lib/arm64-v8a/libil2cpp.so", native_image("linux"))
        self.write("assets/bin/Data/Managed/Metadata/global-metadata.dat", metadata_image())
        self.assertTrue(has_player(self.root))
        result = inspect_player(self.root)
        self.assertEqual((result.status, result.backend), ("identified", "il2cpp"))

    def test_android_mono_layout(self):
        self.write("lib/armeabi-v7a/libunity.so", native_image("linux"))
        self.write("lib/armeabi-v7a/libmonobdwgc-2.0.so", native_image("linux"))
        self.write("assets/bin/Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll", pe_image(True))
        result = inspect_player(self.root)
        self.assertEqual((result.status, result.backend), ("identified", "mono"))


class MagicAndRttiTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_java_class_file_is_not_macho(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            path = Path(tmp) / "DebugProbesKt.bin"
            path.write_bytes(b"\xca\xfe\xba\xbe\x00\x00\x00\x34" + bytes(64))
            self.assertIsNone(_magic(path))

    def test_gcc_rtti_names_are_detected(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            names = b"".join(b"\x00" + n + b"\x00" for n in
                             [b"10FileReader", b"6Player", b"N4Game5EnemyE", b"9Inventory", b"11ShopManager", b"St9exception"])
            (Path(tmp) / "game").write_bytes(elf([".text"]) + names)
            [f] = NativeAdapter().scan(ScanContext(Path(tmp), Manifest()))
            self.assertEqual(f.level, Level.L1)
            self.assertIn("5 C++ class names", f.evidence[0].description)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```py
import io
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from contextlib import redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))

from reshield.adapters import detect_adapters  # noqa: E402
from reshield.cli import main  # noqa: E402
from reshield.manifest import Manifest, load_manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import Confidence, Evidence, Finding, Level, Location, Remediation, Severity  # noqa: E402
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown, to_sarif  # noqa: E402
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult  # noqa: E402
from reshield.scoring import summarize  # noqa: E402

# make fake data for testing
def make_finding(level=Level.L0, asset_id="combat-formulas"):
    return Finding(
        rule_id="RS-TEST-001",
        title="Test finding",
        platform="test",
        severity=Severity.HIGH,
        level=level,
        location=Location(path="Game_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll", symbol="Combat"),
        impact="Logic can be copied.",
        evidence=[Evidence(description="Observed in test")],
        remediation=[Remediation(summary="Fix it", target_level=Level.L2, performance_cost="none")],
        asset_id=asset_id,
    )


class FindingValidationTests(unittest.TestCase):

    # UNITY-1 is not a valid rule ID, so this should raise a ValueError
    def test_rejects_bad_rule_id(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            Finding(
                rule_id="UNITY-1",
                title="x",
                platform="unity",
                severity=Severity.LOW,
                level=Level.L0,
                location=Location(path="a.dll"),
                impact="x",
            )

    # reject absolute paths for Location.path
    def test_rejects_absolute_paths(self):
        for path in ["/etc/game.dll", r"C:\Game\a.dll"]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                Location(path=path)

    # normalize path separators
    def test_normalizes_separators(self):
        self.assertEqual(Location(path=r"Game_Data\Managed\a.dll").path, "Game_Data/Managed/a.dll")

    # confidence defaults to OBSERVED and serializes to string
    def test_confidence_defaults_and_serializes(self):
        f = make_finding()
        self.assertEqual(f.confidence, Confidence.OBSERVED)
        self.assertEqual(f.to_dict()["confidence"], "observed")


class ManifestTests(unittest.TestCase):
    # test that the example manifest loads correctly and has the expected values
    def test_example_manifest_loads(self):
        m = load_manifest(ROOT / "examples" / "reshield.toml")
        self.assertEqual(m.project_name, "Example Game")
        self.assertEqual(m.asset("combat-formulas").target_level, Level.L3)


class DetectionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    # unity godot and web platforms should be detected based on the presence of certain files
    def test_detects_each_platform(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)

            unity = root / "unity"
            (unity / "Game_Data").mkdir(parents=True)
            (unity / "Game_Data" / "globalgamemanagers").touch()

            godot = root / "godot"
            godot.mkdir()
            (godot / "game.pck").touch()

            web = root / "web"
            web.mkdir()
            (web / "index.html").touch()

            empty = root / "empty"
            empty.mkdir()

            self.assertEqual([a.name for a in detect_adapters(unity)], ["unity"])
            self.assertEqual([a.name for a in detect_adapters(godot)], ["godot"])
            self.assertEqual([a.name for a in detect_adapters(web)], ["web"])
            self.assertEqual(detect_adapters(empty), [])


class ScoringTests(unittest.TestCase):
    # one L3 and one L1 finding should result in an overall level of L1, and the asset with the L3 finding should not meet its target level
    def test_overall_level_is_weakest(self):
        m = load_manifest(ROOT / "examples" / "reshield.toml")
        s = summarize([make_finding(Level.L3), make_finding(Level.L1)], m)
        self.assertEqual(s.overall_level, Level.L1)
        combat = next(a for a in s.assets if a.asset_id == "combat-formulas")
        self.assertFalse(combat.meets_target)

    # with declared assets, a finding outside every asset should not lower the overall level
    def test_unlinked_finding_ignored_when_assets_declared(self):
        m = load_manifest(ROOT / "examples" / "reshield.toml")
        s = summarize([make_finding(Level.L3), make_finding(Level.L0, asset_id=None)], m)
        self.assertEqual(s.overall_level, Level.L3)

    # without declared assets, the weakest finding sets the overall level
    def test_overall_falls_back_to_findings(self):
        s = summarize([make_finding(Level.L3), make_finding(Level.L0, asset_id=None)], Manifest())
        self.assertEqual(s.overall_level, Level.L0)

    # none of the findings should result in an overall level of None and a score of None
    def test_no_findings(self):
        s = summarize([], Manifest())
        self.assertIsNone(s.overall_level)
        self.assertIsNone(s.score)


class ReportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    #setup results for testing report generation
    def setUp(self):
        findings = [make_finding()]
        self.result = ScanResult(
            target=Path("."),
            platforms=[],
            manifest=Manifest(),
            findings=findings,
            summary=summarize(findings, Manifest()),
        )

    # Static Analysis Results Interchange Format
    def test_sarif_structure(self):
        sarif = json.loads(to_sarif(self.result))
        self.assertEqual(sarif["version"], "2.1.0")
        run = sarif["runs"][0]
        self.assertEqual(run["results"][0]["ruleId"], "RS-TEST-001")
        self.assertEqual(run["results"][0]["level"], "error")
        self.assertEqual(run["results"][0]["properties"]["reshield.confidence"], "observed")
        self.assertEqual(run["tool"]["driver"]["rules"][0]["id"], "RS-TEST-001")

    # markdown and json output should contain the expected values
    def test_json_and_markdown(self):
        data = json.loads(to_json(self.result))
        self.assertEqual(data["findings"][0]["level"], "L0")
        self.assertIn("Test finding", to_markdown(self.result))


# error codes returned by the CLI should be as expected
class CliTests(unittest.TestCase):

        with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
            self.assertEqual(main(["platforms"]), 0)
        self.assertIn("unity", out.getvalue())

    # undetected platform should return 2 exit code
```

`tests/test_core_checks.py`:

```py
"""Cross-platform checks: credentials and debug files shipped in any build."""

import contextlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from reshield import core_checks  # noqa: E402
from reshield.cli import main  # noqa: E402
from reshield.manifest import Manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import Kind, Level  # noqa: E402
from test_unity_adapter import _Output, make_player  # noqa: E402

AWS = b"AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE"  # AWS documentation example key
KEY_BLOCK = b"-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----\n" + b"MIIEvQIBADANBgkqhkiG9w0BAQEFAASC" * 3 + b"\n-----END PRIVATE KEY-----"


def scan(root):
    findings, stopped = core_checks.scan(root, Manifest())
    return findings, stopped


class SecretTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def write(self, name, data):
        path = self.root / name
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_bytes(data)
        return path

    def test_ascii_and_utf16_keys_are_found(self):
        self.write("a.dll", b"\x00junk" + AWS + b"junk")
        self.write("b.dll", b"\x01\x02" + AWS.decode().encode("utf-16-le") + b"\x00\x00")
        findings, _ = scan(self.root)
        self.assertEqual(sorted(f.location.path for f in findings), ["a.dll", "b.dll"])
        f = findings[0]
        self.assertEqual((f.rule_id, f.kind, f.level), ("RS-CORE-001", Kind.FAIL, Level.L0))

    def test_secret_is_never_shown_in_full(self):
        self.write("game.bin", AWS)
        finding = scan(self.root)[0][0]
        text = json.dumps(finding.to_dict())
        self.assertNotIn(AWS.decode(), text)
        self.assertIn("****", text)

    def test_more_providers(self):
        # Built from parts so the repository itself never contains a key-shaped string.
        keys = {
            "SendGrid API key": "SG." + "a" * 22 + "." + "b" * 43,
            "GitLab token": "glpat-" + "x1" * 10,
            "Discord webhook": "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/" + "1" * 18 + "/" + "k" * 64,
            "Telegram bot token": "123456789:AA" + "t" * 33,
            "Azure storage key": "AccountKey=" + "Q" * 86 + "==",
        }
        for i, key in enumerate(keys.values()):
            self.write(f"f{i}.dat", b"\x00" + key.encode() + b"\x00")
        found = {f.evidence[0].description.split(" found")[0] for f in scan(self.root)[0]}
        self.assertEqual(found, set(keys))

    def test_aws_ids_use_base32(self):
        # Seen in libglib and a bundled JS file: AWS-like prefixes followed by digits AWS never uses.
        self.write("libglib.dll", b"\x00ASIANPT154PTCPXXXXXX\x00AKIAAAB0AAAAAAAAAAAA\x00")
        self.assertEqual(scan(self.root)[0], [])

    def test_private_key_needs_key_data(self):
        # TLS libraries ship the header text alone; that is not a leaked key (seen in the Godot executable).
        self.write("tls.dll", b"\x00-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----\x00-----END PRIVATE KEY-----\x00")
        self.assertEqual(scan(self.root)[0], [])
        self.write("leak.txt", KEY_BLOCK)
        self.assertEqual([f.location.path for f in scan(self.root)[0]], ["leak.txt"])

    def test_utf16_strings_are_not_glued_together(self):
        # Windows locale tables ("sk-SK", "sq-AL", ...) once looked like an OpenAI key.
        table = b"".join(s.encode("utf-16-le") + b"\x00\x00" for s in
                         ["sk-SK", "sq-AL", "sv-SE", "th-TH", "tr-TR", "ur-PK", "id-ID", "uk-UA", "be-BY", "sl-SI"] * 4)
        self.write("UnityPlayer.dll", table)
        self.assertEqual(scan(self.root)[0], [])

    def test_key_split_across_chunks_is_found(self):
        original = core_checks.CHUNK
        core_checks.CHUNK = 64
        self.addCleanup(setattr, core_checks, "CHUNK", original)
        self.write("big.dat", b"x" * 55 + AWS + b"y" * 100)
        self.assertEqual(len(scan(self.root)[0]), 1)

    def test_credential_files(self):
        self.write("release.keystore", b"binary")
        self.write("config/.env", b"API=1")
        paths = sorted(f.location.path for f in scan(self.root)[0])
        self.assertEqual(paths, ["config/.env", "release.keystore"])


class RuntimeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def runtimes(self):
        return {f.title: (f.location.path, f.kind) for f in scan(self.root)[0] if f.rule_id == "RS-CORE-004"}

    def test_code_in_other_runtimes_is_reported_open(self):
        (self.root / "lib/arm64-v8a").mkdir(parents=True)
        (self.root / "lib/arm64-v8a/libapp.so").write_bytes(b"\x7fELF")
        (self.root / "assets").mkdir()
        (self.root / "assets/index.android.bundle").write_bytes(b"var a=1;")
        (self.root / "assets/main.lua").write_bytes(b"print(1)")
        (self.root / "assets/other.lua").write_bytes(b"print(2)")
        self.assertEqual(self.runtimes(), {
            "Flutter (Dart) code": ("lib/arm64-v8a/libapp.so", Kind.OPEN),
            "React Native code": ("assets/index.android.bundle", Kind.OPEN),
            "Lua code": ("assets/main.lua", Kind.OPEN),
        })

    def test_pyinstaller_executable(self):
        exe = b"MZ" + b"\x00" * (2 * 1024 * 1024) + b"MEI\x0c\x0b\x0a\x0b\x0e" + b"\x00" * 80
        (self.root / "tool.exe").write_bytes(exe)
        (self.root / "plain.exe").write_bytes(b"MZ" + b"\x00" * (2 * 1024 * 1024))
        self.assertEqual(self.runtimes(), {"Python code": ("tool.exe", Kind.OPEN)})

    def test_summary_names_the_part_and_the_fix(self):
        (self.root / "game.jar").write_bytes(b"PK")
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(_Output()) as out:
            main(["scan", str(self.root), "--format", "summary"])
        text = out.getvalue()
        self.assertIn("**Java code** in `game.jar`", text)
        self.assertIn("Fix: Run ProGuard", text)


class ProtectionToolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def tools(self):
        return {(f.title, f.location.path, f.kind, f.level) for f in scan(self.root)[0] if f.rule_id == "RS-CORE-005"}

    def test_known_tools_are_recognized(self):
        header = b"MZ" + b"\x00" * 0x1fe + b".text\x00\x00\x00" + b"\x00" * 32 + b".vmp0\x00\x00\x00"
        (self.root / "game.exe").write_bytes(header + b"\x00" * 1024)
        (self.root / "Assembly-CSharp.dll").write_bytes(b"MZ\x00\x00ConfusedByAttribute\x00")
        (self.root / "app.js").write_bytes(b";".join(b"var _0x%04x=1" % i for i in range(300)))
        self.assertEqual(self.tools(), {
            ("VMProtect protection", "game.exe", Kind.PASS, Level.L2),
            ("ConfuserEx protection", "Assembly-CSharp.dll", Kind.PASS, Level.L2),
            ("javascript-obfuscator protection", "app.js", Kind.PASS, Level.L2),
        })

    def test_names_outside_the_header_or_plain_minified_code_are_not_tools(self):
        (self.root / "av.exe").write_bytes(b"MZ" + b"\x00" * 8192 + b".vmp0\x00\x00\x00.themida")
        (self.root / "app.js").write_bytes(b"var _0xabcd=1;var a=2")
        self.assertEqual(self.tools(), set())

    def test_summary_mentions_the_tool(self):
        (self.root / "Assembly-CSharp.dll").write_bytes(b"MZ\x00\x00ConfusedByAttribute\x00")
        (self.root / "game.jar").write_bytes(b"PK")
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(_Output()) as out:
            main(["scan", str(self.root), "--format", "summary"])
        self.assertIn("Protection we found: ConfuserEx in `Assembly-CSharp.dll`", out.getvalue())


class DebugFileTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def test_symbol_files_and_do_not_ship_folders(self):
        (self.root / "Game_Data/Managed").mkdir(parents=True)
        (self.root / "Game_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.pdb").write_bytes(b"pdb")
        (self.root / "Game.app.dSYM/Contents").mkdir(parents=True)
        backup = self.root / "Game_BackUpThisFolder_ButDontShipItWithYourGame"
        backup.mkdir()
        (backup / "Bulk_Assembly-CSharp_0.cpp").write_bytes(b"// generated")
        found = {f.location.path: f.level for f in scan(self.root)[0]}
        self.assertEqual(found, {
            "Game_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.pdb": Level.L1,
            "Game.app.dSYM": Level.L1,
            "Game_BackUpThisFolder_ButDontShipItWithYourGame": Level.L0,
        })


class ScannerIntegrationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_core_checks_run_with_any_platform_and_reach_the_summary(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root, _ = make_player(Path(tmp), "windows", "mono")
            (root / "secrets.json").write_bytes(b'{"aws": "' + AWS + b'"}')
            out = _Output()
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
                main(["scan", str(root), "--format", "json"])
            report = json.loads(out.getvalue())
            self.assertIn("RS-CORE-001", {f["rule_id"] for f in report["findings"]})
            self.assertEqual(report["actions"][0]["title"], "Remove the key from the build and replace it")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

`tests/test_declared_score.py`:

```py
"""Declarations must not yield a numeric assurance score on their own."""
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))
from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Severity
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult, run_scan
from reshield.scoring import summarize


def asset(name, server=False):
    return Asset(name, '', AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L5 if server else Level.L2,
                 symbols=[name], server_side=server)


def finding(level, confidence, asset_id=None, kind=Kind.FAIL):
    return Finding('RS-TEST-001', 'Test', 'test', Severity.HIGH if kind is Kind.FAIL else Severity.INFO,
                   level, Location('code'), 'Test impact', confidence=confidence,
                   asset_id=asset_id, kind=kind)


class DeclaredScoreTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_server_declaration_preserves_level_and_comparison_but_withholds_score(self):
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True)])
        summary = summarize([], manifest)
        self.assertEqual(summary.overall_level, Level.L5)
        self.assertTrue(summary.complete)
        self.assertTrue(summary.declaration_only)
        self.assertIsNone(summary.score)
        self.assertTrue(summary.assets[0].meets_target)
        scan = ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, [], summary)
        data = json.loads(to_json(scan))
        self.assertEqual(data['summary']['overall_level'], 'L5')
        self.assertIsNone(data['summary']['score'])
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertIn('L5 (declared only; unverified)', text)
        self.assertIn('withheld (declared only; unverified)', text)
        self.assertIn('not a verified pass', text)

    def test_unlinked_observation_does_not_unlock_declared_asset_score(self):
        summary = summarize([finding(Level.L0, Confidence.OBSERVED)],
                            Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True)]))
        self.assertTrue(summary.declaration_only)
        self.assertIsNone(summary.score)

    def test_mixed_declared_scoring_scope_withholds_score(self):
        for confidence in (Confidence.OBSERVED, Confidence.INFERRED):
            with self.subTest(confidence=confidence):
                summary = summarize([finding(Level.L2, confidence, 'client')],
                                    Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True), asset('client')]))
                self.assertFalse(summary.declaration_only)
                self.assertIsNone(summary.score)

    def test_no_manifest_declared_findings_also_withhold_score(self):
        for kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                summary = summarize([finding(Level.L2, Confidence.DECLARED, kind=kind)], Manifest())
                self.assertTrue(summary.declaration_only)
                self.assertIsNone(summary.score)

    def test_unknown_assets_supply_no_verification(self):
        summary = summarize([], Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True), asset('unknown')]))
        self.assertTrue(summary.declaration_only)
        self.assertIsNone(summary.score)
        self.assertIsNone(summary.assets[1].observed_level)

    def test_empty_assessment_is_not_declaration_only(self):
        summary = summarize([], Manifest())
        self.assertFalse(summary.declaration_only)
        self.assertIsNone(summary.score)

    def test_incomplete_unity_build_remains_undetermined_under_new_contract(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)
            build = root / 'Build'
            (build / 'Game_Data').mkdir(parents=True)
            (build / 'Game_Data/globalgamemanagers').touch()
            manifest = root / 'reshield.toml'
            manifest.write_text('[[asset]]\nid="server"\nsymbols=["server"]\nserver_side=true')
            scan = run_scan(build, manifest_path=manifest, platform='unity')
            self.assertFalse(scan.summary.complete)
            self.assertIsNone(scan.summary.overall_level)
            self.assertIsNone(scan.summary.score)
            self.assertEqual(scan.summary.assets[0].status, 'undetermined')
            self.assertIn('withheld (assessment incomplete)', to_markdown(scan))

    def test_undetermined_result_takes_precedence_over_declared_score_message(self):
        for kind in (Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                findings = [finding(None, Confidence.OBSERVED, 'client', kind)]
                manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True), asset('client')])
                summary = summarize(findings, manifest)
                self.assertFalse(summary.complete)
                self.assertIsNone(summary.score)
                text = to_markdown(ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, findings, summary))
                self.assertIn('withheld (assessment incomplete)', text)

    def test_higher_observed_level_does_not_verify_lower_declared_level(self):
        summary = summarize([
            finding(Level.L1, Confidence.DECLARED, 'client'),
            finding(Level.L2, Confidence.OBSERVED, 'client'),
        ], Manifest(assets=[asset('client')]))
        self.assertEqual(summary.overall_level, Level.L1)
        self.assertIs(summary.assets[0].confidence, Confidence.DECLARED)
        self.assertTrue(summary.declaration_only)
        self.assertIsNone(summary.score)

    def test_observed_at_same_level_supplies_scoring_evidence(self):
        summary = summarize([
            finding(Level.L1, Confidence.DECLARED, 'client'),
            finding(Level.L1, Confidence.OBSERVED, 'client'),
        ], Manifest(assets=[asset('client')]))
        self.assertIs(summary.assets[0].confidence, Confidence.OBSERVED)
        self.assertFalse(summary.declaration_only)
        self.assertEqual(summary.score, 0)

    def test_not_applicable_observation_does_not_unlock_score(self):
        for manifest in (Manifest(), Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True)])):
            with self.subTest(assets=bool(manifest.assets)):
                findings = [finding(None, Confidence.OBSERVED, kind=Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE)]
                if not manifest.assets:
                    findings.append(finding(Level.L5, Confidence.DECLARED))
                summary = summarize(findings, manifest)
                self.assertTrue(summary.complete)
                self.assertTrue(summary.declaration_only)
                self.assertIsNone(summary.score)

    def test_declared_comparison_is_qualified_even_with_mixed_evidence(self):
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True), asset('client')])
        findings = [finding(Level.L2, Confidence.OBSERVED, 'client')]
        summary = summarize(findings, manifest)
        scan = ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, findings, summary)
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertIn('| server | L5 | L5 (declared) | yes (declared; unverified) |', text)
        self.assertIn('| client | L2 | L2 (observed) | yes |', text)
        self.assertTrue(json.loads(to_json(scan))['summary']['assets'][0]['meets_target'])

    def test_complete_declaration_only_report_does_not_claim_verification(self):
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset('server', True)])
        text = to_markdown(ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, [], summarize([], manifest)))
        self.assertIn('complete (no open/error results)', text)
        self.assertIn('scoring basis is declared only and unverified', text)

```

`tests/test_detection.py`:

```py
"""Observable detection behavior and the agent-facing CLI contract."""
import contextlib
import io
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))

from reshield.cli import main
from reshield.detection import detect_target


class DetectionCommandTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def invoke(self, target):
        output = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output):
            code = main(['detect', str(target)])
        return code, json.loads(output.getvalue())

    def test_unknown_and_missing_paths_have_distinct_statuses(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            code, data = self.invoke(tmp)
            self.assertEqual((code, data['status']), (0, 'unknown'))
            self.assertIsNone(data['selected_platform'])
            code, data = self.invoke(Path(tmp) / 'missing')
            self.assertEqual((code, data['status']), (2, 'error'))
            self.assertTrue(data['errors'])

    def test_overlapping_matches_preserve_scan_priority(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)
            (root / 'Game_Data').mkdir()
            (root / 'Game_Data/globalgamemanagers').touch()
            (root / 'index.html').touch()
            code, data = self.invoke(root)
            self.assertEqual(code, 0)
            self.assertEqual(data['status'], 'detected')
            self.assertEqual(data['selected_platform'], 'unity')
            self.assertEqual([m['platform'] for m in data['matches']], ['unity', 'web'])

    def test_file_target(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            target = Path(tmp) / 'game.pck'
            target.touch()
            result = detect_target(target)
            self.assertEqual(result['selected_platform'], 'godot')
            self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), b'')

    def test_detector_access_error_blocks_selection(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            (Path(tmp) / 'index.html').touch()
            with patch('reshield.adapters.unity.UnityAdapter.detect', side_effect=PermissionError('access denied')):
                code, data = self.invoke(tmp)
            self.assertEqual((code, data['status']), (2, 'error'))
            self.assertIsNone(data['selected_platform'])
            self.assertEqual(data['errors'][0]['platform'], 'unity')
            self.assertEqual(data['matches'][0]['platform'], 'web')

```

`tests/test_developer_summary.py`:

```py
"""Developer summary: grouped by fix, ranked, in plain language."""

import contextlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from reshield.actions import code_areas, developer_actions, to_summary, verdict  # noqa: E402
from reshield.cli import main  # noqa: E402
from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import (  # noqa: E402
    AssetValue, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Remediation, Severity, code_area,
)
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult  # noqa: E402
from reshield.scoring import summarize  # noqa: E402
from test_unity_adapter import _Output, make_player  # noqa: E402

FIX = Remediation("Switch to IL2CPP", Level.L1, "low", ["Set Scripting Backend to IL2CPP."])


def fail(symbol, asset_id=None, severity=Severity.HIGH, fix=FIX, level=Level.L0, rule="RS-TEST-001"):
    return Finding(rule, "Code ships readable", "test", severity, level,
                   Location("Game_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll", symbol=symbol),
                   "Logic can be copied.", remediation=[fix] if fix else [], asset_id=asset_id)


def scan(findings, assets=(), checked=frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1})):
    manifest = Manifest(assets=list(assets))
    return ScanResult(Path("."), [], manifest, findings, summarize(findings, manifest, checked))


def asset(asset_id, value=AssetValue.HIGH, target=Level.L2):
    return Asset(asset_id, "", value, target, symbols=[f"Game.{asset_id.title()}.*"])


class CodeAreaTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_code_area(self):
        self.assertEqual(code_area("Game.Combat.Damage.Calculate"), "Game.Combat")
        self.assertEqual(code_area("Player.Update"), "Player")
        self.assertEqual(code_area("Player..ctor"), "Player")
        self.assertEqual(code_area("Game.Combat.*"), "Game.Combat")
        self.assertIsNone(code_area(None))


class GroupingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_one_fix_resolving_many_results_is_one_action(self):
        result = scan([fail("Game.Combat.Damage.Calc", "combat"), fail("Player.Update")], [asset("combat")])
        actions = developer_actions(result)
        self.assertEqual(len(actions), 1)
        self.assertEqual(actions[0].title, "Switch to IL2CPP")
        self.assertEqual(actions[0].assets, ["combat"])
        self.assertEqual(actions[0].other_places, ["Player"])

    def test_different_fixes_are_separate_actions(self):
        other = Remediation("Remove the debug symbols", Level.L2, "none")
        actions = developer_actions(scan([fail("A.B.C"), fail("A.B.D", fix=other)]))
        self.assertEqual({a.title for a in actions}, {"Switch to IL2CPP", "Remove the debug symbols"})


class PriorityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_important_asset_below_target_is_must_fix(self):
        result = scan([fail("Game.Combat.X.Y", "combat")], [asset("combat", AssetValue.HIGH)])
        self.assertEqual(developer_actions(result)[0].priority, "must")

    def test_low_value_asset_below_target_is_should_fix(self):
        result = scan([fail("Game.Save.X.Y", "save", Severity.MEDIUM)], [asset("save", AssetValue.LOW)])
        self.assertEqual(developer_actions(result)[0].priority, "should")

    def test_without_assets_severity_decides(self):
        self.assertEqual(developer_actions(scan([fail("A.B.C")]))[0].priority, "must")
        self.assertEqual(developer_actions(scan([fail("A.B.C", severity=Severity.MEDIUM)]))[0].priority, "should")

    def test_undetermined_results_become_could_not_check(self):
        open_result = Finding("RS-TEST-002", "Metadata names", "test", Severity.INFO, None,
                              Location("."), "Metadata version is not supported.", kind=Kind.OPEN)
        actions = developer_actions(scan([open_result]))
        self.assertEqual([a.priority for a in actions], ["check"])
        self.assertIn("do not assume", actions[0].why)


class WordingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_summary_avoids_protocol_jargon(self):
        text = to_summary(scan([fail("Game.Combat.X.Y", "combat")], [asset("combat")]))
        for jargon in ("L0", "observed", "notapplicable", "asset-target-v1", "severity-v1", "RS-TEST-001"):
            self.assertNotIn(jargon, text)
        for word in ("decompil", "obfuscat", "metadata", "source code"):
            self.assertNotIn(word, text.lower())
        self.assertIn("Fix th", text)
        self.assertIn("almost exactly as you wrote it", text)
        self.assertIn("Fix:", text)
        self.assertIn("Where:", text)

    def test_verdicts(self):
        self.assertIn("could not check anything", verdict(scan([]), []))
        result = scan([fail("Game.Combat.X.Y", "combat")], [asset("combat")])
        self.assertIn("Your combat is easy to copy", verdict(result, developer_actions(result)))

    def test_code_areas_listed_only_without_assets(self):
        result = scan([fail("Game.Combat.X.Y"), fail("UnitySourceGeneratedTypes.Get")])
        self.assertEqual(code_areas(result), ["Game.Combat"])
        self.assertIn("Which part of your code matters most", to_summary(result))
        self.assertNotIn("Which part of your code matters most",
                         to_summary(scan([fail("Game.Combat.X.Y", "combat")], [asset("combat")])))


class CliTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_summary_format_and_json_actions(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root, _ = make_player(Path(tmp), "windows", "mono")
            out = _Output()
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
                self.assertEqual(main(["scan", str(root), "--format", "summary"]), 0)
            self.assertIn("# Before you release", out.getvalue())
            out = _Output()
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
                main(["scan", str(root), "--format", "json"])
            actions = json.loads(out.getvalue())["actions"]
            self.assertEqual([a["priority"] for a in actions], ["must"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

`tests/test_edge_cases.py`:

```py
"""Edge cases across all three adapters: odd inputs must never crash or look protected."""

import contextlib
import json
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from reshield.adapters import detect_adapters  # noqa: E402
from reshield.cli import main  # noqa: E402
from test_unity_adapter import _Output, make_player  # noqa: E402


def run(args):
    out = _Output()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(_Output()):
        code = main(args)
    return code, out.getvalue()


class EdgeCaseTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def test_missing_target_fails_cleanly(self):
        self.assertEqual(run(["scan", str(self.root / "missing"), "--format", "summary"])[0], 2)

    def test_empty_folder_is_not_a_known_build(self):
        self.assertEqual(detect_adapters(self.root), [])
        self.assertEqual(run(["scan", str(self.root), "--format", "json"])[0], 2)

    def test_unity_build_with_web_files_is_unity(self):
        root, _ = make_player(self.root / "game", "windows", "mono")
        (root / "index.html").write_text("<html></html>", encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual([a.name for a in detect_adapters(root)], ["unity"])

    def test_garbage_pck_is_undetermined_not_protected(self):
        (self.root / "game.pck").write_bytes(b"not a real pack")
        code, out = run(["scan", str(self.root), "--format", "json"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        report = json.loads(out)
        self.assertFalse(report["summary"]["complete"])
        self.assertIsNone(report["summary"]["score"])
        self.assertTrue(any(f["kind"] in ("open", "error") for f in report["findings"]))

    def test_unparseable_javascript_is_undetermined(self):
        (self.root / "index.html").write_text('<script src="app.js"></script>', encoding="utf-8")
        (self.root / "app.js").write_text("function broken( {", encoding="utf-8")
        code, out = run(["scan", str(self.root), "--platform", "web", "--format", "json"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        report = json.loads(out)
        self.assertNotIn("pass", {f["kind"] for f in report["findings"]})
        self.assertFalse(report["summary"]["complete"])

    def test_summary_never_reads_as_safe(self):
        for files in ({"game.pck": b"junk"}, {"index.html": b"<html></html>"}):
            with self.subTest(files=list(files)):
                folder = self.root / next(iter(files)).replace(".", "_")
                folder.mkdir()
                for name, data in files.items():
                    (folder / name).write_bytes(data)
                code, out = run(["scan", str(folder), "--format", "summary"])
                self.assertEqual(code, 0)
                verdict = out.splitlines()[2].lower()
                self.assertIn("can't", verdict)
                self.assertNotIn(" is safe", out.lower().replace("doesn't mean it is safe", ""))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


class BeginnerMistakeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Things a developer new to this will do; each must end in a plain message, never a traceback."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def error_of(self, args):
        err = _Output()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(_Output()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            code = main(args)
        return code, err.getvalue()

    def test_engine_project_instead_of_build(self):
        unity = self.root / "unity"
        (unity / "ProjectSettings").mkdir(parents=True)
        (unity / "ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt").write_text("m_EditorVersion: 6000.0.0f1")
        (unity / "Assets").mkdir()
        godot = self.root / "godot"
        godot.mkdir()
        (godot / "project.godot").write_text("config_version=5")
        for folder, words in ((unity, "Build Settings"), (godot, "Project > Export")):
            code, err = self.error_of(["scan", str(folder)])
            self.assertEqual(code, 2)
            self.assertIn("not a build", err)
            self.assertIn(words, err)

    def test_empty_folder_and_damaged_package(self):
        (self.root / "empty").mkdir()
        (self.root / "game.apk").write_bytes(b"PK\x03\x04 cut off")
        code, err = self.error_of(["scan", str(self.root / "empty")])
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn("folder players get", err)
        code, err = self.error_of(["scan", str(self.root / "game.apk")])
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn("could not be opened", err)

    def test_unreadable_file_is_reported_not_crashed(self):
        from unittest import mock
        from reshield import core_checks
        (self.root / "app.js").write_text("function total(p){return p}")
        (self.root / "locked.js").write_text("function price(p){return p}")
        real = core_checks._scan_secrets

        def deny(path, tools=None):
            if path.name == "locked.js":
                raise PermissionError(13, "Permission denied")
            return real(path, tools)

        with mock.patch.object(core_checks, "_scan_secrets", deny):
            code, out = run(["scan", str(self.root), "--format", "json"])
        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        data = json.loads(out)
        self.assertTrue(any(f["kind"] == "open" and "could not read locked.js" in f["impact"] for f in data["findings"]))
        self.assertFalse(data["summary"]["complete"])

    def test_old_python_gets_a_plain_message(self):
        import io
        import runpy
        from unittest import mock
        cli = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py"
        err = io.StringIO()
        with mock.patch.object(sys, "version_info", (3, 9, 6)), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as stop:
                runpy.run_path(str(cli), run_name="__main__")
        self.assertEqual(stop.exception.code, 2)
        self.assertIn("needs Python 3.11 or later, but this is Python 3.9", err.getvalue())

```

`tests/test_freeze_contract.py`:

```py
"""Pin the frozen public inputs and report fields so unapproved proposals cannot change the contract."""

import contextlib
from dataclasses import fields
import io
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills/reshield/scripts"))

from reshield.adapters.base import ScanContext
from reshield.cli import main
from reshield.models import Finding, Level, Location, Severity


class FreezeContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests for the frozen public contract."""

    def test_scan_context_retains_frozen_inputs(self):
        """New scan inputs need an approved protocol version."""
        self.assertEqual([item.name for item in fields(ScanContext)], ["target", "manifest"])

    def test_finding_retains_frozen_report_fields(self):
        """Protect both the Python constructor and the serialized fields."""
        finding = Finding("RS-TEST-001", "Exposure", "test", Severity.HIGH,
                          Level.L0, Location("Game.dll"), "Managed code ships in the build")
        self.assertNotIn("assessment", {item.name for item in fields(Finding)})
        self.assertNotIn("assessment", finding.to_dict())

    def test_cli_does_not_advertise_unapproved_evidence_input(self):
        """CLI help must only advertise features in the frozen release."""
        output = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output), self.assertRaises(SystemExit) as error:
            main(["scan", "--help"])
        self.assertEqual(error.exception.code, 0)
        self.assertNotIn("--evidence", output.getvalue())
```

`tests/test_godot_adapter.py`:

```py
"""Validate Godot Findings and existing report contracts so format detection never masquerades as protection."""

import contextlib
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "skills/reshield/scripts"))

from reshield.adapters.base import ScanContext
from reshield.adapters.godot import GodotAdapter
from reshield.cli import main
from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Kind, Level
from reshield.scanner import run_scan
from test_godot_pack import REMAP, SOURCE, pack_bytes, token_script


class _Output(io.StringIO):
    """Simulate CLI text output with reconfigure support to keep tests independent of console encoding."""

    def reconfigure(self, **kwargs):
        """Accept kwargs: dict and return None; an in-memory stream needs no encoding switch."""
        pass


class GodotAdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Validate the public adapter; no constructor arguments, each test returns None."""

    def setUp(self):
        """Prepare isolated samples and asset declarations without requiring an installed engine; return None."""
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temporary.name)
        self.pack = self.root / "Game.pck"
        self.adapter = GodotAdapter()
        self.manifest = Manifest(assets=[Asset("combat", "Damage", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L2,
                                              symbols=["res://damage_calculator.gd"])])

    def scan(self, entries=None, manifest=None, target=None):
        """Scan sample entries with optional Manifest/Path inputs and return list[Finding]."""
        self.pack.write_bytes(pack_bytes(entries or [("damage_calculator.gd", SOURCE, 0)]))
        return self.adapter.scan(ScanContext(target or self.root, self.manifest if manifest is None else manifest))

    def test_text_and_tokens_asset_mapping(self):
        """Text and token exports must retain the same original resource identity for meaningful asset assessment."""
        for entries, rule, level in [([("damage_calculator.gd", SOURCE, 0)], "RS-GODOT-001", Level.L0),
                                    ([("damage_calculator.gdc", token_script(), 0),
                                      ("damage_calculator.gd.remap", REMAP, 0)], "RS-GODOT-002", Level.L1)]:
            findings = self.scan(entries)
            exposed = [f for f in findings if f.kind is Kind.FAIL]
            self.assertEqual(len(exposed), 1)
            finding = exposed[0]
            self.assertEqual((finding.rule_id, finding.level, finding.asset_id), (rule, level, "combat"))
            self.assertEqual(finding.confidence, Confidence.OBSERVED)
            self.assertEqual(finding.location.path, "Game.pck")
            self.assertTrue(finding.evidence)
            self.assertEqual({f.rule_id for f in findings}, {"RS-GODOT-001", "RS-GODOT-002"})

    def test_unmatched_and_server_declarations(self):
        """Declarations must not hide exposure or link an asset through a guessed source path."""
        self.manifest.assets[0].server_side = True
        self.assertEqual(next(f for f in self.scan() if f.kind is Kind.FAIL).asset_id, "combat")
        self.manifest.assets[0].symbols = ["res://other/damage_calculator.gd"]
        self.manifest.assets[0].paths = ["src/damage_calculator.gd"]
        self.assertIsNone(next(f for f in self.scan() if f.kind is Kind.FAIL).asset_id)
        self.assertIsNone(next(f for f in self.scan(manifest=Manifest()) if f.kind is Kind.FAIL).asset_id)

    def test_partial_exposure_and_lower_level_precedence(self):
        """Unknown scope must neither erase known exposure nor make an incomplete assessment complete."""
        self.scan([("damage_calculator.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("unknown.gdc", token_script(compressed=100), 0)])
        result = run_scan(self.root, platform="godot")
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.overall_level, Level.L0)
        self.assertFalse(result.summary.complete)
        self.assertTrue(any(f.kind is Kind.OPEN for f in result.findings))
        findings = self.scan([("damage_calculator.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("damage_calculator.gdc", token_script(), 0),
                              ("damage_calculator.gd.remap", REMAP, 0)])
        self.assertEqual({f.level for f in findings if f.asset_id == "combat"}, {Level.L0, Level.L1})

    def test_empty_pck_and_error_are_explicit(self):
        """Keep candidate detection cheap while returning explicit unknown/error findings during scanning."""
        self.pack.touch()
        self.assertTrue(self.adapter.detect(self.pack))
        findings = self.adapter.scan(ScanContext(self.pack, Manifest()))
        self.assertEqual(len(findings), 2)
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.OPEN and f.level is None for f in findings))
        with patch.object(Path, "open", side_effect=PermissionError("denied")):
            findings = self.adapter.scan(ScanContext(self.pack, Manifest()))
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.ERROR for f in findings))
        self.assertEqual(self.adapter.checked_levels, frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1}))

    def test_no_scripts_is_not_a_protection_pass(self):
        """When no script rule applies, report not applicable without awarding protection."""
        findings = self.scan([("icon.png", b"image", 0)])
        self.assertEqual(len(findings), 2)
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE and f.level is None for f in findings))

    def test_single_file_cli_outputs_and_read_only(self):
        """Existing outputs must work without changing a single-file target or leaking absolute finding paths."""
        self.scan()
        before = hashlib.sha256(self.pack.read_bytes()).digest()
        for target in (self.root, self.pack):
            for format_name in ("summary", "json", "markdown", "sarif"):
                with self.subTest(target=target.name, format_name=format_name):
                    output = _Output()
                    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output):
                        code = main(["scan", str(target), "--platform", "godot", "--format", format_name])
                    self.assertEqual(code, 0)
                    text = output.getvalue()
                    # The frozen SARIF contract exports physical locations only; do not add an unapproved symbol field for a test.
                    self.assertIn("RS-GODOT-001" if format_name == "sarif" else "damage_calculator", text)
                    if format_name in ("json", "sarif"):
                        data = json.loads(text)
                        if format_name == "json":
                            self.assertNotIn("assessment", data["findings"][0])
                            expected = "." if target.is_file() else "Game.pck"
                            self.assertEqual(data["findings"][0]["location"]["path"], expected)
        self.assertEqual(before, hashlib.sha256(self.pack.read_bytes()).digest())

    def test_guides_and_summary_disclose_limits(self):
        """Keep guidance synchronized and preserve unchecked protection levels in the developer summary."""
        self.scan([("damage_calculator.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("unknown.gdc", b"GDSC", 0)])
        output = _Output()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output):
            main(["scan", str(self.root), "--format", "summary"])
        text = output.getvalue()
        self.assertIn("couldn't check", text)
        finding = next(f for f in self.scan() if f.kind is Kind.FAIL)
        self.assertEqual(finding.remediation[0].target_level, Level.L1)
        guide = (ROOT / "skills/reshield/references/remediation/godot.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        index = (ROOT / "skills/reshield/references/remediation/README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for rule in ("RS-GODOT-001", "RS-GODOT-002"):
            self.assertIn(rule, guide)
            self.assertIn(rule, index)


class RealGodotArtifactTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Separate real-artifact acceptance from synthetic samples; explicitly skip when exports are absent."""

    def test_real_benchmark_packs(self):
        """Read the two existing PCKs and return None without creating, executing or modifying assessed artifacts."""
        sample = ROOT / "benchmarks/godot/combat-formula"
        packs = {variant: sample / f"builds/{variant}/CombatBenchmark.pck" for variant in ("text", "tokens")}
        if not all(pack.is_file() for pack in packs.values()):
            self.skipTest("Real Godot text/tokens PCKs are absent; synthetic tests do not establish real export acceptance")
        for variant, pack in packs.items():
            with self.subTest(variant=variant):
                before = hashlib.sha256(pack.read_bytes()).digest()
                result = run_scan(pack, manifest_path=sample / "reshield.toml", platform="godot")
                findings = [f for f in result.findings if f.asset_id == "combat-formulas" and f.kind is Kind.FAIL]
                self.assertTrue(findings)
                self.assertEqual({f.level for f in findings}, {Level.L0 if variant == "text" else Level.L1})
                self.assertTrue(all(f.confidence is Confidence.OBSERVED for f in findings))
                self.assertFalse(any(f.kind in (Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR) for f in result.findings))
                self.assertFalse(result.summary.assets[0].meets_target)
                self.assertEqual(before, hashlib.sha256(pack.read_bytes()).digest())
```

`tests/test_godot_pack.py`:

```py
"""Validate boundaries with synthetic artifacts; these samples do not establish real Godot export acceptance."""

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from types import SimpleNamespace
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills/reshield/scripts"))

from reshield.adapters.godot_pack import inspect_pack


SOURCE = b"extends RefCounted\n\nstatic func calculate(value: int) -> int:\n\treturn value * 2\n"
REMAP = b'[remap]\n\npath="res://damage_calculator.gdc"\n'


def token_script(version=101, compressed=0):
    """Build independent format bytes from int version and decompressed-size arguments; return bytes."""
    # Use Godot 4.5.1 format values independently of production parser constants.
    names = ("RefCounted", "calculate", "value", "int")
    encoded = b"".join(struct.pack("<I", len(n)) + bytes(b ^ 0xB6 for b in n.encode("utf-32-le")) for n in names)
    sequence = [(59, 0), (2, 0), (67, 0), (60, 0), (2, 1), (77, 0), (2, 2),
                (84, 0), (2, 3), (78, 0), (86, 0), (2, 3), (84, 0), (48, 0), (2, 2)]
    tokens = b"".join(struct.pack("<II", kind | 0x80 | index << 8, 1) for kind, index in sequence)
    return b"GDSC" + struct.pack("<6I", version, compressed, 4, 0, 0, len(sequence)) + encoded + tokens


def pack_bytes(entries, version=3, flags=None):
    """Build PCK bytes from entries: list[tuple[str, bytes, int]]; versions 2 and 3 are supported."""
    # Use distinct v2/v3 layouts to verify that the parser actually follows directory offsets.
    flags = (2 if version == 3 else 0) if flags is None else flags
    header_size = 104 if version == 3 else 96
    names = [name.encode() + b"\0" * (-len(name.encode()) % 4) for name, _, _ in entries]
    directory_size = 4 + sum(4 + len(name) + 36 for name in names)
    base = header_size if version == 3 else header_size + directory_size
    directory = bytearray(struct.pack("<I", len(entries)))
    payload = bytearray()
    for name, (_, data, entry_flags) in zip(names, entries):
        directory += struct.pack("<I", len(name)) + name
        directory += struct.pack("<QQ", len(payload), len(data)) + hashlib.md5(data).digest() + struct.pack("<I", entry_flags)
        payload += data
    header = b"GDPC" + struct.pack("<5IQ", version, 4, 5, 1, flags, base)
    if version == 3:
        return header + struct.pack("<Q", header_size + len(payload)) + bytes(64) + payload + directory
    return header + bytes(64) + directory + payload


class GodotPackTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Exercise the reader in disposable directories; no constructor arguments, test methods return None."""

    def setUp(self):
        """Prepare isolated samples to avoid reading or writing production artifacts; return None."""
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temporary.name)
        self.pack = self.root / "Game.pck"

    def inspect(self, entries=None, **kwargs):
        """Write test entries and format options, then return PackInspection; this is not a product export workflow."""
        self.pack.write_bytes(pack_bytes(entries or [("res://damage_calculator.gd", SOURCE, 0)], **kwargs))
        return inspect_pack(self.pack)

    def test_text_versions_and_read_only(self):
        """Both container versions must preserve verified text exposure without changing the target."""
        for version in (2, 3):
            with self.subTest(version=version):
                result = self.inspect(version=version)
                before = self.pack.read_bytes()
                self.assertFalse(result.issues)
                self.assertEqual([(e.resource, e.representation) for e in result.exposures],
                                 [("res://damage_calculator.gd", "text")])
                self.assertEqual(result.exposures[0].artifact, ".")
                inspect_pack(self.pack)
                self.assertEqual(before, self.pack.read_bytes())

    def test_tokens_follow_verified_resource_remap(self):
        """Asset linkage must follow an actual remap rather than a guessed extension change."""
        result = self.inspect([("damage_calculator.gdc", token_script(), 0),
                               ("damage_calculator.gd.remap", REMAP, 0)])
        self.assertFalse(result.issues)
        self.assertEqual([(e.resource, e.representation) for e in result.exposures],
                         [("res://damage_calculator.gd", "tokens")])

    def test_bad_containers_are_unknown(self):
        """Container corruption must not be mistaken for encryption or stronger protection."""
        valid = pack_bytes([("main.gd", SOURCE, 0)])
        mutated = []
        for offset, fmt, value in [(4, "<I", 99), (20, "<I", 8), (24, "<Q", 2**63), (32, "<Q", 2**63)]:
            data = bytearray(valid)
            struct.pack_into(fmt, data, offset, value)
            mutated.append(data)
        for data in [b"", b"GDPC", valid[:50], valid[:-1], *mutated]:
            with self.subTest(size=len(data)):
                self.pack.write_bytes(data)
                result = inspect_pack(self.pack)
                self.assertFalse(result.exposures)
                self.assertTrue(result.issues)
                self.assertTrue(all(not issue.error for issue in result.issues))

    def test_resource_path_and_duplicate_rejection(self):
        """Reject traversal and duplicate identities to avoid incorrect linkage or undefined override order."""
        for name in ("../main.gd", "/main.gd", "C:/main.gd", "res://../main.gd", "a\\main.gd", "a/./main.gd"):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                result = self.inspect([(name, SOURCE, 0)])
                self.assertFalse(result.exposures)
                self.assertTrue(result.issues)
        result = self.inspect([("main.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("res://main.gd", SOURCE, 0)])
        self.assertFalse(result.exposures)
        self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_hash_mismatch_retains_other_exposure(self):
        """One corrupt entry must not erase another entry's confirmed exposure."""
        data = pack_bytes([("good.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("bad.gd", SOURCE, 0)])
        # Change only the second payload, preserving the complete directory and first valid observation.
        data = bytearray(data)
        data[104 + len(SOURCE)] ^= 1
        self.pack.write_bytes(data)
        result = inspect_pack(self.pack)
        self.assertEqual([e.resource for e in result.exposures], ["res://good.gd"])
        self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_unsupported_entries_do_not_hide_text(self):
        """Compression, encryption and other languages add uncertainty without upgrading text exposure."""
        for name, data, flags in [("other.gdc", token_script(compressed=1), 0),
                                  ("other.gdc", token_script(version=100), 0),
                                  ("other.gd", SOURCE, 1), ("game.dll", b"MZ", 0),
                                  ("native.gdextension", b"[configuration]", 0), ("other.gd", b"", 2)]:
            with self.subTest(name=name, flags=flags):
                result = self.inspect([("good.gd", SOURCE, 0), (name, data, flags)])
                self.assertEqual([e.resource for e in result.exposures], ["res://good.gd"])
                self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_encrypted_directory_and_sparse_pack(self):
        """Unreadable directories or external sparse payloads must not establish protection."""
        for flags in (1, 3, 4, 6):
            result = self.inspect(flags=flags)
            self.assertFalse(result.exposures)
            self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_invalid_script_structure(self):
        """An extension or magic prefix alone cannot award a protection level."""
        for name, data in [("main.gd", b"arbitrary readable text"), ("main.gd", b"\xff"),
                           ("main.gdc", b"GDSC"), ("main.gdc", token_script()[:-1]),
                           ("main.gdc", token_script() + b"extra")]:
            with self.subTest(name=name, data=data[:12]):
                result = self.inspect([(name, data, 0)])
                self.assertFalse(result.exposures)
                self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_conflicting_and_missing_remaps_are_unknown(self):
        """Keep linkage limitations visible when the original identity cannot be established."""
        for remaps in [[("one.gd.remap", REMAP, 0), ("two.gd.remap", REMAP, 0)],
                       [("one.gd.remap", b'[remap]\npath="res://missing.gdc"', 0)],
                       [("one.gd.remap", b'[remap]\npath="res://../escape.gdc"', 0)]]:
            result = self.inspect([("damage_calculator.gdc", token_script(), 0), *remaps])
            self.assertTrue(result.issues)
            self.assertFalse(any(e.resource in ("res://one.gd", "res://two.gd") for e in result.exposures))

    def test_remap_non_string_values_do_not_crash(self):
        """Non-string untrusted configuration values must not abort the entire scan."""
        for value in (b"123", b"null", b"[]", b"{}", b"true"):
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                result = self.inspect([("good.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("bad.gd.remap", b"[remap]\npath=" + value, 0)])
                self.assertEqual([e.resource for e in result.exposures], ["res://good.gd"])
                self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_token_scalars_and_index_bounds(self):
        """Validate constants and indexes against actual token structure, not merely its header."""
        valid = token_script()
        # Insert a constant after the identifier table and change the final identifier token to a literal.
        identifiers_end = 28 + sum(4 + len(name) * 4 for name in ("RefCounted", "calculate", "value", "int"))
        for constant in (struct.pack("<I", 0), struct.pack("<II", 1, 1), struct.pack("<II", 2, 42),
                         struct.pack("<IQ", 2 | 0x10000, 42), struct.pack("<If", 3, 1.5),
                         struct.pack("<II", 4, 3) + b"abc\0"):
            data = bytearray(valid[:identifiers_end] + constant + valid[identifiers_end:])
            struct.pack_into("<I", data, 16, 1)
            struct.pack_into("<I", data, len(data) - 8, 3 | 0x80)
            result = self.inspect([("main.gdc", data, 0)])
            self.assertTrue(result.exposures)
            self.assertFalse(result.issues)
        for offset, value in [(12, 100001), (24, 0), (len(valid) - 8, 2 | 0x80 | 999 << 8),
                               (len(valid) - 8, 3 | 0x80), (len(valid) - 8, 127)]:
            data = bytearray(valid)
            struct.pack_into("<I", data, offset, value)
            result = self.inspect([("main.gdc", data, 0)])
            self.assertFalse(result.exposures)
            self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_case_variants_remain_visible(self):
        """Case differences must not hide shipped scripts or other-language candidates."""
        result = self.inspect([("MAIN.GD", SOURCE, 0), ("Game.DLL", b"MZ", 0)])
        self.assertEqual([e.resource for e in result.exposures], ["res://MAIN.GD"])
        self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_token_line_and_column_maps(self):
        """Line and column maps must reference the same valid tokens to support structural evidence."""
        valid = token_script()
        identifiers_end = 28 + sum(4 + len(name) * 4 for name in ("RefCounted", "calculate", "value", "int"))
        data = bytearray(valid[:identifiers_end] + struct.pack("<4I", 2, 2, 2, 1) + valid[identifiers_end:])
        struct.pack_into("<I", data, 20, 1)
        self.assertTrue(self.inspect([("main.gdc", data, 0)]).exposures)
        struct.pack_into("<I", data, identifiers_end + 8, 3)
        self.assertTrue(self.inspect([("main.gdc", data, 0)]).issues)

    def test_deeply_nested_remap_is_unknown(self):
        """Remaps accept string paths only; deeply nested collections must not exhaust the parser stack."""
        data = b"[remap]\npath=" + b"[" * 1500 + b"0" + b"]" * 1500
        result = self.inspect([("good.gd", SOURCE, 0), ("bad.gd.remap", data, 0)])
        self.assertEqual([e.resource for e in result.exposures], ["res://good.gd"])
        self.assertTrue(result.issues)

    def test_native_candidates_are_unknown(self):
        """Unsupported native code must not disappear behind two not-applicable results."""
        for suffix in (".so", ".dylib", ".a", ".wasm"):
            result = self.inspect([("libcombat" + suffix, b"native", 0)])
            self.assertTrue(result.issues)
            self.assertFalse(result.exposures)

    def test_whitespace_script_has_bounded_runtime(self):
        """Bound runtime in a separate process so whitespace cannot trigger quadratic backtracking and stall the suite."""
        self.pack.write_bytes(pack_bytes([("blank.gd", b"\n" * 30000, 0)]))
        # The timeout guards against hangs; linear parsing needs no precise performance threshold.
        code = "\n".join([
            "from pathlib import Path", "import sys", "sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1])",
            "from reshield.adapters.godot_pack import inspect_pack",
            "sys.exit(0 if inspect_pack(Path(sys.argv[2])).issues else 1)",
        ])
        result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "-c", code,
                                 str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills/reshield/scripts"), str(self.pack)],
                                capture_output=True, timeout=3)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)

    def test_annotation_identifiers_follow_token_kind(self):
        """Annotation names include @ and may only be referenced by annotation tokens, not ordinary identifiers."""
        names = ("@export", "health", "int")
        table = b"".join(struct.pack("<I", len(n)) + bytes(b ^ 0xB6 for b in n.encode("utf-32-le")) for n in names)
        sequence = [(1, 0), (70, 0), (2, 1), (84, 0), (2, 2)]
        header = b"GDSC" + struct.pack("<6I", 101, 0, 3, 0, 0, 5)
        for first_kind, first_index, expected in [(1, 0, True), (2, 0, False), (1, 1, False)]:
            with self.subTest(kind=first_kind, index=first_index):
                tokens = [(first_kind, first_index), *sequence[1:]]
                data = header + table + b"".join(struct.pack("<II", kind | 0x80 | index << 8, 1) for kind, index in tokens)
                result = self.inspect([("main.gdc", data, 0)])
                self.assertEqual(bool(result.exposures), expected)

    def test_remap_requires_explicit_case_sensitive_path(self):
        """INI inheritance or key folding must not invent asset mappings that Godot does not use."""
        for data in (b'[DEFAULT]\npath="res://damage_calculator.gdc"\n[remap]\n',
                     b'[remap]\nPATH="res://damage_calculator.gdc"',
                     b'[other]\n[remap]\npath="res://damage_calculator.gdc"'):
            result = self.inspect([("damage_calculator.gdc", token_script(), 0), ("sensitive.gd.remap", data, 0)])
            self.assertTrue(result.issues)
            self.assertEqual([e.resource for e in result.exposures], ["res://damage_calculator.gdc"])

    def test_external_native_candidates_are_unknown(self):
        """Explicit code candidates outside PCKs must also remain unknown to avoid false completeness."""
        self.inspect()
        for suffix in (".so", ".dylib", ".a", ".wasm", ".gdextension", ".gde"):
            entry = self.root / ("external" + suffix)
            entry.write_bytes(b"native")
            result = inspect_pack(self.root)
            self.assertTrue(any(i.artifact == entry.name for i in result.issues))
            entry.unlink()

    def test_directory_and_access_failures(self):
        """Preserve evidence for each pack and report access errors rather than pretending nothing was found."""
        self.inspect()
        (self.root / "Other.PCK").write_bytes(pack_bytes([("other.gd", SOURCE, 0)]))
        result = inspect_pack(self.root)
        self.assertEqual({e.artifact for e in result.exposures}, {"Game.pck", "Other.PCK"})
        with patch.object(Path, "open", side_effect=PermissionError("denied")):
            result = inspect_pack(self.pack)
        self.assertTrue(result.issues[0].error)
        self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.root / "missing.pck").issues)

    def test_limits_and_link_rejection(self):
        """Resource budgets and file-type checks must take effect before reading payloads."""
        self.inspect()
        with patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack.MAX_READ_BYTES", 16):
            self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.pack).issues)
        with patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack.MAX_ENTRIES", 0):
            self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.pack).issues)
        with patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES", 1):
            self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.pack).issues)
        with patch.object(Path, "is_symlink", return_value=True):
            self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.pack).issues)

    def test_exact_and_shared_limits(self):
        """Allow exact-boundary samples and share budgets across packs rather than resetting them per file."""
        self.inspect()
        with patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES", len(SOURCE)):
            self.assertFalse(inspect_pack(self.pack).issues)
        with patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack.MAX_ENTRY_BYTES", len(SOURCE) - 1):
            self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.pack).issues)
        (self.root / "Other.pck").write_bytes(self.pack.read_bytes())
        for name, limit in (("MAX_ENTRIES", 1), ("MAX_READ_BYTES", self.pack.stat().st_size - 64)):
            with self.subTest(limit=name), patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack." + name, limit):
                result = inspect_pack(self.root)
                self.assertEqual(len(result.exposures), 1)
                self.assertTrue(result.issues)
        for name in ("MAX_PACKS", "MAX_DIRECTORY_ENTRIES"):
            with self.subTest(limit=name), patch("reshield.adapters.godot_pack." + name, 1):
                self.assertTrue(inspect_pack(self.root).issues)

    def test_windows_reparse_point_is_rejected(self):
        """A junction may not appear as a symlink, so Windows file attributes need an independent check."""
        info = SimpleNamespace(st_mode=0o100644, st_file_attributes=0x400)
        with patch.object(Path, "lstat", return_value=info):
            result = inspect_pack(self.pack)
        self.assertTrue(result.issues)
        self.assertFalse(result.exposures)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Allow standalone execution to validate parser boundaries in CI without a Godot installation.
    unittest.main()
```
