`tests/test_manifest.py`:

```py
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))

from reshield.manifest import ManifestError, find_manifest, load_manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import Confidence, Level  # noqa: E402
from reshield.scoring import summarize  # noqa: E402



def load_text(text):
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        path = Path(tmp) / "reshield.toml"
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        return load_manifest(path)


class AssetDeclarationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    # Test that the manifest loader correctly interprets asset declarations.
    def test_defaults_follow_value(self):
        m = load_text(
            """
            [[asset]]
            id = "a"
            value = "critical"
            symbols = ["A.*"]

            [[asset]]
            id = "b"
            value = "low"
            paths = ["src/b/**"]
            """
        )
        self.assertEqual(m.asset("a").target_level, Level.L3)
        self.assertEqual(m.asset("b").target_level, Level.L1)

    # Test that server_side assets default to L5 and are scored as declared.
    def test_server_side_defaults_to_l5_and_scores_as_declared(self):
        m = load_manifest(ROOT / "examples" / "reshield.toml")
        economy = m.asset("economy")
        self.assertTrue(economy.server_side)
        self.assertEqual(economy.target_level, Level.L5)
        result = next(a for a in summarize([], m).assets if a.asset_id == "economy")
        self.assertEqual(result.observed_level, Level.L5)
        self.assertEqual(result.confidence, Confidence.DECLARED)

    def test_match_asset_by_symbol_and_path(self):
        m = load_manifest(ROOT / "examples" / "reshield.toml")
        self.assertEqual(m.match_asset(symbol="ExampleGame.Combat.DamageSystem").id, "combat-formulas")
        self.assertEqual(m.match_asset(path=r"Assets\Scripts\Save\SaveData.cs").id, "save-format")
        self.assertIsNone(m.match_asset(symbol="ExampleGame.UI.Menu"))

    def test_path_globs(self):
        m = load_text(
            """
            [[asset]]
            id = "a"
            paths = ["src/Combat/*", "src/Save/**", "**/Secrets.cs"]
            """
        )
        a = m.asset("a")
        self.assertTrue(a.matches(path="src/Combat/Damage.cs"))
        self.assertFalse(a.matches(path="src/Combat/Sub/Damage.cs"))  # * stays in one folder
        self.assertTrue(a.matches(path="src/Save/Sub/SaveData.cs"))  # ** spans folders
        self.assertTrue(a.matches(path="Secrets.cs"))
        self.assertTrue(a.matches(path="src/Net/Secrets.cs"))

    def test_find_manifest_stops_at_repo_root(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            outer = Path(tmp)
            (outer / "reshield.toml").write_text('[[asset]]\nid = "x"\nsymbols = ["x"]', encoding="utf-8")
            repo = outer / "repo"
            (repo / ".git").mkdir(parents=True)
            build = repo / "Build"
            build.mkdir()
            self.assertIsNone(find_manifest(build))

            (repo / "reshield.toml").write_text('[[asset]]\nid = "y"\nsymbols = ["y"]', encoding="utf-8")
            self.assertEqual(find_manifest(build), repo / "reshield.toml")

    def test_rejects_invalid_manifests(self):
        bad = {
            "duplicate id": '[[asset]]\nid = "a"\nsymbols = ["x"]\n[[asset]]\nid = "a"\nsymbols = ["y"]',
            "non kebab-case id": '[[asset]]\nid = "Combat_Formulas"\nsymbols = ["x"]',
            "no paths or symbols": '[[asset]]\nid = "a"',
            "bad budget": '[[asset]]\nid = "a"\nsymbols = ["x"]\nperformance_budget = "huge"',
            "bad level": '[[asset]]\nid = "a"\nsymbols = ["x"]\ntarget_level = "L9"',
            "bad toml": "[[asset]\nid = ",
            "single bracket table": '[asset]\nid = "a"\nsymbols = ["x"]',
            "non-string id": '[[asset]]\nid = 1\nsymbols = ["x"]',
            "paths as string": '[[asset]]\nid = "a"\npaths = "src/**"',
            "server_side as string": '[[asset]]\nid = "a"\nsymbols = ["x"]\nserver_side = "false"',
        }
        for name, text in bad.items():
            with self.subTest(name), self.assertRaises(ManifestError):
                load_text(text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_manifest_validation.py`:

```py
"""Malformed declarations must fail at the manifest boundary, before scanning."""
import io
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from contextlib import redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))
from reshield.cli import main
from reshield.manifest import ManifestError, load_manifest
from reshield.models import Level


class ManifestValidationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.manifest = self.root / 'reshield.toml'
        self.build = self.root / 'Build'
        self.build.mkdir()
        (self.build / 'index.html').touch()

    def assert_rejected(self, content, expected):
        if isinstance(content, bytes):
            self.manifest.write_bytes(content)
        else:
            self.manifest.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')
        with self.assertRaises(ManifestError) as caught:
            load_manifest(self.manifest)
        self.assertIn(str(self.manifest), str(caught.exception))
        self.assertIn(expected, str(caught.exception))
        output = self.root / 'report.json'
        stderr = io.StringIO()
        with redirect_stderr(stderr), redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), patch(
            'reshield.adapters.web.WebAdapter.scan'
        ) as scan:
            code = main(['scan', str(self.build), '--manifest', str(self.manifest),
                         '--format', 'json', '--output', str(output)])
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn(expected, stderr.getvalue())
        self.assertNotIn('Traceback', stderr.getvalue())
        self.assertFalse(output.exists())
        scan.assert_not_called()

    def test_invalid_project_shapes(self):
        for value in ['"x"', '1', 'true', '[]']:
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                self.assert_rejected(f'project = {value}', 'project must be a table')
        self.assert_rejected('[project]\nname = []', 'project.name must be a string')

    def test_asset_container_and_entries(self):
        self.assert_rejected('[asset]\nid="a"', 'declare assets with [[asset]]')
        for value in ['1', '"x"', 'true', '[]']:
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                self.assert_rejected(f'asset = [{value}]', 'entry #1: expected a table')
        self.assert_rejected('asset = [{id="a", symbols=["a"]}, 1]', 'entry #2: expected a table')

    def test_asset_field_types_and_allowed_values(self):
        base = '[[asset]]\nid="a"\nsymbols=["a"]\n'
        cases = [
            ('target_level=[]', 'target_level must be a string'),
            ('target_level="L9"', 'target_level must be one of'),
            ('value=[]', 'value must be a string'),
            ('value="huge"', 'value must be one of'),
            ('description=42', 'description must be a string'),
            ('performance_budget=[]', 'performance_budget must be a string'),
            ('server_side="true"', 'server_side must be true or false'),
            ('paths=[1]', 'paths must be a list of strings'),
        ]
        for field, expected in cases:
            with self.subTest(field=field):
                self.assert_rejected(base + field, expected)
        self.assert_rejected('[[asset]]\nsymbols=["a"]', 'id is required')

    def test_invalid_encoding_and_syntax(self):
        self.assert_rejected(b'\xff', 'valid UTF-8')
        self.assert_rejected('[[asset]', 'invalid TOML')

    def test_missing_file_and_permission_errors(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ManifestError, 'unable to read manifest'):
            load_manifest(self.manifest)
        stderr = io.StringIO()
        with redirect_stderr(stderr):
            self.assertEqual(main(['scan', str(self.build), '--manifest', str(self.manifest)]), 2)
        self.assertNotIn('Traceback', stderr.getvalue())
        self.manifest.write_text('')
        with patch('pathlib.Path.open', side_effect=PermissionError(13, 'Permission denied')):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ManifestError, 'Permission denied'):
                load_manifest(self.manifest)

    def test_optional_fields_defaults_and_unknown_fields_stay_supported(self):
        self.manifest.write_text('')
        self.assertIsNone(load_manifest(self.manifest).project_name)
        self.manifest.write_text('''future_field = "allowed"
[project]
name = "Example"
future_project_field = true
[[asset]]
id = "combat"
symbols = ["Combat.*"]
future_asset_field = 1
''')
        manifest = load_manifest(self.manifest)
        self.assertEqual(manifest.project_name, 'Example')
        self.assertEqual(manifest.assets[0].target_level, Level.L2)
        self.assertEqual(manifest.assets[0].description, '')
        self.assertEqual(manifest.match_asset(symbol='Combat.Calculate').id, 'combat')

    def test_unexpected_programming_errors_are_not_hidden(self):
        self.manifest.write_text('[[asset]]\nid="a"\nsymbols=["a"]')
        with patch('reshield.manifest._parse_asset', side_effect=RuntimeError('bug')):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, 'bug'):
                load_manifest(self.manifest)

```

`tests/test_native_adapter.py`:

```py
"""Native and .NET fallback adapter, with synthetic binaries that are never executed."""

import struct
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from reshield.adapters import detect_adapters  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.base import ScanContext  # noqa: E402
from reshield.adapters.native import NativeAdapter  # noqa: E402
from reshield.manifest import Manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import Confidence, Kind, Level  # noqa: E402
from test_unity_adapter import make_player, pe_image  # noqa: E402


def elf(section_names):
    """Minimal 64-bit ELF with the given section names (plus the null and .shstrtab sections)."""
    names = [""] + list(section_names) + [".shstrtab"]
    strtab = b"\0".join(n.encode() for n in names) + b"\0"
    offsets, pos = [], 0
    for n in names:
        offsets.append(pos)
        pos += len(n) + 1
    header = bytearray(64)
    header[:7] = b"\x7fELF\x02\x01\x01"
    struct.pack_into("<H", header, 16, 3)
    shoff = 64 + len(strtab)
    struct.pack_into("<Q", header, 40, shoff)
    struct.pack_into("<HHH", header, 58, 64, len(names), len(names) - 1)
    table = b"".join(struct.pack("<IIQQQQ", offsets[i], 1, 0, 0, 64 if i == len(names) - 1 else 0,
                                 len(strtab) if i == len(names) - 1 else 0) + bytes(24) for i in range(len(names)))
    return bytes(header) + strtab + table


class NativeAdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.adapter = NativeAdapter()

    def scan(self, root=None):
        return self.adapter.scan(ScanContext(root or self.root, Manifest()))

    def write(self, name, data):
        path = self.root / name
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_bytes(data)

    def test_elf_with_symbols_or_debug_info_is_l1(self):
        self.write("app", elf([".text", ".symtab", ".debug_info"]))
        [finding] = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual((finding.rule_id, finding.kind, finding.level), ("RS-NATIVE-001", Kind.FAIL, Level.L1))
        text = " ".join(e.description for e in finding.evidence)
        self.assertIn(".symtab", text)
        self.assertIn("DWARF", text)

    def test_stripped_elf_passes_l2_as_an_estimate(self):
        self.write("app", elf([".text", ".dynsym"]))
        [finding] = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual((finding.kind, finding.level, finding.confidence), (Kind.PASS, Level.L2, Confidence.INFERRED))

    def test_go_binaries_keep_function_names(self):
        self.write("server", elf([".text", ".gopclntab"]))
        [finding] = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual(finding.level, Level.L1)
        self.assertIn("Go", finding.evidence[0].description)

    def test_pe_with_coff_symbols_is_l1(self):
        data = bytearray(pe_image(False))
        offset = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 60)[0]
        struct.pack_into("<I", data, offset + 16, 120)
        self.write("game.exe", bytes(data))
        [finding] = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual(finding.level, Level.L1)
        self.assertIn("120 entries", finding.evidence[0].description)

    def test_dotnet_assembly_is_l0_and_framework_is_skipped(self):
        self.write("MyApp.dll", pe_image(True))
        self.write("System.Text.Json.dll", pe_image(True))
        findings = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual([(f.rule_id, f.level, f.location.path) for f in findings],
                         [("RS-DOTNET-001", Level.L0, "MyApp.dll")])

    def test_unreadable_binary_is_open_not_protected(self):
        self.write("broken.exe", b"MZ" + bytes(10))
        [finding] = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual((finding.kind, finding.level), (Kind.OPEN, None))

    def test_native_is_the_fallback(self):
        self.write("tool", elf([".text"]))
        self.assertEqual([a.name for a in detect_adapters(self.root)], ["native"])
        unity, _ = make_player(self.root / "unity", "windows", "mono")
        self.assertEqual([a.name for a in detect_adapters(unity)], ["unity"])

    def test_non_binary_files_are_ignored(self):
        self.write("readme.txt", b"hello")
        self.assertFalse(self.adapter.detect(self.root))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_output_safety.py`:

```py
"""Report exports must preserve scan target bytes."""
import io
import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from contextlib import redirect_stdout, redirect_stderr
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))
from reshield.cli import main


class OutputSafetyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.build = self.root / 'Build'
        self.build.mkdir()
        self.artifact = self.build / 'app.asar'
        self.original = b'original build bytes'
        self.artifact.write_bytes(self.original)

    def run_scan(self, target, output):
        err = io.StringIO()
        with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), redirect_stderr(err):
            code = main(['scan', str(target), '--platform', 'web', '--format', 'json', '--output', str(output)])
        self.assertEqual(self.artifact.read_bytes(), self.original)
        return code, err.getvalue()

    def link(self, path, target, hard=False):
        try:
            if hard:
                os.link(target, path)
            else:
                path.symlink_to(target, target_is_directory=target.is_dir())
        except (OSError, NotImplementedError) as error:
            self.skipTest(f'Links unavailable: {error}')

    def test_target_file_cannot_be_overwritten(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.artifact, self.artifact)[0], 2)

    def test_output_inside_target_directory_is_rejected_before_scan(self):
        output = self.build / 'report.json'
        with patch('reshield.cli.run_scan') as scan:
            code, error = self.run_scan(self.build, output)
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn('outside the scan target', error)
        scan.assert_not_called()
        self.assertFalse(output.exists())

    def test_symlink_to_build_file_is_rejected(self):
        output = self.root / 'alias.json'
        self.link(output, self.artifact)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.build, output)[0], 2)

    def test_symlinked_parent_into_build_is_rejected(self):
        alias = self.root / 'alias'
        self.link(alias, self.build)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.build, alias / 'new.json')[0], 2)
        self.assertFalse((self.build / 'new.json').exists())

    def test_hard_link_to_file_target_is_rejected(self):
        output = self.root / 'alias.json'
        self.link(output, self.artifact, hard=True)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.artifact, output)[0], 2)
        self.assertEqual(output.read_bytes(), self.original)

    def test_external_hard_link_to_build_member_is_replaced_safely(self):
        output = self.root / 'report.json'
        self.link(output, self.artifact, hard=True)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.build, output)[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(output.read_text())['target'], str(self.build.resolve()))
        self.assertFalse(output.samefile(self.artifact))

    def test_regular_external_report_can_be_created_and_replaced(self):
        output = self.root / 'report.json'
        for _ in range(2):
            self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.build, output)[0], 0)
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(output.read_text())['target'], str(self.build.resolve()))
        self.assertEqual(list(self.root.glob('.reshield-report-*')), [])

    def test_missing_output_parent_reports_error(self):
        code, error = self.run_scan(self.build, self.root / 'missing' / 'report.json')
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn('error:', error)
        self.assertNotIn('Traceback', error)

    def test_replacement_failure_cleans_temporary_file(self):
        output = self.root / 'report.json'
        output.write_text('previous report')
        with patch('reshield.cli.os.replace', side_effect=PermissionError('cannot replace')):
            code, error = self.run_scan(self.build, output)
        self.assertEqual(code, 2)
        self.assertIn('cannot replace', error)
        self.assertEqual(output.read_text(), 'previous report')
        self.assertEqual(list(self.root.glob('.reshield-report-*')), [])


    def test_outward_symlink_named_inside_build_is_rejected(self):
        external = self.root / 'external.json'
        external.write_text('keep external bytes')
        output = self.build / 'report.json'
        self.link(output, external)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.build, output)[0], 2)
        self.assertEqual(external.read_text(), 'keep external bytes')
        self.assertEqual(output.read_text(), 'keep external bytes')

    def test_outward_link_via_target_directory_alias_is_rejected(self):
        external = self.root / 'external.json'
        external.write_text('keep external bytes')
        self.link(self.build / 'report.json', external)
        alias = self.root / 'build-alias'
        self.link(alias, self.build)
        for target, output in [
            (alias, self.build / 'report.json'),
            (self.build, alias / 'report.json'),
        ]:
            with self.subTest(target=target, output=output):
                self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(target, output)[0], 2)
                self.assertEqual(external.read_text(), 'keep external bytes')


    def test_dangling_outward_symlink_does_not_create_external_report(self):
        external = self.root / 'not-created.json'
        output = self.build / 'report.json'
        self.link(output, external)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(self.build, output)[0], 2)
        self.assertFalse(external.exists())
        self.assertTrue(output.is_symlink())


    def test_external_export_with_symlinked_target_parent_uses_resolved_target(self):
        alias = self.root / 'parent-alias'
        self.link(alias, self.root)
        target = alias / 'Build'
        output = self.root / 'report.json'
        self.assertEqual(self.run_scan(target, output)[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(output.read_text())['target'], str(self.build.resolve()))

```

`tests/test_report_coverage.py`:

```py
"""Regression cases for findings excluded from declared-asset summaries."""
import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))

from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Finding, Level, Location, Severity
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult
from reshield.scoring import summarize


def asset(name, server_side=False):
    return Asset(name, name, AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L2, symbols=[name], server_side=server_side)


def finding(level, asset_id=None, confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED):
    return Finding('RS-TEST-001', 'Exposed code', 'test', Severity.HIGH,
                   level, Location('code.dll'), 'Logic can be recovered.',
                   asset_id=asset_id, confidence=confidence)


def result(assets, findings):
    manifest = Manifest(assets=assets)
    return ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, findings, summarize(findings, manifest))


class ReportCoverageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_unlinked_l0_and_unknown_declared_asset_are_visible(self):
        scan = result([asset('combat')], [finding(Level.L0)])
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertIn('**Overall level:** n/a', text)
        self.assertIn('**Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** 1', text)
        self.assertIn('**Lowest reported level among these findings:** L0', text)
        self.assertIn('**Declared assets with unknown levels:** 1 (combat)', text)
        self.assertIn('**Asset:** no asset association', text)
        self.assertIsNone(scan.summary.score)
        self.assertIsNone(json.loads(to_json(scan))['summary']['overall_level'])

    def test_partial_coverage_warns_even_with_overall_level(self):
        scan = result([asset('combat'), asset('world')],
                      [finding(Level.L3, 'combat'), finding(Level.L0)])
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertEqual(scan.summary.overall_level, Level.L3)
        self.assertIn('**Overall level:** L3', text)
        self.assertIn('**Declared assets with unknown levels:** 1 (world)', text)
        self.assertIn('**Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** 1', text)
        self.assertIn('**Asset:** combat', text)

    def test_unknown_asset_id_is_not_treated_as_linked(self):
        text = to_markdown(result([asset('combat')], [finding(Level.L0, 'typo')]))
        self.assertIn('**Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** 1', text)
        self.assertIn("'typo' (undeclared asset ID)", text)

    def test_no_manifest_uses_all_findings_without_selector_warning(self):
        scan = result([], [finding(Level.L0)])
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertEqual(scan.summary.overall_level, Level.L0)
        self.assertIn('all findings; no assets are declared', text)
        self.assertNotIn('Review the manifest', text)
        self.assertNotIn('Asset association and coverage limits', text)

    def test_fully_linked_results_do_not_show_association_warning(self):
        text = to_markdown(result([asset('combat')], [finding(Level.L2, 'combat')]))
        self.assertNotIn('Asset association and coverage limits', text)
        self.assertIn('**Asset:** combat', text)

    def test_declared_server_side_level_is_not_called_observed_coverage(self):
        scan = result([asset('server', server_side=True)], [finding(Level.L0)])
        self.assertEqual(scan.summary.assets[0].confidence, Confidence.DECLARED)
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertIn('L5 (declared)', text)
        self.assertIn('**Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** 1', text)
        self.assertNotIn('assets assessed', text)

    def test_no_findings_still_reports_unknown_assets_and_existing_caveat(self):
        text = to_markdown(result([asset('combat')], []))
        self.assertIn('**Declared assets with unknown levels:** 1 (combat)', text)
        self.assertIn('No results. The adapter reported nothing', text)
        self.assertNotIn('Lowest reported level among these findings', text)


class ReportAssociationDetailTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_unassociated_and_invalid_references_have_distinct_guidance(self):
        text = to_markdown(result([asset('combat')], [
            finding(Level.L0), finding(Level.L1, 'typo'), finding(Level.L2, ''),
        ]))
        self.assertIn('**Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** 3', text)
        self.assertIn('**Findings without an asset association:** 1', text)
        self.assertIn('**Findings referencing undeclared asset IDs:** 2', text)
        self.assertIn("'' (undeclared asset ID)", text)

    def test_lowest_excluded_level_retains_all_its_confidences(self):
        scan = result([asset('combat')], [
            finding(Level.L0, 'combat'),
            finding(Level.L1, confidence=Confidence.INFERRED),
            finding(Level.L1, confidence=Confidence.DECLARED),
            finding(Level.L2, confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED),
        ])
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertIn('**Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary:** 3', text)
        self.assertIn('**Lowest reported level among these findings:** L1 (declared, inferred)', text)
        self.assertEqual(scan.summary.overall_level, Level.L0)

    def test_single_confidence_is_shown_next_to_lowest_level(self):
        for confidence in Confidence:
            with self.subTest(confidence=confidence):
                text = to_markdown(result([asset('combat')], [
                    finding(Level.L0, confidence=confidence),
                ]))
                self.assertIn(f'**Lowest reported level among these findings:** L0 ({confidence.value})', text)

    def test_invalid_reference_alone_does_not_suggest_selector_failure(self):
        text = to_markdown(result([asset('combat')], [finding(Level.L0, 'typo')]))
        self.assertIn('Investigate the inconsistent asset references', text)
        self.assertNotIn('Review the manifest', text)
```

`tests/test_report_format.py`:

```py
"""Readable report: result sections and evidence boundaries, so formatting never hides unknown results."""

import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest

# Import the code that ships with the skill.
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills/reshield/scripts"))

from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Severity
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown, to_sarif
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult
from reshield.scoring import summarize


def make_asset(name: str, value: AssetValue = AssetValue.HIGH) -> Asset:
    """Create a declared asset with the given id and value."""
    return Asset(name, name, value, Level.L2, symbols=[name])


def make_finding(kind: Kind, asset_id: str | None, number: int = 1) -> Finding:
    """Create a valid result of the given kind, asset id and rule number."""
    # Undetermined results carry no level, matching the real data contract.
    leveled = kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS)
    return Finding(
        f"RS-TEST-{number:03d}", f"Result {number}", "unity",
        Severity.HIGH if kind is Kind.FAIL else Severity.INFO,
        Level.L1 if leveled else None,
        Location("code.dll", symbol="Game.Combat", line=12),
        f"Reason {number}", asset_id=asset_id, kind=kind,
    )


def make_scan(assets: list[Asset], findings: list[Finding]) -> ScanResult:
    """Build a ScanResult through real scoring."""
    manifest = Manifest(assets=assets)
    return ScanResult(Path("."), [], manifest, findings, summarize(findings, manifest))


class ReportFormatTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests for the readable report contract."""

    def test_all_result_kinds_keep_the_protocol_order(self):
        """Unknown and pass results never appear under Findings."""
        kinds = (Kind.PASS, Kind.ERROR, Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE, Kind.FAIL, Kind.OPEN)
        scan = make_scan([make_asset("combat"), make_asset("unknown")], [
            make_finding(kind, "combat" if kind is not Kind.ERROR else "typo", i)
            for i, kind in enumerate(kinds, 1)
        ])
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        markers = [
            "# ReShield Report", "**Overall level basis:**",
            "## Asset association and coverage limits", "## Assets",
            "## Findings", "## Not assessed", "## Confirmed protection",
            "Rules not applicable to this build:",
        ]
        positions = [text.index(marker) for marker in markers]
        self.assertEqual(positions, sorted(positions))
        problems = text.split("## Findings", 1)[1].split("## Not assessed", 1)[0]
        self.assertIn("RS-TEST-004", problems)
        for number in (1, 2, 3, 5):
            self.assertNotIn(f"RS-TEST-{number:03d}", problems)
        self.assertIn("could not complete", text)
        self.assertIn("not enough information", text)

    def test_non_fail_results_have_visible_association_limits(self):
        """Invalid asset references are shown for every result kind, not only fail."""
        for kind in (Kind.PASS, Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR, Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                text = to_markdown(make_scan([make_asset("combat")], [
                    make_finding(Kind.FAIL, "combat"), make_finding(kind, "typo", 2),
                ]))
                self.assertIn("**Findings referencing undeclared asset IDs:** 1", text)
                coverage = text.split("## Asset association and coverage limits", 1)[1].split("## Assets", 1)[0]
                self.assertIn("RS-TEST-002", coverage)
                self.assertIn("typo", coverage)
                self.assertIn(kind.value, coverage)
                if kind is not Kind.PASS:
                    self.assertNotIn("Lowest reported level among these findings", coverage)

    def test_unassociated_non_fail_results_remain_visible(self):
        """Pass, open and error results without an asset still appear as association gaps."""
        for kind in (Kind.PASS, Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                text = to_markdown(make_scan([make_asset("combat")], [make_finding(kind, None)]))
                self.assertIn("**Findings without an asset association:** 1", text)

    def test_unassociated_not_applicable_is_not_a_gap(self):
        """A rule that does not apply belongs to no asset, so it must not suggest a manifest error."""
        text = to_markdown(make_scan([make_asset("combat")], [
            make_finding(Kind.FAIL, "combat"), make_finding(Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE, None, 2),
        ]))
        self.assertNotIn("Findings without an asset association", text)
        self.assertNotIn("## Asset association and coverage limits", text)
        self.assertIn("Rules not applicable to this build: RS-TEST-002", text)

    def test_unknown_and_undetermined_assets_are_distinguished(self):
        """Unmatched assets and undetermined assets are reported separately."""
        text = to_markdown(make_scan([make_asset("unknown"), make_asset("blocked")], [
            make_finding(Kind.OPEN, "blocked"),
        ]))
        coverage = text.split("## Asset association and coverage limits", 1)[1].split("## Assets", 1)[0]
        self.assertIn("**Declared assets with unknown levels:** 1 (unknown)", coverage)
        self.assertIn("**Declared assets with undetermined levels:** 1 (blocked)", coverage)
        self.assertIn("| unknown | L2 | n/a | n/a |", text)
        self.assertIn("| blocked | L2 | undetermined | n/a |", text)

    def test_locations_keep_line_and_symbol_for_each_detailed_kind(self):
        """Locations keep their line numbers and symbols."""
        for kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS, Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                text = to_markdown(make_scan([], [make_finding(kind, None)]))
                self.assertIn("code.dll:12 → `Game.Combat`", text)

    def test_missing_evidence_and_remediation_are_explicit(self):
        """Missing evidence or fixes are stated, not invented."""
        text = to_markdown(make_scan([], [make_finding(Kind.FAIL, None)]))
        self.assertIn("**Evidence:** not provided", text)
        self.assertIn("**Fix:** not provided", text)

    def test_withheld_score_preserves_full_weight_without_claiming_attainment(self):
        """Unknown assets stay in the denominator and declared levels are not verified attainment."""
        for confidence in (Confidence.INFERRED, Confidence.DECLARED):
            with self.subTest(confidence=confidence):
                finding = make_finding(Kind.PASS, "combat")
                finding.level = Level.L2
                finding.confidence = confidence
                text = to_markdown(make_scan([
                    make_asset("combat", AssetValue.CRITICAL), make_asset("unknown", AssetValue.LOW),
                ], [finding]))
                self.assertIn("**Attained asset weight:** withheld", text)
                self.assertIn("total declared weight: 5", text)
                self.assertNotIn("**Attained asset weight:** 4/5", text)

    def test_global_unknowns_are_listed_as_undetermined_coverage(self):
        """Whole-build unknown results also appear in the coverage section."""
        text = to_markdown(make_scan([make_asset("combat"), make_asset("save")], [
            make_finding(Kind.ERROR, None),
        ]))
        self.assertIn("**Declared assets with undetermined levels:** 2 (combat, save)", text)
        self.assertIn("**Scope:** whole build", text)

    def test_empty_results_omit_empty_sections_and_keep_no_protection_caveat(self):
        """A scan with no results is never presented as a successful assessment."""
        text = to_markdown(make_scan([], []))
        self.assertIn("# ReShield Report", text)
        self.assertIn("establishes nothing about protection", text.replace("\n", " "))
        self.assertNotIn("## ", text)

    def test_sarif_rules_carry_owasp_maswe_tags(self):
        from dataclasses import replace
        finding = replace(make_finding(Kind.FAIL, "combat"), rule_id="RS-UNITY-001")
        scan = make_scan([make_asset("combat")], [finding, make_finding(Kind.FAIL, "combat", 2)])
        rules = {r["id"]: r for r in json.loads(to_sarif(scan))["runs"][0]["tool"]["driver"]["rules"]}
        self.assertEqual(rules["RS-UNITY-001"]["properties"]["tags"], ["security", "MASWE-0059"])
        self.assertNotIn("properties", rules["RS-TEST-002"])

    def test_all_writers_preserve_the_same_scan_data(self):
        """Markdown changes never alter the JSON or SARIF exports."""
        scan = make_scan([make_asset("combat")], [
            make_finding(Kind.FAIL, "combat"), make_finding(Kind.ERROR, "typo", 2),
        ])
        before_json = to_json(scan)
        before_sarif = to_sarif(scan)
        text = to_markdown(scan)
        self.assertEqual(to_json(scan), before_json)
        self.assertEqual(to_sarif(scan), before_sarif)
        data = json.loads(before_json)
        self.assertEqual(data["summary"]["overall_level"], "L1")
        self.assertIsNone(data["summary"]["score"])
        self.assertNotIn("score_reason", data["summary"])
        sarif = json.loads(before_sarif)["runs"][0]
        self.assertEqual([item["kind"] for item in sarif["results"]], ["fail", "open"])
        self.assertFalse(sarif["invocations"][0]["executionSuccessful"])
        self.assertNotIn("reshield.score", sarif["properties"])
        self.assertIn("at most L1", text)
        self.assertIn("withheld (assessment incomplete)", text)

```

`tests/test_result_kind.py`:

```py
"""Result kinds (SARIF 3.27.9 / XCCDF 1.2) and fail-safe scoring."""

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills" / "reshield" / "scripts"))

from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest  # noqa: E402
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Severity  # noqa: E402
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown, to_sarif  # noqa: E402
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult  # noqa: E402
from reshield.scoring import summarize  # noqa: E402


def result(kind, level=None, asset_id=None, severity=None, rule="RS-TEST-001"):
    return Finding(
        rule_id=rule,
        title="Test rule",
        platform="test",
        severity=severity or (Severity.HIGH if kind is Kind.FAIL else Severity.INFO),
        level=level,
        location=Location(path="build/file"),
        impact="Explanation.",
        kind=kind,
        asset_id=asset_id,
    )


def asset(asset_id, server_side=False, target=Level.L2):
    return Asset(asset_id, "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L5 if server_side else target,
                 symbols=["X.*"], server_side=server_side)


def scan(findings, assets=()):
    manifest = Manifest(assets=list(assets))
    return ScanResult(Path("."), [], manifest, findings, summarize(findings, manifest))


class KindValidationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_kind_defaults_to_fail_like_sarif(self):
        f = Finding("RS-TEST-001", "t", "test", Severity.HIGH, Level.L0, Location("a"), "i")
        self.assertIs(f.kind, Kind.FAIL)

    def test_fail_and_pass_need_a_level(self):
        for kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                result(kind, level=None)

    def test_undetermined_kinds_must_not_carry_a_level(self):
        for kind in (Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR, Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                result(kind, level=Level.L2)

    def test_only_fail_carries_a_problem_severity(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            result(Kind.OPEN, severity=Severity.HIGH)


class FailSafeScoringTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_undetermined_build_never_scores_declared_l5(self):
        # Regression: an unassessable build plus a server-side asset reported L5 / 100.
        s = scan([result(Kind.OPEN)], [asset("economy", server_side=True)]).summary
        self.assertFalse(s.complete)
        self.assertIsNone(s.score)
        self.assertIsNone(s.overall_level)
        self.assertEqual(s.assets[0].status, "undetermined")

    def test_declared_l5_counts_when_assessment_is_complete(self):
        s = scan([result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L0, asset_id="combat")],
                 [asset("combat"), asset("economy", server_side=True)]).summary
        self.assertTrue(s.complete)
        economy = next(a for a in s.assets if a.asset_id == "economy")
        self.assertEqual((economy.observed_level, economy.status), (Level.L5, "determined"))
        self.assertEqual(s.overall_level, Level.L0)

    def test_asset_specific_open_leaves_only_that_asset_undetermined(self):
        s = scan([result(Kind.OPEN, asset_id="save"), result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L1, asset_id="combat")],
                 [asset("combat"), asset("save")]).summary
        status = {a.asset_id: a.status for a in s.assets}
        self.assertEqual(status, {"combat": "determined", "save": "undetermined"})
        self.assertEqual(s.overall_level, Level.L1)  # upper bound
        self.assertIsNone(s.score)

    def test_pass_sets_level_when_no_fail(self):
        s = scan([result(Kind.PASS, Level.L2, asset_id="combat")], [asset("combat")]).summary
        self.assertEqual(s.assets[0].observed_level, Level.L2)
        self.assertTrue(s.assets[0].meets_target)

    def test_fail_outweighs_pass(self):
        s = scan([result(Kind.PASS, Level.L2, asset_id="combat"),
                  result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L0, asset_id="combat")], [asset("combat")]).summary
        self.assertEqual(s.assets[0].observed_level, Level.L0)

    def test_not_applicable_does_not_affect_completeness_or_severity(self):
        s = scan([result(Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE), result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L0)]).summary
        self.assertTrue(s.complete)
        self.assertEqual(s.severity_counts["high"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(s.kind_counts["notapplicable"], 1)

    def test_not_checked_levels(self):
        findings = [result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L0)]
        s = summarize(findings, Manifest(), checked_levels=frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1}))
        self.assertEqual(s.not_checked_levels, [Level.L2, Level.L3, Level.L4])


class ReportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_sarif_kind_level_and_invocation(self):
        sarif = json.loads(to_sarif(scan([
            result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L0, rule="RS-TEST-001"),
            result(Kind.OPEN, rule="RS-TEST-002"),
            result(Kind.ERROR, rule="RS-TEST-003"),
            result(Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE, rule="RS-TEST-004"),
        ])))
        run = sarif["runs"][0]
        kinds = {r["ruleId"]: (r["kind"], r["level"]) for r in run["results"]}
        self.assertEqual(kinds, {
            "RS-TEST-001": ("fail", "error"),
            "RS-TEST-002": ("open", "none"),
            "RS-TEST-003": ("open", "none"),
            "RS-TEST-004": ("notApplicable", "none"),
        })
        invocation = run["invocations"][0]
        self.assertFalse(invocation["executionSuccessful"])
        self.assertEqual([n["associatedRule"]["id"] for n in invocation["toolExecutionNotifications"]],
                         ["RS-TEST-003"])
        self.assertFalse(run["properties"]["reshield.complete"])

    def test_json_exposes_completeness(self):
        data = json.loads(to_json(scan([result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L1), result(Kind.OPEN)])))
        summary = data["summary"]
        self.assertFalse(summary["complete"])
        self.assertTrue(summary["overall_level_is_upper_bound"])
        self.assertIsNone(summary["score"])
        self.assertEqual([f["kind"] for f in data["findings"]], ["fail", "open"])

    def test_markdown_separates_findings_from_not_assessed(self):
        text = to_markdown(scan([result(Kind.FAIL, Level.L1), result(Kind.OPEN)]))
        self.assertIn("**incomplete**", text)
        self.assertIn("at most L1 (assessment incomplete)", text)
        self.assertIn("withheld (assessment incomplete)", text)
        self.assertIn("## Not assessed", text)
        self.assertEqual(text.count("### [HIGH]"), 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_severity_policy.py`:

```py
"""Shared severity policy: context, evidence invariants and export consistency."""
import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))
from reshield import __version__
from reshield.adapters.base import Adapter
from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Evidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Severity
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown, to_sarif
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult, run_scan
from reshield.scoring import Summary, summarize
from reshield.severity import ExposureClass, RulePolicy, SEVERITY_POLICY, normalize_severities


def asset(value=AssetValue.MEDIUM, name='core', target=Level.L2, server=False):
    return Asset(name, '', value, target, symbols=['Game.Combat.*'], server_side=server)


def finding(rule='RS-UNITY-001', asset_id='core', severity=Severity.HIGH,
            confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED, kind=Kind.FAIL, platform='unity', level=None):
    if level is None and kind in (Kind.FAIL, Kind.PASS):
        level = Level.L0
    return Finding(rule, 'Test rule', platform,
                   severity if kind is Kind.FAIL else Severity.INFO,
                   level, Location('code.dll', symbol='Game.Combat.Calculate'),
                   'Test impact', confidence=confidence, asset_id=asset_id, kind=kind,
                   evidence=[Evidence('Synthetic evidence', 'code.dll')])


class SeverityPolicyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_every_matrix_cell(self):
        cases = {
            'RS-UNITY-001': (Severity.LOW, Severity.MEDIUM, Severity.HIGH, Severity.CRITICAL),
            'RS-UNITY-002': (Severity.LOW, Severity.LOW, Severity.MEDIUM, Severity.HIGH),
        }
        values = (AssetValue.LOW, AssetValue.MEDIUM, AssetValue.HIGH, AssetValue.CRITICAL)
        for rule, severities in cases.items():
            for value, expected in zip(values, severities):
                with self.subTest(rule=rule, value=value):
                    out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding(rule)], Manifest(assets=[asset(value)]))
                    self.assertIs(out[0].severity, expected)
                    self.assertIn(f'declared asset value {value.value}', decisions[0].basis)
                    self.assertIs(decisions[0].finding, out[0])

    def test_unassociated_rules_use_baseline_not_manifest_extremes(self):
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL), asset(AssetValue.LOW, name='other')])
        for rule, expected in [('RS-UNITY-001', Severity.HIGH), ('RS-UNITY-002', Severity.MEDIUM)]:
            with self.subTest(rule=rule):
                out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding(rule, asset_id=None)], manifest)
                self.assertIs(out[0].severity, expected)
                self.assertIn('no asset value is associated', decisions[0].basis)

    def test_unknown_rule_preserves_any_adapter_severity(self):
        for severity in Severity:
            with self.subTest(severity=severity):
                out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding('RS-NEW-001', severity=severity)],
                                                       Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.LOW)]))
                self.assertIs(out[0].severity, severity)
                self.assertIn('no registered contextual policy', decisions[0].basis)

    def test_undeclared_and_empty_asset_ids_are_not_unassociated_baselines(self):
        for asset_id in ('typo', ''):
            with self.subTest(asset_id=asset_id):
                out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding(asset_id=asset_id, severity=Severity.CRITICAL)],
                                                       Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.LOW)]))
                self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.CRITICAL)
                self.assertIn('asset ID is not declared', decisions[0].basis)

    def test_platform_mismatch_preserves_adapter_value(self):
        out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding(platform='web', severity=Severity.LOW)],
                                               Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)]))
        self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.LOW)
        self.assertIn('rule/platform mismatch', decisions[0].basis)

    def test_invalid_internal_value_is_not_coerced(self):
        for value in ('critical', None, 4):
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding(severity=Severity.HIGH)],
                                                       Manifest(assets=[asset(value)]))
                self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.HIGH)
                self.assertIn('invalid internal asset value', decisions[0].basis)

    def test_non_fail_results_remain_info_and_do_not_escalate(self):
        for kind in (Kind.PASS, Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR, Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                out, decisions = normalize_severities([finding(kind=kind)],
                                                       Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)]))
                self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.INFO)
                self.assertIs(out[0].kind, kind)
                self.assertIn('Non-fail', decisions[0].basis)

    def test_confidence_target_cost_and_server_declarations_do_not_reduce_exposure(self):
        for confidence in Confidence:
            for target in (Level.L0, Level.L5):
                with self.subTest(confidence=confidence, target=target):
                    declared = asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL, target=target, server=True)
                    declared.performance_budget = 'none'
                    out, _ = normalize_severities([finding(confidence=confidence)], Manifest(assets=[declared]))
                    self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.CRITICAL)
                    self.assertIs(out[0].confidence, confidence)
                    self.assertEqual(out[0].asset_id, 'core')
                    self.assertIs(out[0].level, Level.L0)

    def test_equivalent_exposure_classes_are_platform_independent(self):
        registry = {f'RS-{platform.upper()}-999': RulePolicy(platform, ExposureClass.SOURCE, Severity.HIGH)
                    for platform in ('unity', 'godot', 'web')}
        for platform in ('unity', 'godot', 'web'):
            with self.subTest(platform=platform):
                out, _ = normalize_severities(
                    [finding(f'RS-{platform.upper()}-999', platform=platform)],
                    Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)]), registry=registry)
                self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.CRITICAL)
        # An explicit empty test registry must not silently enable production policies.
        out, _ = normalize_severities([finding()], Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.LOW)]), registry={})
        self.assertIs(out[0].severity, Severity.HIGH)

    def test_copies_preserve_all_other_fields_and_are_idempotent(self):
        original = finding()
        before = copy.deepcopy(original.to_dict())
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.LOW)])
        out, _ = normalize_severities([original], manifest)
        self.assertIsNot(out[0], original)
        self.assertEqual(original.to_dict(), before)
        expected = dict(before, severity='low')
        self.assertEqual(out[0].to_dict(), expected)
        again, _ = normalize_severities(out, manifest)
        self.assertEqual(again[0].to_dict(), expected)
        self.assertEqual(normalize_severities([], manifest), ([], []))

    def test_normalization_changes_counts_but_not_scoring_or_completeness(self):
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.LOW)])
        findings = [finding(), finding(kind=Kind.OPEN, asset_id=None)]
        before = summarize(findings, manifest)
        normalized, _ = normalize_severities(findings, manifest)
        after = summarize(normalized, manifest)
        self.assertEqual(after.overall_level, before.overall_level)
        self.assertEqual(after.score, before.score)
        self.assertEqual(after.complete, before.complete)
        self.assertEqual(after.assets, before.assets)
        self.assertEqual(after.severity_counts['low'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(after.severity_counts['high'], 0)


class SeverityReportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def result(self, findings, manifest):
        normalized, decisions = normalize_severities(findings, manifest)
        return ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, normalized,
                          summarize(normalized, manifest), decisions)

    def test_all_formats_render_same_normalized_findings_without_new_fields(self):
        report = self.result([finding(), finding(kind=Kind.OPEN, asset_id=None)],
                             Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)]))
        data = json.loads(to_json(report))
        self.assertEqual([f['severity'] for f in data['findings']], ['critical', 'info'])
        self.assertEqual(data['summary']['severity_counts']['critical'], 1)
        self.assertFalse(data['summary']['complete'])
        self.assertIsNone(data['summary']['score'])
        text = to_markdown(report)
        self.assertIn(SEVERITY_POLICY, text)
        self.assertIn('### [CRITICAL]', text)
        self.assertIn('**Severity basis:** source-like-implementation; declared asset value critical', text)
        sarif = json.loads(to_sarif(report))
        self.assertEqual([r['level'] for r in sarif['runs'][0]['results']], ['error', 'none'])
        self.assertNotIn('severity_decisions', data)
        self.assertNotIn('severity_basis', data['findings'][0])
        self.assertNotIn('severity_counts', sarif['runs'][0]['properties'])

    def test_each_fallback_reason_is_visible_in_markdown(self):
        cases = [
            (finding('RS-NEW-001'), Manifest(), 'no registered contextual policy'),
            (finding(platform='web'), Manifest(), 'rule/platform mismatch'),
            (finding(asset_id=None), Manifest(), 'rule baseline used'),
            (finding(asset_id='typo'), Manifest(), 'asset ID is not declared'),
        ]
        for f, manifest, expected in cases:
            with self.subTest(expected=expected):
                # Isolate rationale rendering from summary calculation.
                normalized, decisions = normalize_severities([f], manifest)
                report = ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, normalized,
                                    Summary(None, None, {}), decisions)
                self.assertIn(expected, to_markdown(report))

    def test_manual_or_replaced_findings_are_not_labeled_policy_normalized(self):
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)])
        supplied = finding()
        manual = ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, [supplied], summarize([supplied], manifest))
        text = to_markdown(manual)
        self.assertIn('not applied by this result', text)
        self.assertIn('### [HIGH]', text)
        self.assertIn('normalization is not recorded', text)
        report = self.result([supplied], manifest)
        report.findings = [supplied]
        self.assertIn('not applied by this result', to_markdown(report))

    def test_changed_decision_inputs_hide_stale_rationale(self):
        for field, value in [('asset_id', 'typo'), ('rule_id', 'RS-NEW-001'),
                             ('platform', 'web'), ('severity', Severity.LOW),
                             ('kind', Kind.PASS)]:
            with self.subTest(field=field):
                manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)])
                report = self.result([finding()], manifest)
                setattr(report.findings[0], field, value)
                text = to_markdown(report)
                self.assertIn('not applied by this result', text)
                self.assertNotIn('declared asset value critical', text)
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[asset(AssetValue.CRITICAL)])
        report = self.result([finding()], manifest)
        manifest.assets[0].value = AssetValue.LOW
        self.assertIn('not applied by this result', to_markdown(report))
        self.assertNotIn('declared asset value critical', to_markdown(report))
        refreshed = self.result(report.findings, manifest)
        self.assertIn('declared asset value low', to_markdown(refreshed))


class SeverityScannerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_scanner_normalizes_copies_before_summary(self):
        class FakeAdapter(Adapter):
            name = 'unity'
            display_name = 'Test adapter'
            checked_levels = frozenset({Level.L0})

            def detect(self, target):
                return True

            def scan(self, context):
                return [supplied]

        supplied = finding()
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)
            target = root / 'Build'
            target.mkdir()
            manifest = root / 'reshield.toml'
            manifest.write_text('[[asset]]\nid="core"\nvalue="critical"\nsymbols=["core"]\ntarget_level="L0"')
            with patch('reshield.scanner.detect_adapters', return_value=[FakeAdapter()]):
                report = run_scan(target, manifest_path=manifest)
            self.assertIs(supplied.severity, Severity.HIGH)
            self.assertIs(report.findings[0].severity, Severity.CRITICAL)
            self.assertEqual(report.summary.severity_counts['critical'], 1)
            self.assertEqual(report.summary.score, 100)
            self.assertIn('Severity basis', to_markdown(report))

    def test_synthetic_unity_cli_uses_contextual_severity_in_all_formats(self):
        from test_unity_adapter import make_player
        cli = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py'
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)
            for backend, level, expected, sarif_level in (
                ('mono', 'L0', 'critical', 'error'),
                ('il2cpp', 'L1', 'high', 'error'),
            ):
                with self.subTest(backend=backend):
                    target, _ = make_player(root / backend, 'windows', backend)
                    manifest = root / f'{backend}.toml'
                    manifest.write_text('[[asset]]\nid="core"\nvalue="critical"\n'
                                        f'symbols=["Game.Combat.*"]\ntarget_level="{level}"', encoding='utf-8')
                    outputs = {}
                    for fmt in ('json', 'markdown', 'sarif'):
                        output = root / f'{backend}.{fmt}'
                        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', '-S', str(cli), 'scan', str(target),
                                               '--manifest', str(manifest), '--format', fmt, '--output', str(output)],
                                              cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8')
                        self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, proc.stderr)
                        outputs[fmt] = output.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
                    data = json.loads(outputs['json'])
                    fail = next(f for f in data['findings'] if f['kind'] == 'fail')
                    self.assertEqual(fail['severity'], expected)
                    self.assertEqual(data['summary']['severity_counts'][expected], 1)
                    self.assertEqual(data['summary']['score'], 100)
                    self.assertEqual(data['tool']['version'], __version__)
                    self.assertIn(f'### [{expected.upper()}]', outputs['markdown'])
                    self.assertIn('declared asset value critical', outputs['markdown'])
                    sarif = json.loads(outputs['sarif'])
                    fail_result = next(r for r in sarif['runs'][0]['results'] if r['kind'] == 'fail')
                    self.assertEqual(fail_result['level'], sarif_level)
```

`tests/test_target_scoring.py`:

```py
"""Regression tests for asset-target scoring rules, eligibility and output consistency."""
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts'))
from reshield import __version__
from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Finding, Kind, Level, Location, Severity
from reshield.report import to_json, to_markdown, to_sarif
from reshield.scanner import ScanResult
from reshield.scoring import SCORE_POLICY, summarize


def asset(name, value=AssetValue.HIGH, target=Level.L2, server=False):
    return Asset(name, '', value, target, symbols=[name], server_side=server)


def finding(name, level, confidence=Confidence.OBSERVED, kind=Kind.FAIL, platform='test'):
    return Finding('RS-TEST-001', 'Test result', platform,
                   Severity.HIGH if kind is Kind.FAIL else Severity.INFO,
                   level, Location('code'), 'Test impact', confidence=confidence,
                   asset_id=name, kind=kind)


def scan(assets, findings):
    manifest = Manifest(assets=assets)
    return ScanResult(Path('.'), [], manifest, findings, summarize(findings, manifest))


class TargetScoreTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_each_asset_value_has_the_documented_share_of_target_weight(self):
        """One asset per value class, attained one at a time, to verify the published weights."""
        values = (AssetValue.CRITICAL, AssetValue.HIGH, AssetValue.MEDIUM, AssetValue.LOW)
        assets = [asset(value.value, value) for value in values]
        for attained, expected in zip(values, (40, 30, 20, 10)):
            with self.subTest(value=attained):
                findings = [finding(a.id, Level.L2 if a.value is attained else Level.L0) for a in assets]
                self.assertEqual(scan(assets, findings).summary.score, expected)

    def test_critical_gap_and_low_attainment_yield_twenty_percent(self):
        result = scan([asset('core', AssetValue.CRITICAL, Level.L3),
                       asset('ui', AssetValue.LOW, Level.L1)],
                      [finding('core', Level.L0), finding('ui', Level.L1)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 20)
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.overall_level, Level.L0)
        text = to_markdown(result)
        self.assertIn('**Attained asset weight:** 1/5', text)
        self.assertIn('**Critical assets below target:** core', text)
        self.assertIn(SCORE_POLICY, text)
        self.assertIn('20% of declared asset target weight', text)

    def test_many_low_assets_cannot_hide_critical_warning(self):
        assets = [asset('core', AssetValue.CRITICAL, Level.L3)]
        assets += [asset(f'low-{i}', AssetValue.LOW, Level.L1) for i in range(10)]
        findings = [finding('core', Level.L0)] + [finding(a.id, Level.L1) for a in assets[1:]]
        result = scan(assets, findings)
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 71)
        self.assertIn('Critical assets below target', to_markdown(result))

    def test_low_goal_can_attain_one_hundred_without_raising_level(self):
        result = scan([asset('public', target=Level.L0)], [finding('public', Level.L0)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 100)
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.overall_level, Level.L0)
        self.assertIn('not absolute reverse-engineering resistance', to_markdown(result))

    def test_duplicate_and_reordered_findings_do_not_reweight_assets(self):
        assets = [asset('a'), asset('b', AssetValue.LOW)]
        evidence = [finding('a', Level.L0), finding('b', Level.L2)]
        baseline = scan(assets, evidence).summary.score
        self.assertEqual(baseline, 25)
        self.assertEqual(scan(assets, list(reversed(evidence)) + evidence * 4).summary.score, baseline)
        self.assertEqual(scan(list(reversed(assets)), evidence).summary.score, baseline)

    def test_any_unknown_asset_withholds_even_when_complete(self):
        result = scan([asset('a'), asset('b')], [finding('a', Level.L2)])
        self.assertTrue(result.summary.complete)
        self.assertIsNone(result.summary.score)
        self.assertIn('asset levels are unknown', to_markdown(result))

    def test_inferred_or_declared_anywhere_in_scope_withholds(self):
        for confidence in (Confidence.INFERRED, Confidence.DECLARED):
            with self.subTest(confidence=confidence):
                result = scan([asset('a'), asset('b')],
                              [finding('a', Level.L2), finding('b', Level.L2, confidence)])
                self.assertIsNone(result.summary.score)
                self.assertTrue(result.summary.assets[1].meets_target)
                self.assertIn(f'yes ({confidence.value}; unverified)', to_markdown(result))

    def test_downgrading_or_removing_evidence_never_improves_score(self):
        assets = [asset('a')]
        self.assertEqual(scan(assets, [finding('a', Level.L2)]).summary.score, 100)
        self.assertIsNone(scan(assets, [finding('a', Level.L2, Confidence.INFERRED)]).summary.score)
        self.assertIsNone(scan(assets, []).summary.score)

    def test_same_level_observed_support_is_preserved(self):
        result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', Level.L2, Confidence.INFERRED),
                                    finding('a', Level.L2)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 100)

    def test_unlinked_results_cannot_unlock_unknown_or_change_valid_score(self):
        self.assertIsNone(scan([asset('a')], [finding(None, Level.L2)]).summary.score)
        result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', Level.L2), finding(None, Level.L0)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 100)
        self.assertIn('Findings excluded from the declared-asset summary', to_markdown(result))

    def test_no_manifest_retains_level_but_withholds_target_score(self):
        result = scan([], [finding(None, Level.L1)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.overall_level, Level.L1)
        self.assertIsNone(result.summary.score)
        self.assertIn('no declared assets', to_markdown(result))

    def test_open_and_error_override_an_otherwise_attained_asset(self):
        for kind in (Kind.OPEN, Kind.ERROR):
            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
                result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', Level.L2), finding(None, None, kind=kind)])
                self.assertFalse(result.summary.complete)
                self.assertIsNone(result.summary.score)
                self.assertIn('withheld (assessment incomplete)', to_markdown(result))

    def test_not_applicable_is_not_attainment_evidence(self):
        result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', None, kind=Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE)])
        self.assertTrue(result.summary.complete)
        self.assertIsNone(result.summary.score)

    def test_rounding_is_nearest_integer_ties_to_even(self):
        result = scan([asset('a', AssetValue.CRITICAL), asset('b', AssetValue.HIGH),
                       asset('c', AssetValue.LOW)],
                      [finding('a', Level.L0), finding('b', Level.L0), finding('c', Level.L2)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 12)  # 100 * 1 / 8 = 12.5

    def test_platform_and_severity_do_not_change_target_attainment(self):
        for platform in ('unity', 'godot', 'web'):
            with self.subTest(platform=platform):
                result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', Level.L2, platform=platform)])
                self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 100)
                self.assertEqual(result.findings[0].severity, Severity.HIGH)

    def test_fail_precedence_remains_and_all_below_target_is_zero(self):
        result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', Level.L0), finding('a', Level.L2, kind=Kind.PASS)])
        self.assertEqual(result.summary.score, 0)
        self.assertFalse(result.summary.assets[0].meets_target)

    def test_tool_version_identifies_policy_without_new_json_fields(self):
        result = scan([asset('a')], [finding('a', Level.L2)])
        data = json.loads(to_json(result))
        self.assertEqual(data['summary']['score'], 100)
        self.assertEqual(data['tool']['version'], __version__)
        self.assertNotIn('score_reason', data['summary'])
        self.assertNotIn('score_policy', data['summary'])
        sarif = json.loads(to_sarif(result))
        self.assertEqual(sarif['runs'][0]['tool']['driver']['version'], __version__)
        self.assertIn('complete', to_markdown(result))


class TargetScoreCliTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_synthetic_unity_artifacts_produce_observed_target_scores_through_cli(self):
        """Run the CLI on synthetic samples to check wiring; not a calibration against real builds."""
        from test_unity_adapter import make_player

        cli = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'skills/reshield/scripts/reshield_cli.py'
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp)
            for backend, level in (('mono', 'L0'), ('il2cpp', 'L1')):
                with self.subTest(backend=backend):
                    target, _ = make_player(root / backend, 'windows', backend)
                    before = {
                        file.relative_to(target): hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                        for file in target.rglob('*') if file.is_file()
                    }
                    manifest = root / f'{backend}.toml'
                    manifest.write_text(
                        '[[asset]]\nid="combat"\nvalue="critical"\n'
                        'symbols=["Game.Combat.*"]\n'
                        f'target_level="{level}"\n', encoding='utf-8',
                    )
                    outputs = {}
                    for fmt in ('json', 'markdown', 'sarif'):
                        output = root / f'{backend}.{fmt}'
                        command = subprocess.run(
                            [sys.executable, '-B', '-S', str(cli), 'scan', str(target),
                             '--manifest', str(manifest), '--format', fmt, '--output', str(output)],
                            cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8',
                        )
                        self.assertEqual(command.returncode, 0, command.stderr)
                        outputs[fmt] = output.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
                    data = json.loads(outputs['json'])
                    self.assertEqual(data['tool']['version'], __version__)
                    self.assertEqual(data['summary']['score'], 100)
                    self.assertEqual(data['summary']['overall_level'], level)
                    self.assertTrue(data['summary']['complete'])
                    self.assertEqual(data['summary']['assets'][0]['confidence'], 'observed')
                    self.assertEqual(data['summary']['assets'][0]['id'], 'combat')
                    self.assertEqual(data['target'], str(target.resolve()))
                    self.assertEqual(data['manifest'], str(manifest))
                    self.assertIn(SCORE_POLICY, outputs['markdown'])
                    self.assertIn('100% of declared asset target weight', outputs['markdown'])
                    self.assertIn('**Attained asset weight:** 4/4', outputs['markdown'])
                    sarif = json.loads(outputs['sarif'])
                    self.assertEqual(sarif['runs'][0]['tool']['driver']['version'], __version__)
                    self.assertTrue(sarif['runs'][0]['invocations'][0]['executionSuccessful'])
                    after = {
                        file.relative_to(target): hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                        for file in target.rglob('*') if file.is_file()
                    }
                    self.assertEqual(before, after)
```
