`tests/test_unity_adapter.py`:

```py
"""Verify exposure evidence with temporary synthetic samples. These samples are not real Unity builds."""

import contextlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "skills/reshield/scripts"))

from reshield.adapters.base import ScanContext
from reshield.adapters.unity import UnityAdapter
from reshield.adapters.unity_layout import inspect_player
from reshield.cli import main
from reshield.manifest import Asset, Manifest
from reshield.models import AssetValue, Confidence, Kind, Level


class _Output(io.StringIO):
    """Text stream that mimics the CLI output stream, keeping the reconfigure interface without changing product code."""

    def reconfigure(self, **kwargs):
        """Accept kwargs; an in-memory stream needs no re-encoding. Returns None."""
        pass


def pe_image(managed=False):
    """Build a structural PE sample; managed: bool controls whether it has a CLI header. Returns non-executable bytes."""
    data = bytearray(2048)
    data[:2] = b"MZ"
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, 60, 128)
    data[128:132] = b"PE\0\0"
    struct.pack_into("<HHIIIHH", data, 132, 0x14C, 1, 0, 0, 0, 224, 0x2102)
    struct.pack_into("<H", data, 152, 0x10B)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, 152 + 92, 16)
    data[376:384] = b".text\0\0\0"
    struct.pack_into("<IIII", data, 384, 1536, 0x2000, 1536, 512)
    if managed:
        # CLI and metadata use RVAs; a bare BSJB prefix must not pass the managed assembly check.
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, 152 + 96 + 14 * 8, 0x2000, 72)
        struct.pack_into("<IHHIII", data, 512, 72, 2, 5, 0x2080, 256, 1)
        root = 640
        data[root:root + 4] = b"BSJB"
        struct.pack_into("<HHII", data, root + 4, 1, 1, 0, 12)
        data[root + 16:root + 28] = b"v4.0.30319\0\0"
        struct.pack_into("<HH", data, root + 28, 0, 2)
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, root + 32, 64, 80)
        data[root + 40:root + 44] = b"#~\0\0"
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, root + 44, 144, 80)
        data[root + 52:root + 64] = b"#Strings\0\0\0\0"
        tables = root + 64
        # One TypeDef row and one MethodDef row; real type names live only in the string heap.
        struct.pack_into("<IBBBBQQII", data, tables, 0, 2, 0, 0, 1, 0x44, 0, 1, 1)
        struct.pack_into("<IHHHHH", data, tables + 32, 1, 1, 12, 0, 1, 1)
        struct.pack_into("<IHHHHH", data, tables + 46, 0x2200, 0, 6, 24, 0, 1)
        names = b"\0Calculator\0Game.Combat\0Damage\0"
        data[root + 144:root + 144 + len(names)] = names
        data[1024:1026] = b"\x06\x2a"  # Tiny IL body containing a one-byte ret.
    return bytes(data)


def native_image(platform):
    """Build a native file structure for platform: str and return bytes, without depending on a host compiler."""
    if platform == "windows":
        return pe_image()
    data = bytearray(256)
    if platform == "linux":
        data[:16] = b"\x7fELF\x02\x01\x01" + bytes(9)
        struct.pack_into("<HHIQQQIHHHHHH", data, 16, 3, 62, 1, 0, 64, 0, 0, 64, 56, 1, 0, 0, 0)
        struct.pack_into("<IIQQQQQQ", data, 64, 1, 5, 0, 0, 0, 256, 256, 4096)
    else:
        struct.pack_into("<IIIIIIII", data, 0, 0xFEEDFACF, 0x01000007, 3, 6, 1, 72, 0, 0)
        struct.pack_into("<II16sQQQQIIII", data, 32, 0x19, 72, b"__TEXT", 0, 256, 0, 256, 5, 5, 0, 0)
    return bytes(data)


def metadata_image():
    """Build a v31 metadata sample following Unity 2022.3.62f2 / 6000.0.68f1; returns bytes."""
    strings = b"\0Game.dll\0Calculator\0Game.Combat\0Damage\0"
    image_offset = 256 + len(strings)
    type_offset = image_offset + 40
    method_offset = type_offset + 88
    data = bytearray(method_offset + 36)
    struct.pack_into("<II", data, 0, 0xFAB11BAF, 31)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, 8, 256)
    # Section indexes were checked independently against the Unity header, not taken from the code under test.
    for pair, offset, size in ((2, 256, len(strings)), (5, method_offset, 36), (19, type_offset, 88), (20, image_offset, 40)):
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, 8 + pair * 8, offset, size)
    data[256:image_offset] = strings
    struct.pack_into("<IIII", data, image_offset, 1, 0, 0, 1)
    struct.pack_into("<II", data, type_offset, 10, 21)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, type_offset + 36, 0)
    struct.pack_into("<H", data, type_offset + 64, 1)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, type_offset + 84, 0x02000001)
    struct.pack_into("<II", data, method_offset, 33, 0)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, method_offset + 24, 0x06000001)
    return bytes(data)


def write_file(path, content):
    """Write a synthetic sample (path: Path, content: bytes) and return the Path. Test use only."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(content)
    return path


def make_player(parent, platform, backend):
    """Create one Player sample from (Path, str, str); returns (root, evidence file)."""
    root = parent / ("Tëst Gâme.app" if platform == "macos" else "Tëst Gâme")  # non-ASCII path on purpose
    data = root / ("Contents/Resources/Data" if platform == "macos" else "Game_Data")
    write_file(data / "globalgamemanagers", b"Unity layout fixture")
    if backend == "mono":
        runtime = {
            "windows": "MonoBleedingEdge/EmbedRuntime/mono-2.0-bdwgc.dll",
            # Real Linux players keep the runtime inside the data folder (2022.3 build).
            "linux": "Game_Data/MonoBleedingEdge/x86_64/libmonobdwgc-2.0.so",
            "macos": "Contents/Frameworks/MonoBleedingEdge/MonoEmbedRuntime/osx/libmonobdwgc-2.0.dylib",
        }[platform]
        write_file(root / runtime, native_image(platform))
        artifact = write_file(data / "Managed/Game.Logic.dll", pe_image(managed=True))
    else:
        library = {"windows": "GameAssembly.dll", "linux": "GameAssembly.so", "macos": "Contents/Frameworks/GameAssembly.dylib"}[platform]
        write_file(root / library, native_image(platform))
        artifact = write_file(data / "il2cpp_data/Metadata/global-metadata.dat", metadata_image())
    return root, artifact


class UnityAdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Verify detection, exposure reporting and incomplete-scan results through the public Adapter behavior."""

    def setUp(self):
        # Each case gets its own directory so evidence from a previous build cannot leak in.
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.adapter = UnityAdapter()

    def scan_all(self, root, manifest=None):
        """Scan root: Path with manifest: Manifest | None; return one result per rule as list[Finding]."""
        return self.adapter.scan(ScanContext(root, manifest or Manifest()))

    def scan(self, root, manifest=None):
        """Return only kind=fail exposure results as list[Finding], for the exposure test cases."""
        return [f for f in self.scan_all(root, manifest) if f.kind is Kind.FAIL]

    def assert_unknown(self, root, kind=Kind.OPEN):
        """Assert that an undeterminable root: Path yields open/error results without a level. Returns None."""
        results = self.scan_all(root)
        self.assertTrue(results)
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is kind and f.level is None for f in results), results)
        self.assertTrue(all("could not determine" in f.impact for f in results))

    def test_all_desktop_layouts_emit_evidenced_findings(self):
        # Every host runs the same six combinations, so rules are never chosen by host OS.
        for platform in ("windows", "linux", "macos"):
            for backend, rule, level in (("mono", "RS-UNITY-001", Level.L0), ("il2cpp", "RS-UNITY-002", Level.L1)):
                with self.subTest(platform=platform, backend=backend):
                    root, artifact = make_player(self.root / platform / backend, platform, backend)
                    self.assertTrue(self.adapter.detect(root))
                    findings = self.scan(root)
                    self.assertEqual(len(findings), 1)
                    finding = findings[0]
                    self.assertEqual((finding.rule_id, finding.level, finding.confidence), (rule, level, Confidence.OBSERVED))
                    self.assertEqual(finding.location.path, artifact.relative_to(root).as_posix())
                    self.assertTrue(finding.evidence)
                    self.assertTrue(finding.remediation)

    def webgl(self, compress, name="webgl.data.gz"):
        import gzip
        meta = metadata_image()
        entry = b"Il2CppData/Metadata/global-metadata.dat"
        header = 20 + 12 + len(entry)
        container = b"UnityWebData1.0\x00" + struct.pack("<I", header) + struct.pack("<III", header, len(meta), len(entry)) + entry + meta
        build = self.root / "webgl"
        write_file(build / "index.html", b"<script src='Build/webgl.loader.js'></script>")
        write_file(build / "Build/webgl.loader.js", b"function createUnityInstance(){}")
        write_file(build / "Build" / name, gzip.compress(container) if compress else container)
        return build

    def test_webgl_metadata_inside_data_file(self):
        for compress, name in ((True, "webgl.data.gz"), (False, "webgl.data")):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                build = self.webgl(compress, name)
                self.assertTrue(self.adapter.detect(build))
                finding = self.scan(build)[0]
                self.assertEqual((finding.rule_id, finding.level), ("RS-UNITY-002", Level.L1))
                self.assertEqual(finding.location.path, f"Build/{name}!/Il2CppData/Metadata/global-metadata.dat")
                (build / "Build" / name).unlink()

    def test_webgl_brotli_is_open_with_a_way_to_check(self):
        build = self.webgl(False, "webgl.data.br")
        results = self.scan_all(build)
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.OPEN for f in results))
        self.assertIn("Compression Format to Gzip", results[0].impact)

    def test_empty_truncated_and_misnamed_files_are_not_evidence(self):
        # A file name, a magic number, or a reference assembly without method bodies does not prove code exposure.
        for index, payload in enumerate((b"", b"MZ", pe_image(), pe_image(True)[:700])):
            root, artifact = make_player(self.root / str(index), "windows", "mono")
            artifact.write_bytes(payload)
            self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_metadata_header_alone_and_unknown_versions_do_not_become_l2(self):
        # Encrypted, damaged or unreadable metadata must not be treated as stronger protection.
        hidden = bytearray(metadata_image())
        struct.pack_into("<I", hidden, 4, 999)
        hidden[256:296] = bytes(40)  # an unknown version whose names are not stored as plain text
        for index, payload in enumerate((b"", b"encrypted?", metadata_image()[:256], bytes(hidden))):
            root, artifact = make_player(self.root / str(index), "linux", "il2cpp")
            artifact.write_bytes(payload)
            self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_unknown_metadata_version_with_plain_names_is_l1(self):
        # The verdict does not depend on the layout: names an analyst can read are exposed in any version.
        for version in (24, 29, 39, 999):
            with self.subTest(version=version):
                root, artifact = make_player(self.root / str(version), "windows", "il2cpp")
                payload = bytearray(metadata_image())
                struct.pack_into("<I", payload, 4, version)
                artifact.write_bytes(payload)
                findings = self.scan(root)
                self.assertEqual([(f.rule_id, f.level, f.confidence) for f in findings],
                                 [("RS-UNITY-002", Level.L1, Confidence.OBSERVED)])
                self.assertEqual(findings[0].location.symbol, "Game.Combat.*")
                self.assertIn(f"metadata v{version}", findings[-1].evidence[-1].description)

    def test_unknown_metadata_version_links_declared_symbols(self):
        # Mirrors the v39 benchmark: a namespaced type, plus a global-namespace type found through the manifest.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "il2cpp")
        names = b"\0Assembly-CSharp.dll\0Reshield.Benchmarks.Combat\0DamageCalculator\0SaveSlot\0"
        artifact.write_bytes(struct.pack("<II", 0xFAB11BAF, 39) + bytes(248) + names)
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[
            Asset("combat", "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L2, symbols=["Reshield.Benchmarks.Combat.*"]),
            Asset("save", "", AssetValue.MEDIUM, Level.L2, symbols=["SaveSlot.*"]),
        ])
        findings = self.scan(root, manifest)
        self.assertEqual(sorted(f.asset_id for f in findings), ["combat", "save"])
        self.assertTrue(all(f.level is Level.L1 for f in findings))

    def test_runtime_namespace_with_stray_prefix_is_not_a_game_namespace(self):
        # Seen in a real v39 build: a neighboring byte stuck to "UnityEngine.TextCoreTextEngineModule".
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "il2cpp")
        names = b"\0Assembly-CSharp.dll\0HUnityEngine.TextCoreTextEngineModule\0Game.Combat\0"
        artifact.write_bytes(struct.pack("<II", 0xFAB11BAF, 39) + bytes(248) + names)
        inspection = inspect_player(root)
        self.assertEqual(inspection.exposures[0].symbols, ("Game.Combat.*",))

    def test_global_namespace_names_without_declared_symbols_stay_open(self):
        # Without a namespace or a declared symbol there is nothing to confirm, so the rule stays open.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "il2cpp")
        artifact.write_bytes(struct.pack("<II", 0xFAB11BAF, 39) + bytes(248) + b"\0Assembly-CSharp.dll\0Player\0")
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_metadata_invalid_string_index_or_offset_is_rejected(self):
        # A valid header with out-of-range names must not be reported as readable type and method names.
        for index, offset, value in ((0, 24, 0x7FFFFFFF), (1, len(metadata_image()) - 36, 0x7FFFFFFF)):
            root, artifact = make_player(self.root / str(index), "windows", "il2cpp")
            payload = bytearray(metadata_image())
            struct.pack_into("<I", payload, offset, value)
            artifact.write_bytes(payload)
            self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_cli_overlapping_streams_are_not_treated_as_managed_code(self):
        # #Blob overlapping #Strings is inconsistent metadata; it must not be ignored because other methods are readable.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        payload = bytearray(pe_image(True))
        struct.pack_into("<H", payload, 640 + 30, 3)
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 640 + 44, 160, 80)
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 640 + 64, 160, 80)
        payload[640 + 72:640 + 80] = b"#Blob\0\0\0"
        # Move the table to make room for the extra stream header, keeping the deliberate Blob/Strings overlap.
        original = pe_image(True)[640 + 64:640 + 144]
        payload[640 + 80:640 + 160] = original
        payload[640 + 160:640 + 240] = pe_image(True)[640 + 144:640 + 224]
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 640 + 32, 80, 60)
        artifact.write_bytes(payload)
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_duplicate_metadata_image_ranges_are_rejected(self):
        # Overlapping image ranges would attribute a method twice and could cause unbounded loop work.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "linux", "il2cpp")
        original = metadata_image()
        image_offset = 296
        payload = bytearray(original[:image_offset] + original[image_offset:image_offset + 40] + original[image_offset:])
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 8 + 20 * 8, image_offset, 80)
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 8 + 19 * 8, image_offset + 80, 88)
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 8 + 5 * 8, image_offset + 168, 36)
        artifact.write_bytes(payload)
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_universal_macos_binary_is_supported(self):
        # Universal Mach-O slices must be validated on every host, not just single-architecture magic numbers.
        root, _ = make_player(self.root, "macos", "il2cpp")
        payload = bytearray(1024)
        struct.pack_into(">II", payload, 0, 0xCAFEBABE, 2)
        struct.pack_into(">IIIII", payload, 8, 0x01000007, 3, 256, 256, 8)
        struct.pack_into(">IIIII", payload, 28, 0x0100000C, 0, 512, 256, 8)
        payload[256:512] = native_image("macos")
        payload[512:768] = native_image("macos")
        write_file(root / "Contents/Frameworks/GameAssembly.dylib", payload)
        self.assertEqual(self.scan(root)[0].level, Level.L1)

    def test_pe32_plus_managed_assembly_is_supported(self):
        # 64-bit PE data directories sit at a different offset; the PE32 CLI location must not be reused.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        payload = bytearray(pe_image(True))
        section = payload[376:416]
        payload[152:392] = bytes(240)
        struct.pack_into("<H", payload, 132, 0x8664)
        struct.pack_into("<H", payload, 148, 240)
        struct.pack_into("<H", payload, 152, 0x20B)
        struct.pack_into("<I", payload, 152 + 108, 16)
        struct.pack_into("<II", payload, 152 + 112 + 14 * 8, 0x2000, 72)
        payload[392:432] = section
        artifact.write_bytes(payload)
        self.assertEqual(self.scan(root)[0].level, Level.L0)

    def test_resource_limits_do_not_attempt_unbounded_reads(self):
        # A small file declaring a huge section must be rejected immediately, without allocating the declared size.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        payload = bytearray(pe_image(True))
        struct.pack_into("<I", payload, 512 + 12, 0xFFFFFFFF)
        artifact.write_bytes(payload)
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_missing_runtime_and_missing_metadata_are_unknown(self):
        # An assembly or a native file on its own is not enough evidence to decide.
        root, _ = make_player(self.root / "mono", "windows", "mono")
        (root / "MonoBleedingEdge/EmbedRuntime/mono-2.0-bdwgc.dll").unlink()
        self.assert_unknown(root)
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root / "il2cpp", "windows", "il2cpp")
        artifact.unlink()
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_corrupted_native_library_cannot_establish_backend(self):
        # All three native formats must provide structural evidence; metadata alone is not enough.
        for platform, relative in (("windows", "GameAssembly.dll"), ("linux", "GameAssembly.so"), ("macos", "Contents/Frameworks/GameAssembly.dylib")):
            root, _ = make_player(self.root / platform, platform, "il2cpp")
            write_file(root / relative, native_image(platform)[:16])
            self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_case_collisions_are_reported_when_filesystem_supports_them(self):
        # Case-sensitive hosts allow two names that differ only by case; picking either one must be refused.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "linux", "il2cpp")
        other = artifact.with_name("GLOBAL-METADATA.DAT")
        if other.exists():
            self.skipTest("file system does not support names that differ only by case")
        other.write_bytes(artifact.read_bytes())
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_reference_only_assembly_does_not_prove_code_exposure(self):
        # CLI names without IL bodies must not be confused with code that actually ships.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        payload = bytearray(pe_image(True))
        struct.pack_into("<I", payload, 640 + 64 + 46, 0)
        artifact.write_bytes(payload)
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_cli_string_heap_can_share_string_suffixes(self):
        # Real CLR assemblies share string suffixes; the byte before an index need not be NUL.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        payload = bytearray(pe_image(True))
        struct.pack_into("<H", payload, 640 + 64 + 32 + 6, 17)
        artifact.write_bytes(payload)
        findings = self.scan(root)
        self.assertEqual(len(findings), 1)
        finding = findings[0]
        self.assertEqual(finding.location.symbol, "Combat.Calculator.Damage")

    def test_both_backends_and_multiple_players_are_not_merged(self):
        # Mixed backends in one root, and multiple game folders, must both be refused explicitly.
        root, _ = make_player(self.root / "mixed", "windows", "mono")
        make_player(self.root / "mixed", "windows", "il2cpp")
        self.assert_unknown(root)
        root, _ = make_player(self.root / "multiple", "windows", "mono")
        write_file(root / "Other_Data/globalgamemanagers", b"Unity")
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_asset_matching_does_not_copy_findings_to_unrelated_assets(self):
        # Link actual name evidence only to matching assets; server_side must not hide real exposure.
        for backend in ("mono", "il2cpp"):
            root, _ = make_player(self.root / backend, "windows", backend)
            manifest = Manifest(assets=[
                Asset("combat", "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L5, symbols=["Game.Combat.*"], server_side=True),
                Asset("economy", "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L3, symbols=["Game.Economy.*"]),
            ])
            findings = self.scan(root, manifest)
            self.assertEqual([f.asset_id for f in findings], ["combat"])
            self.assertTrue(findings[0].location.symbol.startswith("Game.Combat."))

    def test_unmatched_evidence_is_reported_without_invented_asset(self):
        # When source paths cannot be mapped to the build, keep the result unlinked instead of inventing an asset_id.
        root, _ = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        manifest = Manifest(assets=[Asset("other", "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L3, paths=["Assets/Scripts/**"])])
        self.assertEqual([f.asset_id for f in self.scan(root, manifest)], [None])

    def test_server_declaration_cannot_override_shipped_code_in_summary(self):
        """On every layout, observed exposure overrides a declared L5 without affecting unrelated assets."""
        from reshield.scoring import summarize

        # All six layout/backend combinations must keep the asset link, so no host or backend hides a regression.
        for platform in ("windows", "linux", "macos"):
            for backend, level in (("mono", Level.L0), ("il2cpp", Level.L1)):
                with self.subTest(platform=platform, backend=backend):
                    root, _ = make_player(self.root / platform / backend, platform, backend)
                    manifest = Manifest(assets=[
                        Asset("combat", "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L5,
                              symbols=["Game.Combat.*"], server_side=True),
                        Asset("economy", "", AssetValue.HIGH, Level.L5,
                              symbols=["Game.Economy.*"], server_side=True),
                    ])
                    findings = self.scan_all(root, manifest)
                    summary = summarize(findings, manifest, self.adapter.checked_levels)
                    combat, economy = summary.assets
                    self.assertEqual((combat.observed_level, combat.confidence, combat.meets_target),
                                     (level, Confidence.OBSERVED, False))
                    self.assertEqual((economy.observed_level, economy.confidence),
                                     (Level.L5, Confidence.DECLARED))
                    self.assertEqual(summary.overall_level, level)

    def test_scan_preserves_file_bytes_and_modification_times(self):
        # Read-only means no writes: compare input contents and mtimes directly.
        root, _ = make_player(self.root, "macos", "il2cpp")
        before = {p: (p.read_bytes(), p.stat().st_mtime_ns) for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
        self.scan(root)
        self.assertEqual(before, {p: (p.read_bytes(), p.stat().st_mtime_ns) for p in before})

    def test_case_variant_is_host_independent(self):
        # Windows and Linux case rules must not change the result.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "linux", "il2cpp")
        artifact.rename(artifact.with_name("GLOBAL-METADATA.DAT"))
        self.assertEqual(self.scan(root)[0].level, Level.L1)

    def test_permission_failure_becomes_incomplete_scan_warning(self):
        # Simulate the same read failure on every OS, since chmod does not work on Windows or as root.
        root, _ = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        with patch.object(Path, "open", side_effect=PermissionError("denied")):
            self.assert_unknown(root, Kind.ERROR)

    def test_external_symlink_is_not_followed(self):
        # Evidence outside the target is not part of the scan; hosts that cannot create links skip explicitly.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        outside = write_file(self.root / "outside.dll", artifact.read_bytes())
        artifact.unlink()
        try:
            artifact.symlink_to(outside)
        except OSError as error:
            self.skipTest(f"host cannot create symbolic links: {error}")
        self.assert_unknown(root)

    def test_existing_cli_exports_findings_and_unknown_diagnostics(self):
        # Verify through the existing CLI entry point that undetermined results reach the JSON output.
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "il2cpp")
        output = _Output()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output):
            self.assertEqual(main(["scan", str(root), "--format", "json"]), 0)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(output.getvalue())["findings"][0]["rule_id"], "RS-UNITY-002")
        artifact.write_bytes(b"unknown")
        output = _Output()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(output):
            main(["scan", str(root), "--format", "json"])
        report = json.loads(output.getvalue())
        self.assertFalse(report["summary"]["complete"])
        self.assertIsNone(report["summary"]["score"])
        self.assertEqual({f["kind"] for f in report["findings"]}, {"open"})

    def test_identified_build_marks_other_backend_rule_not_applicable(self):
        # Both rules must have a result: one exposure, one not applicable. No rule is left silent.
        for backend, rule, other in (("mono", "RS-UNITY-001", "RS-UNITY-002"), ("il2cpp", "RS-UNITY-002", "RS-UNITY-001")):
            with self.subTest(backend=backend):
                root, _ = make_player(self.root / backend, "windows", backend)
                kinds = {f.rule_id: f.kind for f in self.scan_all(root)}
                self.assertEqual(kinds, {rule: Kind.FAIL, other: Kind.NOT_APPLICABLE})

    def test_undetermined_backend_reports_both_rules_open(self):
        root, artifact = make_player(self.root, "windows", "mono")
        artifact.write_bytes(b"")
        results = self.scan_all(root)
        # When the backend is known to be Mono (valid runtime, damaged assembly), only its rule is reported.
        self.assertEqual({f.rule_id for f in results}, {"RS-UNITY-001"})
        for path in root.rglob("*"):
            if path.name.casefold() == "mono-2.0-bdwgc.dll":
                path.write_bytes(b"")
        self.assertEqual({f.rule_id for f in self.scan_all(root)}, {"RS-UNITY-001", "RS-UNITY-002"})

    def test_checked_levels_cover_only_l0_and_l1(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.adapter.checked_levels, frozenset({Level.L0, Level.L1}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`tests/test_web_adapter.py`:

```py
"""Static adapter and benchmark-to-report integration checks."""

import json
import shutil
import struct
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "skills/reshield/scripts"))

from reshield.adapters.web import WebAdapter
from reshield.manifest import Manifest
from reshield.adapters.base import ScanContext
from reshield.models import Confidence, Kind, Level
from reshield.scanner import run_scan
from reshield.adapters.web_asar import ScanBudget


def asar(files, payload):
    header = json.dumps({"files": files}).encode()
    padded = (len(header) + 3) // 4 * 4
    return (struct.pack("<4I", 4, padded + 8, padded + 4, len(header))
            + header.ljust(padded, b"\0") + payload)


class WebTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(tmp.name)

    def scan(self):
        adapter = WebAdapter()
        self.assertTrue(adapter.detect(self.root))
        return adapter.scan(ScanContext(self.root, Manifest()))

    def test_missing_and_invalid_artifacts(self):
        (self.root / "index.html").write_text('<script src="missing.js"></script>')
        (self.root / "bad.js").write_bytes(b"\xff")
        (self.root / "bad.map").write_text("{")
        findings = self.scan()
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.OPEN and f.level is None for f in findings))
        self.assertTrue(any("missing.js" in f.impact for f in findings))
        self.assertTrue({"bad.js", "bad.map"} <= {f.location.path for f in findings})

    def test_layout_and_comment_invariance(self):
        entry = self.root / "renamed.js"
        for params, level in [("attack, defense", Level.L0), ("a,b", Level.L1), ("a, defense", None)]:
            for gap in [" ", "\n  ", " /* ;" + "x" * 150 + "\n */ "]:
                with self.subTest(params=params, gap=gap):
                    entry.write_text(f"function{gap}price{gap}({params}){gap}{{ return 2; }}")
                    finding, = self.scan()
                    self.assertEqual(finding.level, level)
                    if level is None:
                        self.assertEqual(finding.kind, Kind.OPEN)
                    else:
                        self.assertEqual(finding.confidence, Confidence.OBSERVED)

    def test_bad_html_does_not_interrupt_other_files(self):
        (self.root / "good.js").write_text("function total(price){ return price; }")
        entry = self.root / "index.html"
        # Python 3.14 reads "<![bogus]>" as a comment instead of failing, so only the
        # truncated script must be reported as open; both must leave good.js checked.
        for html, unreadable in (("<![bogus]>", None), ("<script>function truncated(value){", True)):
            entry.write_text(html)
            findings = self.scan()
            if unreadable:
                self.assertTrue(any(f.location.path == "index.html" and f.kind is Kind.OPEN for f in findings))
            self.assertTrue(any(f.location.symbol == "total" and f.kind is Kind.FAIL for f in findings))

    def test_root_relative_nested_and_out_of_bounds_urls(self):
        (self.root / "entry.js").write_text("function total(price){ return price; }")
        pages = self.root / "pages"
        pages.mkdir()
        (pages / "test.html").write_text("".join(f'<script src="{url}"></script>' for url in
            ["/entry.js?v=2", "../entry.js#section", "./missing.js", "%2e%2e/../entry.js"]))
        opened = [f for f in self.scan() if f.kind is Kind.OPEN]
        self.assertEqual(len(opened), 2)
        self.assertTrue(any("escapes" in f.impact for f in opened))
        self.assertTrue(any("missing.js" in f.impact for f in opened))

    def test_inline_external_and_unsupported_scripts(self):
        (self.root / "entry.js").write_text("function external(value){return value;}")
        (self.root / "index.html").write_text('<script src="/entry.js"></script>\n<script type="module">\n'
            'function inline(total){return total;}</script><script type="text/unknown">secret</script>')
        findings = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual({f.location.symbol for f in findings if f.kind is Kind.FAIL}, {"inline", "external"})
        inline = next(f for f in findings if f.location.symbol == "inline")
        self.assertEqual((inline.location.path, inline.location.line), ("index.html", 3))
        self.assertTrue(any("text/unknown" in f.impact and f.kind is Kind.OPEN for f in findings))

    def test_asar_html_nested_paths_links_and_unpacked(self):
        resources = self.root / "resources"
        resources.mkdir()
        code = b"function external(value){return value;}"
        html = b'<script src="/app.js"></script><script>function inline(price){return price;}</script>'
        files = {"pages": {"files": {"index.html": {"offset": str(len(code)), "size": len(html)}}},
                 "app.js": {"offset": "0", "size": len(code)}, "shortcut": {"link": "outside"},
                 "other.js": {"unpacked": True, "size": 45}}
        (resources / "app.asar").write_bytes(asar(files, code + html))
        findings = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual({f.location.path for f in findings if f.kind is Kind.OPEN},
                         {"resources/app.asar/shortcut", "resources/app.asar/other.js"})
        self.assertEqual({f.location.symbol for f in findings if f.kind is Kind.FAIL}, {"inline", "external"})

    def test_third_party_packages_are_not_the_developers_code(self):
        # A real Electron app was told to minify node_modules/@emotion/memoize.
        resources = self.root / "resources"
        resources.mkdir()
        own = b"function damage(attack){return attack;}"
        lib = b"function memoize(fn){return fn;}"
        files = {"dist": {"files": {"app.js": {"offset": "0", "size": len(own)}}},
                 "node_modules": {"files": {"memoize": {"files": {"index.js": {"offset": str(len(own)), "size": len(lib)},
                                                                    "big.js": {"unpacked": True, "size": 9}}}}}}
        (resources / "app.asar").write_bytes(asar(files, own + lib))
        (self.root / "node_modules/lib").mkdir(parents=True)
        (self.root / "node_modules/lib/index.js").write_text("function helper(v){return v;}")
        findings = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual({f.location.symbol for f in findings if f.kind is Kind.FAIL}, {"damage"})
        self.assertFalse(any("node_modules" in f.location.path for f in findings))

    def test_large_app_file_list(self):
        # The file list of a real 875 MB Electron app is about 2 MB.
        resources = self.root / "resources"
        resources.mkdir()
        code = b"function damage(attack){return attack;}"
        files = {"app.js": {"offset": "0", "size": len(code)},
                 **{f"asset{i:05d}.png": {"offset": "0", "size": 0} for i in range(40000)}}
        (resources / "app.asar").write_bytes(asar(files, code))
        self.assertIn("damage", {f.location.symbol for f in self.scan() if f.kind is Kind.FAIL})

    def test_asar_rejects_overlap_and_invalid_ranges_before_analysis(self):
        resources = self.root / "resources"
        resources.mkdir()
        code = b"function price(value){return value;}"
        for offset, reason in [("1", "Overlapping"), ("1000", "range")]:
            files = {"first.js": {"offset": "0", "size": len(code)}, "other.js": {"offset": offset, "size": 3}}
            (resources / "app.asar").write_bytes(asar(files, code))
            findings = self.scan()
            self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.OPEN for f in findings))
            self.assertTrue(any(reason in f.impact for f in findings))

    def test_scan_limits_are_shared_and_report_incomplete(self):
        resources = self.root / "resources"
        resources.mkdir()
        code = b"function price(value){return value;}"
        files = {f"{i}.js": {"offset": str(i * len(code)), "size": len(code)} for i in range(12)}
        archive = asar(files, code * len(files))
        (resources / "app.asar").write_bytes(archive)
        for key, maximum in [("entries", 6), ("bytes", len(archive) - 4), ("work", 15)]:
            budget = ScanBudget()
            budget.remaining[key] = maximum
            with patch("reshield.adapters.web.ScanBudget", return_value=budget):
                findings = self.scan()
            self.assertTrue(any(f.kind is Kind.OPEN and f.location.path == "." and
                                f"{key} limit" in f.impact for f in findings))

    def test_many_functions_preserve_line_numbers(self):
        (self.root / "many.js").write_text("\n".join(
            f"function f{i}(value){{return value;}}" for i in range(3000)) +
            "\nfunction last(a){return a;}")
        last = next(f for f in self.scan() if f.location.symbol == "last")
        self.assertEqual(last.location.line, 3001)

    def test_damaged_vlq_preserves_independent_source_evidence(self):
        (self.root / "asset.js").write_text("function amount(a){return a;}")
        (self.root / "asset.js.map").write_text(json.dumps(dict(version=3, sources=["original.js"],
            sourcesContent=["function amount(price){return price;}"], mappings="g")))
        mapped = [f for f in self.scan() if f.rule_id == "RS-WEB-002"]
        self.assertTrue(any(f.kind is Kind.OPEN and "VLQ" in f.impact for f in mapped))
        self.assertTrue(any(f.level is Level.L0 and f.confidence is Confidence.OBSERVED for f in mapped))

    def test_electron_directory_and_archive(self):
        code = b"function calculate(value) {\n  return value * 2;\n}\n"
        resources = self.root / "resources"
        app = resources / "app"
        app.mkdir(parents=True)
        file = app / "main.js"
        file.write_bytes(code)
        self.assertEqual(self.scan()[0].level, Level.L0)
        file.unlink()
        app.rmdir()
        source_map = json.dumps(dict(version=3, sources=["input.js"], sourcesContent=[code.decode()],
                                     mappings="AAAA")).encode()
        entries = {"main.js": {"offset": "0", "size": len(code)},
                   "main.js.map": {"offset": str(len(code)), "size": len(source_map)}}
        archive = resources / "app.asar"
        archive.write_bytes(asar(entries, code + source_map))
        findings = self.scan()
        self.assertEqual({f.location.path for f in findings},
                         {"resources/app.asar/main.js", "resources/app.asar/main.js.map"})
        self.assertTrue(all(f.level is Level.L0 for f in findings))
        archive.write_bytes(b"bad")
        self.assertTrue(all(f.kind is Kind.OPEN for f in self.scan()))

    def test_real_publications(self):
        sample = ROOT / "benchmarks/web/combat-formula"
        for variant, expected in [("plain", Level.L0), ("minified", Level.L1), ("minified-map", Level.L0)]:
            with self.subTest(variant=variant):
                target = sample / "builds" / variant
                if not target.exists():
                    self.skipTest("Benchmark publication has not been built")
                scan = run_scan(target, sample / "reshield.toml")
                asset = [f for f in scan.findings if f.asset_id == "combat-formulas"]
                self.assertEqual(min(f.level for f in asset), expected)
                self.assertTrue(all(f.evidence and f.to_dict()["platform"] == "web" for f in asset))
                self.assertEqual(scan.summary.overall_level, expected)
                self.assertTrue(scan.summary.complete)
                if variant == "minified-map":
                    self.assertTrue(any(f.rule_id == "RS-WEB-002" and f.level is Level.L0 and
                                        f.confidence is Confidence.OBSERVED for f in asset))
                    self.assertFalse(any(f.rule_id == "RS-WEB-001" and f.level is Level.L0 for f in asset))
                    shutil.copytree(target, self.root, dirs_exist_ok=True)
                    for path in self.root.glob("*.map"):
                        path.unlink()
                    without_map = run_scan(self.root, sample / "reshield.toml")
                    self.assertFalse(without_map.summary.complete)
                    self.assertFalse(any(f.level is Level.L0 for f in without_map.findings))
```
